          return { success: true, data: devs };
        }
        if (action === 'upsert') {
          const studentId = params.student_id;
          const semester = params.semester || '1';
          const academicYear = params.academic_year || '2026/2027';

          // Check if exists
          const existing = await supabaseRest(`developments?student_id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(studentId)}&semester=eq.${encodeURIComponent(semester)}&academic_year=eq.${encodeURIComponent(academicYear)}&select=id`);

          const payload = {
            student_id: studentId,
            student_name: params.student_name,
            class_id: params.class_id,
            semester,
            academic_year: academicYear,
            aspect_nam: params.aspect_nam || params.nilai_agama || 'BSH',
            aspect_fisik_motorik: params.aspect_fisik_motorik || 'BSH',
            aspect_kognitif: params.aspect_kognitif || 'BSH',
            aspect_bahasa: params.aspect_bahasa || 'BSH',
            aspect_sosial_emosional: params.aspect_sosial_emosional || 'BSH',
            aspect_seni: params.aspect_seni || 'BSH',
            teacher_notes: params.teacher_notes || '',
            updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
          };

          if (existing && existing.length > 0) {
            await supabaseRest(`developments?id=eq.${existing[0].id}`, 'PATCH', payload);
          } else {
            payload.id = 'DEV-' + Date.now();
            await supabaseRest('developments', 'POST', payload);
          }
          return { success: true, message: 'Rapor perkembangan tersimpan' };
        }
      }

      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // 12. PPDB
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      if (module === 'ppdb') {
        if (action === 'list') {
          let query = 'ppdb_registrations?select=*&order=created_at.desc';
          if (params.status) query += `&status=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.status)}`;
          if (params.search || params.q) {
            query += `&full_name.ilike.*${encodeURIComponent(params.search || params.q)}*`;
          }
          let regs = [];
          try {
            regs = await supabaseRest(query);
          } catch (e) {
            console.warn('PPDB registrations fetch fallback:', e);
          }
          if (!regs || regs.length === 0) {
            try {
              const localRegs = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('siy_local_ppdb_regs') || '[]');
              if (localRegs.length > 0) regs = localRegs;
            } catch (_) {}
          }
          return { success: true, data: regs };
        }
        if (action === 'get') {
          const id = params.id;
          const regs = await supabaseRest(`ppdb_registrations?or=(id.eq.${encodeURIComponent(id)},registration_number.eq.${encodeURIComponent(id)})&select=*`);
          return { success: true, data: regs[0] || null };
        }
        if (action === 'submitPublic' || action === 'submit') {
          // Upload documents if provided as File or base64
          let photoUrl = params.photo_url || params.photo_dataurl;
          if (params.photo_file || (photoUrl && typeof photoUrl === 'string' && photoUrl.startsWith('data:') && photoUrl.length > 50)) {
            photoUrl = await uploadToSupabaseStorage(params.photo_file || photoUrl, 'ppdb_photo').catch(() => '');
          }
          let certUrl = params.birth_certificate_url || params.akta_dataurl;
          if (params.birth_certificate_file || (certUrl && typeof certUrl === 'string' && certUrl.startsWith('data:') && certUrl.length > 50)) {
            certUrl = await uploadToSupabaseStorage(params.birth_certificate_file || certUrl, 'ppdb_akta').catch(() => '');
          }
          let kkUrl = params.family_card_url || params.kk_dataurl;
          if (params.family_card_file || (kkUrl && typeof kkUrl === 'string' && kkUrl.startsWith('data:') && kkUrl.length > 50)) {
            kkUrl = await uploadToSupabaseStorage(params.family_card_file || kkUrl, 'ppdb_kk').catch(() => '');
          }

          const id = 'REG-' + Date.now();
          const regNum = 'REG-' + new Date().getFullYear() + '-' + String(Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000));
          
          const studentName = params.student_name || params.child_name || params.full_name || params.nama || 'Calon Siswa';
          const parentName = params.parent_name || params.father_name || params.mother_name || 'Orang Tua';
          const parentPhone = params.phone || params.parent_phone || params.father_phone || params.mother_phone || '';
          const parentEmail = params.email || params.parent_email || params.father_email || '';
          const gender = params.gender || params.student_gender || 'L';
          const birthPlace = params.birth_place || params.student_birth_place || 'Samarinda';
          const birthDate = params.birth_date || params.student_birth_date || '2021-01-01';
          const level = params.form_type === 'tpq' ? 'TPQ' : (params.level || 'PAUD');
          const address = params.address || '';
          const password = params.password || (typeof birthDate === 'string' ? birthDate.replace(/-/g, '') : '123456');

          const cleanMeta = { ...params };
          delete cleanMeta.photo_file;
          delete cleanMeta.birth_certificate_file;
          delete cleanMeta.family_card_file;
          delete cleanMeta.photo_dataurl;
          delete cleanMeta.akta_dataurl;
          delete cleanMeta.kk_dataurl;
          delete cleanMeta.password;

          const regPayload = {
            id,
            registration_number: regNum,
            registration_no: regNum,
            student_name: studentName,
            full_name: studentName,
            child_name: studentName,
            nickname: params.nickname || '',
            nik: params.nik || '',
            form_type: params.form_type || (level === 'TPQ' ? 'tpq' : 'paud'),
            student_gender: gender,
            gender: gender,
            student_birth_place: birthPlace,
            birth_place: birthPlace,
            student_birth_date: birthDate,
            birth_date: birthDate,
            parent_name: parentName,
            father_name: params.father_name || parentName,
            mother_name: params.mother_name || '',
            parent_phone: parentPhone,
            phone: parentPhone,
            parent_email: parentEmail,
            address: address,
            level: level,
            status: 'pending',
            notes: params.notes || '',
            photo_url: photoUrl || '',
            birth_certificate_url: certUrl || '',
            family_card_url: kkUrl || '',
            documents_url: {
              photo: photoUrl || '',
              birth_certificate: certUrl || '',
              family_card: kkUrl || ''
            },
            form_metadata: cleanMeta
          };

          // 1. Simpan ke tabel ppdb_registrations dengan penanganan skema
          let res = null;
          try {
            res = await supabaseRest('ppdb_registrations', 'POST', regPayload);
          } catch (err) {
            console.warn('PPDB insert primary failed, trying alternate schema:', err);
            const altPayload = {
              id,
              registration_number: regNum,
              registration_no: regNum,
              full_name: studentName,
              child_name: studentName,
              gender: gender,
              birth_place: birthPlace,
              birth_date: birthDate,
              father_name: parentName,
              phone: parentPhone,
              address: address,
              photo_url: photoUrl || '',
              birth_certificate_url: certUrl || '',
              family_card_url: kkUrl || '',
              status: 'pending'
            };
            res = await supabaseRest('ppdb_registrations', 'POST', altPayload).catch(e => {
              console.warn('Alternate PPDB insert fallback:', e);
              return [regPayload];
            });
          }

          // Safe local persistence fallback
          try {
            const localRegs = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('siy_local_ppdb_regs') || '[]');
            localRegs.unshift({ ...regPayload, id, registration_number: regNum, registration_no: regNum, created_at: new Date().toISOString() });
            localStorage.setItem('siy_local_ppdb_regs', JSON.stringify(localRegs));
          } catch (_) {}

          // 2. Buat akun login Portal Siswa untuk Calon Siswa
          const userId = 'USR-' + Date.now();
          const studentId = 'STU-' + Date.now();
          const emailForLogin = parentEmail || `${regNum.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '')}@pendaftar.yayasan.sch.id`;

          const newUser = {
            id: userId,
            email: emailForLogin,
            username: regNum,
            password_hash: password,
            full_name: studentName,
            name: studentName,
            phone: parentPhone,
            role: 'calon_siswa',
            role_label: 'Calon Siswa / Pendaftar',
            status: 'active',
            student_id: studentId,
            is_password_changed: true,
            created_at: new Date().toISOString()
          };

          try {
            const localUsers = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('siy_local_users') || '[]');
            localUsers.push(newUser);
            localStorage.setItem('siy_local_users', JSON.stringify(localUsers));
          } catch (_) {}

          await supabaseRest('users', 'POST', newUser).catch(err => console.warn('User account warning:', err));

          // 3. Buat record di students
          const newStudent = {
            id: studentId,
            user_id: userId,
            full_name: studentName,
            name: studentName,
            gender: gender,
            birth_place: birthPlace,
            birth_date: birthDate,
            parent_name: parentName,
            parent_phone: parentPhone,
            parent_email: emailForLogin,
            status_pendaftaran: 'pending',
            is_active: false,
            status: 'pending',
            created_at: new Date().toISOString()
          };

          try {
            const localStudents = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('siy_local_students') || '[]');
            localStudents.push(newStudent);
            localStorage.setItem('siy_local_students', JSON.stringify(localStudents));
          } catch (_) {}

          await supabaseRest('students', 'POST', newStudent).catch(err => console.warn('Student profile warning:', err));

          // 4. Simpan sesi login otomatis ke localStorage
          const roleObj = { code: 'calon_siswa', name: 'Calon Siswa / Pendaftar' };
          const userSession = { ...newUser, role: roleObj, student_id: studentId };
          delete userSession.password_hash;
          const token = 'siy_' + userId + '_' + Date.now();
          localStorage.setItem('siy_token', token);
          localStorage.setItem('siy_user', JSON.stringify(userSession));

          return {
            success: true,
            data: (res && res[0]) || regPayload,
            registration_number: regNum,
            registration_no: regNum,
            login_email: emailForLogin,
            login_password: password,
            message: 'Pendaftaran PPDB berhasil diajukan! Akun portal calon siswa Anda telah aktif.'
          };
        }
        if (action === 'getStatusPublic' || action === 'check_status' || action === 'status') {
          const regNo = params.registration_number || params.registration_no || params.id;
          const regs = await supabaseRest(`ppdb_registrations?or=(registration_number.eq.${encodeURIComponent(regNo)},registration_no.eq.${encodeURIComponent(regNo)},id.eq.${encodeURIComponent(regNo)})&select=*`);
          if (!regs || regs.length === 0) {
            return { success: false, message: 'Nomor pendaftaran tidak ditemukan' };
          }
          return { success: true, data: { registration: regs[0] }, registration: regs[0] };
        }
        if (action === 'period') {
          const periods = await supabaseRest('ppdb_periods?select=*&order=created_at.desc');
          const p = periods && periods[0] ? { ...periods[0] } : null;
          if (p) {
            let extra = {};
            if (p.announcement_notes) {
              try {
                extra = JSON.parse(p.announcement_notes);
              } catch (_) {
                extra = { notes: p.announcement_notes };
              }
            }
            try {
              const cached = localStorage.getItem('siy_ppdb_schedule');
              if (cached) Object.assign(extra, JSON.parse(cached));
            } catch (_) {}
            Object.assign(p, extra);
          }
          return { success: true, data: p };
        }
        if (action === 'update_period') {
          const id = params.id || 'PPDPER-2026';
          const scheduleData = {
            online_registration_dates: params.online_registration_dates || '',
            verification_dates: params.verification_dates || '',
            announcement_dates: params.announcement_dates || '',
            re_registration_dates: params.re_registration_dates || '',
            notes: params.notes || ''
          };
          try {
            localStorage.setItem('siy_ppdb_schedule', JSON.stringify(scheduleData));
          } catch (_) {}

          const payload = {
            status: params.status || 'open',
            academic_year: params.academic_year || '2026/2027',
            announcement_notes: JSON.stringify(scheduleData)
          };
          const res = await supabaseRest(`ppdb_periods?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, 'PATCH', payload);
          return { success: true, data: { ...(res[0] || payload), ...scheduleData }, message: 'Pengaturan gelombang & jadwal PPDB diperbarui' };
        }
        if (action === 'update') {
          const id = params.id;
          const patchData = { ...params };
          delete patchData.id;
          delete patchData.token;
          const res = await supabaseRest(`ppdb_registrations?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, 'PATCH', patchData);
          return { success: true, data: (res && res[0]) || patchData, message: 'Data pendaftar berhasil diperbarui' };
        }
        if (action === 'delete') {
          const id = params.id;
          await supabaseRest(`ppdb_registrations?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, 'DELETE');
          return { success: true, message: 'Data pendaftar berhasil dihapus' };
        }
        if (action === 'approve') {
          const res = await supabaseRest(`ppdb_registrations?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}`, 'PATCH', {
            status: 'accepted',
            notes: params.notes || ''
          });
          return { success: true, data: res[0], message: 'Pendaftar diterima' };
        }
        if (action === 'reject') {
          const res = await supabaseRest(`ppdb_registrations?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}`, 'PATCH', {
            status: 'rejected',
            notes: params.reason || params.notes || ''
          });
          return { success: true, data: res[0], message: 'Pendaftar ditolak' };
        }
        if (action === 'convert') {
          const regs = await supabaseRest(`ppdb_registrations?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}&select=*`);
          if (!regs || regs.length === 0) return { success: false, message: 'Pendaftar tidak ditemukan' };
          const reg = regs[0];

          // 1. Guard Idempotensi: Cegah konversi ganda
          if (reg.status === 'converted') {
            return { success: false, message: 'Pendaftar ini sudah pernah dikonversi menjadi siswa aktif.' };
          }

          const targetClassId = params.class_id || 'CLS-001';

          // Ambil nama kelas
          let targetClassName = 'PAUD / TK Terpadu';
          if (targetClassId) {
            const cls = await supabaseRest(`classes?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(targetClassId)}&select=name`);
            if (cls && cls[0]) targetClassName = cls[0].name;
          }

          // 2. Tentukan NIS Siswa (bisa input dari admin atau auto-generate)
          const studentNis = params.nis || ('2026' + String(Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)));

          // 3. Guard Keunikan NIS: Pastikan belum ada siswa yang memiliki NIS ini
          const existingNis = await supabaseRest(`students?nis=eq.${encodeURIComponent(studentNis)}&select=id`);
          if (existingNis && existingNis.length > 0) {
            return { success: false, message: `NIS ${studentNis} sudah digunakan oleh siswa aktif lain. Silakan gunakan nomor NIS lain.` };
          }

          // 4. Password awal dari Tanggal Lahir (Format default: DDMMYYYY)
          let birthDateStr = reg.birth_date || reg.student_birth_date ? String(reg.birth_date || reg.student_birth_date).trim() : '2021-01-01';
          let birthDatePassword = birthDateStr;
          if (birthDateStr.includes('-')) {
            const p = birthDateStr.split('-');
            if (p.length === 3) birthDatePassword = `${p[2]}${p[1]}${p[0]}`; // DDMMYYYY
          }

          const regNo = reg.registration_number || reg.registration_no || '';
          const pendaftarEmail = `${regNo.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '')}@pendaftar.yayasan.sch.id`;
          const cleanPhone = (reg.phone || reg.parent_phone || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
          const studentEmail = reg.email || reg.parent_email || (cleanPhone ? `${studentNis}@siswa.yayasan.sch.id` : `siswa_${studentNis}@yayasan.sch.id`);
          const studentName = reg.student_name || reg.full_name || reg.child_name || 'Santri Baru';

          // 5. Cek apakah user akun pendaftar sudah ada (cari via email, username regNo, atau email pendaftar)
          let existingUsers = await supabaseRest(
            `users?or=(email.eq.${encodeURIComponent(studentEmail)},username.eq.${encodeURIComponent(regNo)},email.eq.${encodeURIComponent(pendaftarEmail)})&select=*`
          ).catch(() => []);

          let userId = (existingUsers && existingUsers[0]) ? existingUsers[0].id : ('USR-' + studentNis);
          let stuId = (existingUsers && existingUsers[0] && existingUsers[0].student_id)
            ? existingUsers[0].student_id
            : ('STU-' + String(Date.now()).slice(-5));

          if (existingUsers && existingUsers.length > 0) {
            await supabaseRest(`users?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(userId)}`, 'PATCH', {
              email: studentEmail,
              role: 'siswa_aktif',
              role_label: 'Siswa Aktif',
              nis: studentNis,
              student_id: stuId,
              password_hash: birthDatePassword,
              is_password_changed: false,
              status: 'active'
            });
          } else {
            await supabaseRest('users', 'POST', {
              id: userId,
              email: studentEmail,
              password_hash: birthDatePassword,
              full_name: studentName,
              name: studentName,
              phone: reg.phone || reg.parent_phone || '',
              role: 'siswa_aktif',
              role_label: 'Siswa Aktif',
              nis: studentNis,
              student_id: stuId,
              avatar: reg.photo_url || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1500648767791-00dcc994a43e?w=150',
              status: 'active',
              is_password_changed: false
            });
          }

          // 6. Buat atau Update Data Siswa Aktif (Tanpa Duplikasi Baris Pending)
          const studentPayload = {
            id: stuId,
            user_id: userId,
            nis: studentNis,
            full_name: studentName,
            name: studentName,
            child_name: studentName,
            nickname: reg.nickname || null,
            gender: reg.gender || reg.student_gender || 'L',
            birth_place: reg.birth_place || reg.student_birth_place || 'Samarinda',
            birth_date: birthDateStr,
            class_id: targetClassId,
            class_name: targetClassName,
            kelas_sekarang: targetClassName,
            semester_sekarang: 'Semester 1',
            status_kenaikan: 'Aktif',
            status_pendaftaran: 'accepted',
            is_active: true,
            parent_name: reg.parent_name || reg.father_name || reg.mother_name || '',
            father_name: reg.father_name || reg.parent_name || '',
            mother_name: reg.mother_name || '',
            parent_phone: reg.parent_phone || reg.phone || '',
            phone: reg.phone || reg.parent_phone || '',
            parent_email: studentEmail,
            photo_url: reg.photo_url || null,
            address: reg.address || '',
            status: 'active'
          };

          const existingStu = await supabaseRest(`students?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(stuId)}&select=id`).catch(() => []);
          if (existingStu && existingStu.length > 0) {
            const patchStu = { ...studentPayload };
            delete patchStu.id;
            await supabaseRest(`students?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(stuId)}`, 'PATCH', patchStu);
          } else {
            await supabaseRest('students', 'POST', studentPayload);
          }

          // Update jumlah siswa di kelas tujuan
          if (targetClassId) {
            supabaseRest(`students?class_id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(targetClassId)}&status=eq.active&select=id`).then(list => {
              supabaseRest(`classes?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(targetClassId)}`, 'PATCH', { student_count: (list || []).length });
            }).catch(() => {});
          }

          // 7. Update Status Registrasi PPDB menjadi 'converted'
          await supabaseRest(`ppdb_registrations?id=eq.${reg.id}`, 'PATCH', {
            status: 'converted',
            notes: `Diterima sebagai Siswa Aktif (NIS: ${studentNis}, Kelas: ${targetClassName})`
          });

          // 8. Ringkasan Kredensial Siswa Baru
          const origin = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : '';
          const loginUrl = `${origin}/login.html`;
          const summaryText = `Akses Portal Siswa Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma Samarinda\nNama: ${studentName}\nNIS (Username Login): ${studentNis}\nKata Sandi Awal: ${birthDatePassword} (Tanggal Lahir: DDMMYYYY)\nLink Portal: ${loginUrl}`;

          return {
            success: true,
            data: {
              student: studentPayload,
              nis: studentNis,
              class_name: targetClassName,
              initial_credentials: {
                student_name: studentName,
                nis: studentNis,
                default_password: birthDatePassword,
                login_url: loginUrl
              },
              summary_text: summaryText
            },
            message: `Siswa berhasil diaktifkan dengan NIS: ${studentNis}`
          };
        }
      }

      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // 13. CONTENT (NEWS, ANNOUNCEMENTS, EVENTS, GALLERIES)
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      if (module === 'news') {
        if (action === 'list') {
          const news = await supabaseRest('news?select=*&order=published_at.desc');
          return { success: true, data: news, raw: { items: news } };
        }
        if (action === 'get') {
          const res = await supabaseRest(`news?or=(id.eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id || '')},slug.eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.slug || '')})&select=*`);
          return { success: true, data: res[0] || null };
        }
        if (action === 'create') {
          let img = params.image || params.featured_image_url || params.cover_url || params.image_url;
          if (params.image_file || (img && img.startsWith('data:'))) {
            img = await uploadToSupabaseStorage(params.image_file || img, 'berita');
          }
          const id = 'NWS-' + Date.now();
          const slug = (params.title || 'berita').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-') + '-' + String(Date.now()).slice(-4);
          const authorVal = params.author || params.author_name || 'Admin Sekolah';
          const payload = {
            id,
            slug,
            title: params.title || 'Berita Baru',
            category: params.category || 'Umum',
            summary: params.summary || params.excerpt || (params.content ? params.content.substring(0, 150) : ''),
            content: params.content || params.body || '',
            image: img || '',
            author: authorVal,
            author_name: authorVal,
            status: params.status || (params.is_published ? 'published' : 'draft'),
            published_at: params.published_at || new Date().toISOString()
          };
          const res = await supabaseRest('news', 'POST', payload);
          return { success: true, data: res[0] || payload, message: 'Berita berhasil diterbitkan' };
        }
        if (action === 'update') {
          const payload = {};
          if (params.title) payload.title = params.title;
          if (params.category) payload.category = params.category;
          if (params.summary || params.excerpt) payload.summary = params.summary || params.excerpt;
          if (params.content || params.body) payload.content = params.content || params.body;
          if (params.status) payload.status = params.status;
          let img = params.image || params.featured_image_url || params.cover_url || params.image_url;
          if (params.image_file || (img && img.startsWith('data:'))) {
            img = await uploadToSupabaseStorage(params.image_file || img, 'berita');
            if (img) payload.image = img;
          } else if (img) {
            payload.image = img;
          }
          const res = await supabaseRest(`news?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}`, 'PATCH', payload);
          return { success: true, data: res[0] || payload, message: 'Berita diperbarui' };
        }
        if (action === 'delete' || action === 'remove') {
          await supabaseRest(`news?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}`, 'DELETE');
          return { success: true, message: 'Berita dihapus' };
        }
      }

      if (module === 'announcements') {
        if (action === 'list') {
          const ann = await supabaseRest('announcements?select=*&order=published_at.desc');
          return { success: true, data: ann, raw: { items: ann } };
        }
        if (action === 'get') {
          const ann = await supabaseRest(`announcements?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}&select=*`);
          return { success: true, data: ann[0] || null };
        }
        if (action === 'create') {
          const id = 'ANN-' + Date.now();
          const targetAud = params.target_audience || params.target_role || params.category || 'all';
          const payload = {
            id,
            title: params.title || 'Pengumuman Baru',
            content: params.content || params.body || params.excerpt || '',
            target_audience: targetAud,
            target_role: targetAud,
            is_pinned: !!params.is_pinned,
            status: params.status || (params.is_published ? 'published' : 'draft'),
            published_at: params.published_at || new Date().toISOString()
          };
          const res = await supabaseRest('announcements', 'POST', payload);
          return { success: true, data: res[0] || payload, message: 'Pengumuman diterbitkan' };
        }
        if (action === 'update') {
          const payload = {};
          if (params.title) payload.title = params.title;
          if (params.content || params.body) payload.content = params.content || params.body;
          if (params.target_audience || params.target_role) {
            payload.target_audience = params.target_audience || params.target_role;
            payload.target_role = payload.target_audience;
          }
          if (params.status) payload.status = params.status;
          if (params.is_pinned !== undefined) payload.is_pinned = !!params.is_pinned;
          const res = await supabaseRest(`announcements?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}`, 'PATCH', payload);
          return { success: true, data: res[0] || params, message: 'Pengumuman diperbarui' };
        }
        if (action === 'delete' || action === 'remove') {
          await supabaseRest(`announcements?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}`, 'DELETE');
          return { success: true, message: 'Pengumuman dihapus' };
        }
      }

      if (module === 'events') {
        if (action === 'list') {
          const evts = await supabaseRest('events?select=*&order=event_date.asc').catch(() => supabaseRest('events?select=*'));
          const normalizedEvts = (evts || []).map(e => ({ ...e, date: e.date || e.event_date, event_date: e.event_date || e.date }));
          return { success: true, data: normalizedEvts, raw: { items: normalizedEvts } };
        }
        if (action === 'create') {
          const id = 'EVT-' + Date.now();
          const evtDate = params.date || params.event_date || new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
          const payload = {
            id,
            title: params.title || 'Agenda Baru',
            date: evtDate,
            event_date: evtDate,
            time: params.time || '08:00 - Selesai',
            location: params.location || 'Lingkungan Yayasan',
            description: params.description || params.content || params.excerpt || '',
            status: params.status || (params.is_published ? 'upcoming' : 'draft')
          };
          const res = await supabaseRest('events', 'POST', payload);
          return { success: true, data: res[0] || payload, message: 'Agenda dibuat' };
        }
        if (action === 'update') {
          const payload = {};
          if (params.title) payload.title = params.title;
          if (params.date || params.event_date) {
            payload.date = params.date || params.event_date;
            payload.event_date = payload.date;
          }
          if (params.time) payload.time = params.time;
          if (params.location) payload.location = params.location;
          if (params.description || params.content) payload.description = params.description || params.content;
          if (params.status) payload.status = params.status;
          const res = await supabaseRest(`events?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}`, 'PATCH', payload);
          return { success: true, data: res[0] || payload, message: 'Agenda diperbarui' };
        }
        if (action === 'delete' || action === 'remove') {
          await supabaseRest(`events?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}`, 'DELETE');
          return { success: true, message: 'Agenda dihapus' };
        }
      }

      // Galeri: Dukung alias 'gallery' dan 'galleries' dengan upload storage
      if (module === 'galleries' || module === 'gallery') {
        if (action === 'list') {
          const gals = await supabaseRest('galleries?select=*&order=created_at.desc');
          return { success: true, data: gals, raw: { items: gals } };
        }
        if (action === 'create') {
          let imgUrl = params.image_url || params.image || params.cover_url;
          if (params.image_file || (imgUrl && imgUrl.startsWith('data:'))) {
            imgUrl = await uploadToSupabaseStorage(params.image_file || imgUrl, 'galeri');
          }
          const id = 'GAL-' + Date.now();
          const payload = {
            id,
            title: params.title || 'Foto Galeri',
            category: params.category || 'Umum',
            description: params.description || params.content || params.excerpt || '',
            image_url: imgUrl || ''
          };
          const res = await supabaseRest('galleries', 'POST', payload);
          return { success: true, data: res[0] || payload, message: 'Foto galeri ditambahkan' };
        }
        if (action === 'update') {
          let imgUrl = params.image_url || params.image || params.cover_url;
          if (params.image_file || (imgUrl && imgUrl.startsWith('data:'))) {
            imgUrl = await uploadToSupabaseStorage(params.image_file || imgUrl, 'galeri');
          }
          const payload = {};
          if (params.title) payload.title = params.title;
          if (params.category) payload.category = params.category;
          if (params.description || params.content || params.excerpt) {
            payload.description = params.description || params.content || params.excerpt;
          }
          if (imgUrl) payload.image_url = imgUrl;
          const res = await supabaseRest(`galleries?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}`, 'PATCH', payload);
          return { success: true, data: res[0] || payload, message: 'Foto galeri diperbarui' };
        }
        if (action === 'delete' || action === 'remove') {
          await supabaseRest(`galleries?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}`, 'DELETE');
          return { success: true, message: 'Galeri dihapus' };
        }
      }

      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // 14. SCHOOLS / PENGATURAN IDENTITAS SEKOLAH & MEDSOS
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      if (module === 'schools' || module === 'school') {
        if (action === 'get') {
          const schools = await supabaseRest('schools?select=*');
          const sch = (schools && schools[0]) ? { ...schools[0] } : {};
          try {
            const cachedExt = localStorage.getItem('siy_school_ext');
            if (cachedExt) Object.assign(sch, JSON.parse(cachedExt));
          } catch (_) {}
          return { success: true, data: sch };
        }
        if (action === 'update') {
          const id = params.id || 'SCH-001';
          const fullPayload = { ...params };
          delete fullPayload.id;
          fullPayload.updated_at = new Date().toISOString();

          // Simpan selalu ke cache lokal agar langsung aktif
          const extPayload = {
            campus_note: params.campus_note || '',
            maps_url: params.maps_url || '',
            maps_embed_url: params.maps_embed_url || '',
            whatsapp_number: params.whatsapp_number || '',
            instagram_url: params.instagram_url || '',
            youtube_url: params.youtube_url || '',
            facebook_url: params.facebook_url || ''
          };
          try {
            localStorage.setItem('siy_school_ext', JSON.stringify(extPayload));
            sessionStorage.setItem('siyayasan_school_profile', JSON.stringify({ ...fullPayload, ...extPayload }));
          } catch (_) {}

          try {
            const res = await supabaseRest(`schools?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, 'PATCH', fullPayload);
            return { success: true, data: res[0] || fullPayload, message: 'Identitas & tautan sekolah berhasil diperbarui' };
          } catch (patchErr) {
            console.warn('Full school patch failed, fallback to core fields:', patchErr.message);
            const corePayload = {
              name: params.name,
              address: params.address,
              phone: params.phone,
              email: params.email
            };
            const res = await supabaseRest(`schools?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, 'PATCH', corePayload);
            return { success: true, data: { ...corePayload, ...extPayload }, message: 'Identitas & tautan sekolah berhasil diperbarui' };
          }
        }
      }

      // 15. LOGS / AKTIVITAS
      if (module === 'logs' || module === 'activity_logs') {
        if (action === 'list') {
          const logs = await supabaseRest('activity_logs?select=*&order=created_at.desc&limit=50');
          return { success: true, data: logs, raw: { items: logs } };
        }
      }

      // 16. REPORTS
      if (module === 'reports') {
        if (action === 'generate') {
          const repType = params.report_type || 'students';
          let headers = [];
          let rows = [];

          if (repType === 'students') {
            headers = ['No', 'NIS', 'Nama Lengkap', 'Kelas', 'Jenis Kelamin', 'Nama Wali', 'No. HP / WA', 'Status'];
            const stu = await supabaseRest('students?status=eq.active&select=*&order=full_name.asc').catch(() => []);
            rows = (stu || []).map((s, idx) => [
              idx + 1,
              s.nis || '-',
              s.full_name || s.name || '-',
              s.class_name || s.kelas_sekarang || s.class_id || '-',
              s.gender === 'L' ? 'Laki-laki' : (s.gender === 'P' ? 'Perempuan' : (s.gender || '-')),
              s.parent_name || s.father_name || s.mother_name || '-',
              s.parent_phone || s.phone || '-',
              (s.status || 'active').toUpperCase()
            ]);
          } else if (repType === 'payments' || repType === 'finance') {
            headers = ['No', 'No. Tagihan', 'Nama Siswa', 'Keterangan / Periode', 'Nominal (Rp)', 'Jatuh Tempo', 'Status Tagihan'];
            const inv = await supabaseRest('invoices?select=*').catch(() => []);
            let list = inv || [];
            if (params.month) {
              list = list.filter(i => (i.due_date && i.due_date.startsWith(params.month)) || (i.period && i.period.includes(params.month)));
            }
            rows = list.map((i, idx) => [
              idx + 1,
              i.invoice_number || '-',
              i.student_name || '-',
              i.description || i.item_name || i.period || i.month || '-',
              Number(i.amount || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID'),
              i.due_date || '-',
              i.status === 'paid' ? 'LUNAS' : (i.status === 'menunggu_verifikasi' ? 'MENUNGGU VERIFIKASI' : 'BELUM LUNAS')
            ]);
          } else if (repType === 'ppdb') {
            headers = ['No', 'No. Pendaftaran', 'Nama Calon Siswa', 'Jalur / Jenjang', 'Nama Wali', 'No. HP', 'Status', 'Tgl Daftar'];
            const regs = await supabaseRest('ppdb_registrations?select=*').catch(() => []);
            let list = regs || [];
            if (params.academic_year) {
              list = list.filter(r => (r.academic_year && r.academic_year.includes(params.academic_year)) || (r.period_name && r.period_name.includes(params.academic_year)));
            }
            rows = list.map((r, idx) => [
              idx + 1,
              r.registration_number || r.registration_no || '-',
              r.full_name || r.child_name || '-',
              (r.form_type || 'PAUD').toUpperCase(),
              r.father_name || r.parent_name || '-',
              r.phone || r.father_phone || '-',
              (r.status || 'pending').toUpperCase(),
              (r.registration_date || r.created_at || '').slice(0, 10)
            ]);
          } else if (repType === 'attendance') {
            headers = ['No', 'Tanggal', 'Nama Siswa', 'Kelas', 'Status Kehadiran', 'Catatan'];
            const att = await supabaseRest('attendance?select=*').catch(() => []);
            let list = att || [];
            if (params.month) {
              list = list.filter(a => a.date && a.date.startsWith(params.month));
            }
            rows = list.map((a, idx) => [
              idx + 1,
              a.date || '-',
              a.student_name || '-',
              a.class_id || '-',
              (a.status || 'hadir').toUpperCase(),
              a.notes || '-'
            ]);
          } else if (repType === 'development') {
            headers = ['No', 'Siswa', 'Semester', 'Tahun Ajaran', 'NAM', 'Fisik Motorik', 'Kognitif', 'Bahasa', 'Sosial Emosional', 'Seni'];
            const devs = await supabaseRest('developments?select=*').catch(() => []);
            let list = devs || [];
            if (params.semester) list = list.filter(d => String(d.semester) === String(params.semester));
            if (params.year) list = list.filter(d => d.academic_year && d.academic_year.includes(params.year));
            rows = list.map((d, idx) => [
              idx + 1,
              d.student_name || '-',
              d.semester || '1',
              d.academic_year || '2026/2027',
              d.aspect_nam || 'Baik',
              d.aspect_fisik_motorik || 'Baik',
              d.aspect_kognitif || 'Baik',
              d.aspect_bahasa || 'Baik',
              d.aspect_sosial_emosional || 'Baik',
              d.aspect_seni || 'Baik'
            ]);
          } else if (repType === 'all') {
            headers = ['No', 'Modul Data', 'Jumlah Entitas Terdaftar', 'Keterangan'];
            const [stu, cls, inv, regs] = await Promise.all([
              supabaseRest('students?select=id').catch(() => []),
              supabaseRest('classes?select=id').catch(() => []),
              supabaseRest('invoices?select=id').catch(() => []),
              supabaseRest('ppdb_registrations?select=id').catch(() => [])
            ]);
            rows = [
              [1, 'Siswa Aktif', `${(stu || []).length} Siswa`, 'Terdaftar di database yayasan'],
              [2, 'Rombongan Belajar (Kelas)', `${(cls || []).length} Kelas`, 'Tahun Ajaran 2026/2027'],
              [3, 'Tagihan SPP Tercatat', `${(inv || []).length} Tagihan`, 'Total transaksi keuangan'],
              [4, 'Pendaftar PPDB', `${(regs || []).length} Berkas`, 'Penerimaan siswa baru']
            ];
          }

          return {
            success: true,
            data: {
              report_type: repType,
              headers,
              rows,
              total: rows.length,
              generated_at: new Date().toISOString()
            },
            message: 'Laporan berhasil dibuat'
          };
        }
      }

      // 17. BACKUP & RESTORE
      if (module === 'backup') {
        if (action === 'backups' || action === 'list') {
          let backups = [];
          try {
            const raw = localStorage.getItem('siy_backup_history');
            if (raw) backups = JSON.parse(raw);
          } catch (_) {}
          return { success: true, data: backups, items: backups, total: backups.length };
        }
        if (action === 'create') {
          let [sch, usr, stu, cls, tea, nws, gal, inv, reg] = await Promise.all([
            supabaseRest('schools?select=*').catch(() => []),
            supabaseRest('users?select=*').catch(() => []),
            supabaseRest('students?select=*').catch(() => []),
            supabaseRest('classes?select=*').catch(() => []),
            supabaseRest('teachers?select=*').catch(() => []),
            supabaseRest('news?select=*').catch(() => []),
            supabaseRest('galleries?select=*').catch(() => []),
            supabaseRest('invoices?select=*').catch(() => []),
            supabaseRest('ppdb_registrations?select=*').catch(() => [])
          ]);
          const dStr = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-').slice(0, 19);
          const filename = `backup-yayasan-${dStr}.json`;
          const backupObj = {
            id: 'BCK-' + Date.now(),
            created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
            folder_name: filename,
            filename: filename,
            file_count: 9,
            data: {
              schools: sch || [],
              users: usr || [],
              students: stu || [],
              classes: cls || [],
              teachers: tea || [],
              news: nws || [],
              galleries: gal || [],
              invoices: inv || [],
              registrations: reg || []
            }
          };
          try {
            let list = [];
            const raw = localStorage.getItem('siy_backup_history');
            if (raw) list = JSON.parse(raw);
            list.unshift(backupObj);
            localStorage.setItem('siy_backup_history', JSON.stringify(list.slice(0, 15)));
          } catch (_) {}
          return { success: true, data: backupObj, backup: backupObj, message: 'Backup berhasil dibuat: ' + filename };
        }
        if (action === 'restore') {
          if (params.data && typeof params.data === 'object') {
            try {
              localStorage.setItem('siy_restored_snapshot', JSON.stringify({ restored_at: new Date().toISOString(), ...params.data }));
            } catch (_) {}
          }
          return { success: true, message: 'Data backup berhasil dipulihkan ke sistem!' };
        }
        if (action === 'delete') {
          try {
            let list = [];
            const raw = localStorage.getItem('siy_backup_history');
            if (raw) list = JSON.parse(raw);
            list = list.filter(b => b.id !== params.id);
            localStorage.setItem('siy_backup_history', JSON.stringify(list));
          } catch (_) {}
          return { success: true, message: 'Riwayat cadangan berhasil dihapus' };
        }
      }

      // Default fallback generic CRUD if module corresponds directly to a table
      const items = await supabaseRest(`${module}?select=*`);
      return { success: true, data: items, raw: { items } };

    } catch (err) {
      const isLocalHost = typeof window !== 'undefined' && (window.location.hostname === 'localhost' || window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1');
      if (isLocalHost && !isSupabaseOffline) {
        isSupabaseOffline = true;
        const localRes = await callLocalBackend(module, action, params, method);
        if (localRes && localRes.success !== undefined) {
          return localRes;
        }
      }
      console.error(`apiCall Error [${module}/${action}]:`, err);
      return {
        success: false,
        message: err.message || 'Terjadi kesalahan sistem',
        error: { message: err.message }
      };
    }
  }

  function showToast(message, type = 'info') {
    let container = document.getElementById('toast-container');
    if (!container) {
      container = document.createElement('div');
      container.id = 'toast-container';
      container.className = 'fixed top-4 right-4 z-50 space-y-2';
      document.body.appendChild(container);
    }
    const colors = {
      success: 'bg-sky-50 border-sky-200 text-sky-900',
      error:   'bg-red-50 border-red-200 text-red-800',
      warning: 'bg-yellow-50 border-yellow-200 text-yellow-800',
      info:    'bg-blue-50 border-blue-200 text-blue-800'
    };
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = `px-4 py-3 rounded-md border text-sm shadow-sm transition-all duration-300 ${colors[type] || colors.info}`;
    el.textContent = message;
    container.appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(() => {
      el.classList.add('opacity-0');
      setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 300);
    }, 4000);
  }

  function formatIDR(n) {
    return 'Rp ' + (Number(n) || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID');
  }
  function formatDate(iso) {
    if (!iso) return '-';
    const d = new Date(iso);
    if (isNaN(d)) return iso;
    return d.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });
  }
  function formatDateTime(iso) {
    if (!iso) return '-';
    const d = new Date(iso);
    if (isNaN(d)) return iso;
    return d.toLocaleString('id-ID', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
  }

  // Global exports
  window.apiCall = apiCall;
  window.showToast = showToast;
  window.formatIDR = formatIDR;
  window.formatDate = formatDate;
  window.formatDateTime = formatDateTime;
  window.uploadToSupabaseStorage = uploadToSupabaseStorage;
})();

  function getMenuIcon(m) {
    if (m.icon) return m.icon;
    const found = ADMIN_MENU.find(item => item.href === m.href);
    return (found && found.icon) || DEFAULT_ICON;
  }

  function renderDesktopNav(menu, active) {
    return menu.map(m => {
      const isActive = m.href === active || (active && active.endsWith(m.href));
      const cls = isActive ? 'siy-nav-item siy-nav-active' : 'siy-nav-item';
      const icon = getMenuIcon(m);
      return `<a href="${m.href}" class="${cls}">${icon}<span>${m.label}</span></a>`;
    }).join('');
  }

  function renderMobileNav(menu, active) {
    return menu.map(m => {
      const isActive = m.href === active || (active && active.endsWith(m.href));
      const cls = isActive ? 'siy-nav-item siy-nav-active' : 'siy-nav-item';
      const icon = getMenuIcon(m);
      return `<a href="${m.href}" class="${cls}" onclick="Shell.closeNav()">${icon}<span>${m.label}</span></a>`;
    }).join('');
  }

  function buildShell(opts) {

            </button>` : ''}
            ${userAvatarHtml}
          </div>

        <div class="px-3 py-3 border-t border-gray-200">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-2 py-2 mb-2 rounded-md bg-gray-50">
            ${userAvatarHtml}
            <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-900 truncate">${userName}</div>
              <div class="text-[10px] text-gray-500 capitalize truncate">${userRoleLabel}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onclick="logout()" class="flex items-center gap-3 w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-md">
            <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M17 16l4-4m0 0l-4-4m4 4H7m6 4v1a3 3 0 01-3 3H6a3 3 0 01-3-3V7a3 3 0 013-3h4a3 3 0 013 3v1"/></svg>
            Logout
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div class="flex-1 md:ml-64 min-w-0">
        <header class="hidden md:block bg-white border-b border-gray-200 sticky top-0 z-20">
          <div class="px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-4 flex justify-between items-center gap-4">
            <div class="min-w-0">
              <h1 class="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 truncate">${title}</h1>
              ${subtitle ? `<p class="text-sm text-gray-500 truncate">${subtitle}</p>` : ''}
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
              ${opts.showRefresh ? `
              <button onclick="Shell.refresh()" class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-50">
                <svg id="siy-refresh-icon-desktop" class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 4v5h.582M19.418 15A8.003 8.003 0 014 12m0 0V7m0 5h5m11 4v-5h-.581M4.582 9A8.003 8.003 0 0120 12m0 0v5m0-5h-5"/></svg>
                Refresh
              </button>` : ''}
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                ${userAvatarHtml}
                <span class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hidden lg:inline truncate">${userName}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>
        <div id="siy-error-banner" role="alert" class="hidden mx-4 sm:mx-6 lg:mx-8 mt-4 p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-md flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <div class="min-w-0">
            <div class="text-sm font-medium text-red-800">Gagal memuat data</div>
            <div id="siy-error-message" class="text-xs text-red-700 mt-1 break-words"></div>
          </div>
          <button onclick="Shell.refresh()" class="shrink-0 text-sm font-medium text-red-700 hover:text-red-900 underline">Coba lagi</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  const state = { onRefresh: null };

  function ensurePageTransitions() {
    if (window.__SIY_PAGE_TRANSITIONS_LOADED__) return;
    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = '/shared/js/page-transitions.js';
    script.onerror = function () {
      const script2 = document.createElement('script');
      script2.src = '../../shared/js/page-transitions.js';
      document.body.appendChild(script2);
    };
    document.body.appendChild(script);
  }

  function init(opts) {
    ensurePageTransitions();
    state.onRefresh = opts && opts.onRefresh ? opts.onRefresh : null;
    const shell = buildShell(opts || {});
    const slot = document.getElementById('app-shell');
    if (!slot) {
      // Fallback: prepend ke body
      const wrap = document.createElement('div');
      wrap.innerHTML = shell;
      document.body.insertBefore(wrap.firstElementChild, document.body.firstChild);
    } else {
      slot.innerHTML = shell;
    }
    const main = document.querySelector('main') || document.getElementById('main-slot');
    if (main && !main.classList.contains('md:ml-64')) {
      main.classList.add('md:ml-64', 'min-w-0');
    }
  }

  function openNav() {
    const drawer = document.getElementById('siy-mobile-nav');
    const backdrop = document.getElementById('siy-nav-backdrop');
    if (!drawer || !backdrop) return;
    drawer.classList.remove('pointer-events-none');
    drawer.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
    requestAnimationFrame(() => backdrop.classList.replace('opacity-0', 'opacity-100'));
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
  }
  function closeNav() {
    const drawer = document.getElementById('siy-mobile-nav');
    const backdrop = document.getElementById('siy-nav-backdrop');
    if (!drawer || !backdrop) return;
    backdrop.classList.replace('opacity-100', 'opacity-0');
    drawer.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    setTimeout(() => {
      drawer.classList.add('pointer-events-none');
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }, 250);
  }

  function showError(msg) {
    const banner = document.getElementById('siy-error-banner');
    const msgEl = document.getElementById('siy-error-message');
    if (banner && msgEl) {
      banner.classList.remove('hidden');
      msgEl.textContent = msg || 'Tidak dapat terhubung ke server.';
    }
  }
  function hideError() {
    const banner = document.getElementById('siy-error-banner');
    if (banner) banner.classList.add('hidden');
  }
  function startRefresh() {
    const icons = [document.getElementById('siy-refresh-icon'), document.getElementById('siy-refresh-icon-desktop')].filter(Boolean);
    icons.forEach(i => i.classList.add('animate-spin'));
    return () => icons.forEach(i => i.classList.remove('animate-spin'));
  }
  function refresh() {
    if (typeof state.onRefresh === 'function') {
      hideError();
      state.onRefresh();
    }
  }

  // Skeleton helper: replace isi node dengan shimmer placeholder.
  function skeleton(node, h = 'h-7', w = 'w-16') {
    if (!node) return;
    node.innerHTML = `<div class="skeleton ${h} ${w} rounded inline-block"></div>`;
  }

  // Skeleton rows helper for tables
  function skeletonRows(cols = 5, rows = 3) {
    let html = '';
    for (let i = 0; i < rows; i++) {
      html += '<tr>';
      for (let j = 0; j < cols; j++) {
        html += '<td class="px-4 py-3"><div class="skeleton h-4 w-full rounded"></div></td>';
      }
      html += '</tr>';
    }
    return html;
  }

  // Set text helper: kalau null/undefined/empty tampil "-".
  function setText(id, val) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!el) return;
    el.textContent = (val === null || val === undefined || val === '') ? '-' : val;
  }

  window.Shell = { init, openNav, closeNav, refresh, showError, hideError, startRefresh, skeleton, skeletonRows, setText, ADMIN_MENU };
})();

// Sessions store: token -> user
const SESSIONS = new Map();

function createToken(user) {
  const token = 'TOKEN-' + user.role.toUpperCase() + '-' + Date.now();
  SESSIONS.set(token, user);
  return token;
}

// Pre-create initial tokens for fast login
const ADMIN_TOKEN = createToken(DB.users[0]);
const TU_TOKEN = createToken(DB.users[1]);
const GURU_TOKEN = createToken(DB.users[2]);
const ORTU_TOKEN = createToken(DB.users[3]);

// RBAC helper — return 403 if requester's role is not in `allowed`.
// `allowed` may be a string or array. Empty array = require any authenticated role.
function rbacDeny(params, allowed) {
  let role = params._user_role;
  if (role && typeof role === 'object') role = role.code;
  if (!role) return { success: false, message: 'Sesi tidak valid. Silakan login ulang.', error: { code: 'UNAUTHORIZED' } };
  const roleNorm = String(role).toLowerCase();
  const list = (Array.isArray(allowed) ? allowed : [allowed]).map(r => String(r).toLowerCase());
  const isMatch = list.includes(roleNorm) || 
                  (roleNorm === 'siswa_aktif' && list.includes('siswa')) ||
                  (roleNorm === 'siswa' && list.includes('siswa_aktif'));
  if (list.length > 0 && !isMatch) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Anda tidak memiliki akses ke fitur ini', error: { code: 'FORBIDDEN' } };
  }
  return null;
}

function handleApiRequest(apiPath, params, method) {
  const cleanPath = String(apiPath).replace(/^\/+/, '').replace(/^api\/v1\//, '');
  const [module, action] = cleanPath.split('/');

  // 1. AUTH
  if (module === 'auth') {
    if (action === 'login') {
      const identifier = (params.identifier || params.email || params.username || params.nis || '').trim();
      const password = (params.password || '').trim();
      const idLower = identifier.toLowerCase();

      // 1. Cek DB.users (Admin, TU, Guru, Ortu)
      let user = DB.users.find(u => 
        (u.email && u.email.toLowerCase() === idLower) ||
        (u.id && u.id.toLowerCase() === idLower) ||
        (u.username && u.username.toLowerCase() === idLower)
      );

      if (user) {
        if (user.password !== password) {
          return { success: false, message: 'Password salah.', error: { code: 'UNAUTHORIZED', message: 'Password salah.' } };
        }
        const token = createToken(user);
        return {
          success: true,
          data: {
            token,
            user: {
              id: user.id,
              name: user.name,
              full_name: user.full_name || user.name,
              email: user.email,
              role: user.role,
              role_label: user.role_label,
              avatar: user.avatar,
              teacher_id: user.teacher_id,
              parent_id: user.parent_id,
              student_id: user.student_id
            }
          }
        };
      }

      // 2. Cek DB.students (Siswa Aktif via NIS / ID)
      let student = DB.students.find(s => 
        (s.nis && String(s.nis) === identifier) ||
        (s.id && s.id.toLowerCase() === idLower) ||
        (s.nisn && String(s.nisn) === identifier)
      );

      if (student) {
        const bDate = student.birth_date ? String(student.birth_date).trim() : '';
        const bDateClean = bDate.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
        const passClean = password.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
        let ddmmyyyy = '';
        if (bDate && bDate.includes('-')) {
          const parts = bDate.split('-');
          if (parts.length === 3) ddmmyyyy = parts[2] + parts[1] + parts[0];
        }

        const matchesBirthdate = password === bDate || 
                                 password === ddmmyyyy || 
                                 (passClean.length >= 6 && passClean === bDateClean) || 
                                 (passClean.length >= 6 && passClean === ddmmyyyy);
        const matchesCustomPwd = student.password && student.password === password;

        if (!matchesBirthdate && !matchesCustomPwd) {
          return { 
            success: false, 
            message: 'Password salah. Untuk siswa awal, gunakan Tanggal Lahir (YYYY-MM-DD).', 
            error: { code: 'UNAUTHORIZED', message: 'Password salah.' } 
          };
        }

        const isPasswordChanged = Boolean(student.is_password_changed);
        const studentUser = {
          id: 'USR-' + student.nis,
          name: student.nickname || student.name || student.full_name,
          full_name: student.full_name || student.name,
          email: student.parent_email || `${student.nis}@siswa.yayasan.sch.id`,
          role: { code: 'siswa_aktif', name: 'Siswa Aktif' },
          role_label: 'Siswa Aktif',
          avatar: student.photo_url || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1544717305-2782549b5136?w=150',
          student_id: student.id,
          nis: student.nis,
          class_id: student.class_id,
          class_name: student.class_name,
          is_password_changed: isPasswordChanged
        };
        const token = 'TOKEN-SISWA-' + student.nis + '-' + Date.now();
        SESSIONS.set(token, studentUser);

        return {
          success: true,
          data: {
            token,
            user: studentUser
          }
        };
      }

      return { success: false, message: 'Akun tidak ditemukan. Periksa Email atau NIS Anda.', error: { code: 'UNAUTHORIZED', message: 'Akun tidak ditemukan' } };
    }

    if (action === 'change_password') {
      const { user_id, new_password, old_password, skip_old } = params;
      const student = DB.students.find(s => ('USR-' + s.nis === user_id) || (s.id === user_id) || (s.nis === user_id) || (s.user_id === user_id));
      if (student) {
        student.password = String(new_password);
        student.is_password_changed = true;
      }
      const u = DB.users.find(x => x.id === user_id || (student && x.student_id === student.id) || (params._user_email && x.email && x.email.toLowerCase() === params._user_email.toLowerCase()));
      if (u) {
        if (!skip_old && old_password && u.password !== old_password) {
          return { success: false, message: 'Password lama salah', error: { code: 'INVALID_OLD_PASSWORD' } };
        }
        u.password = String(new_password);
        u.is_password_changed = true;
      }
      return { success: true, message: 'Password berhasil diperbarui', data: { message: 'Password berhasil diperbarui' } };
    }

    if (action === 'me') {
      const token = params.token;
      const user = SESSIONS.get(token) || DB.users[0];
      return {
        success: true,
        data: {
          user: {
            id: user.id,
            name: user.name,
            full_name: user.full_name || user.name,
            email: user.email,
            role: user.role,
            role_label: user.role_label,
            avatar: user.avatar,
            teacher_id: user.teacher_id,
            parent_id: user.parent_id,
            student_id: user.student_id,
            nis: user.nis,
            class_id: user.class_id,
            class_name: user.class_name
          }
        }
      };
    }

    if (action === 'logout') {
      if (params.token) SESSIONS.delete(params.token);
      return { success: true, data: { message: 'Berhasil logout' } };
    }
  }

  // 2. PPDB
  if (module === 'ppdb') {
    if (action === 'submit' || action === 'register') {
      const regId = 'REG-' + new Date().getFullYear() + '-' + String(DB.ppdb_registrations.length + 1).padStart(3, '0');
      const studentName = params.full_name || params.child_name || params.student_name || 'Santri Baru';
      const parentName = params.parent_name || params.father_name || params.mother_name || params.guardian_name || 'Orang Tua';
      const phoneNum = params.phone || params.parent_phone || params.father_phone || params.mother_phone || '';
      const emailAddr = params.email || params.parent_email || '';
      const lvl = params.level || (params.form_type === 'tpq' ? 'TPQ' : 'PAUD');
      const photo = params.photo_url || params.child_photo_url || '';
      const birthCert = params.birth_certificate_url || '';
      const familyCard = params.family_card_url || '';

      const newReg = {
        id: regId,
        registration_number: regId,
        registration_no: regId,
        full_name: studentName,
        child_name: studentName,
        student_name: studentName,
        nickname: params.nickname || '',
        gender: params.gender || params.student_gender || 'L',
        student_gender: params.gender || params.student_gender || 'L',
        birth_place: params.birth_place || params.student_birth_place || 'Samarinda',
        student_birth_place: params.birth_place || params.student_birth_place || 'Samarinda',
        birth_date: params.birth_date || params.student_birth_date || '2020-01-01',
        student_birth_date: params.birth_date || params.student_birth_date || '2020-01-01',
        address: params.address || '',
        form_type: params.form_type || (lvl === 'TPQ' ? 'tpq' : 'paud'),
        level: lvl,
        period_id: params.period_id || 'PPDPER-2026',
        period_name: params.period_name || 'PPDB 2026/2027 Gelombang 1',
        father_name: params.father_name || parentName,
        father_occupation: params.father_occupation || '',
        father_phone: params.father_phone || phoneNum,
        mother_name: params.mother_name || '',
        mother_occupation: params.mother_occupation || '',
        mother_phone: params.mother_phone || '',
        guardian_name: params.guardian_name || '',
        parent_name: parentName,
        phone: phoneNum,
        parent_phone: phoneNum,
        email: emailAddr,
        parent_email: emailAddr,
        photo_url: photo,
        child_photo_url: photo,
        birth_certificate_url: birthCert,
        family_card_url: familyCard,
        documents_url: {
          photo: photo,
          birth_certificate: birthCert,
          family_card: familyCard
        },
        notes: params.notes || '',
        status: 'pending',
        created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        registration_date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]
      };
      DB.ppdb_registrations.unshift(newReg);
      return {
        success: true,
        data: {
          id: regId,
          registration_no: regId,
          registration_number: regId,
          child_name: newReg.child_name,
          full_name: newReg.full_name,
          registration: newReg
        }
      };
    }

    if (action === 'check_status' || action === 'status') {
      const regQuery = params.registration_number || params.id || params.reg || '';
      const reg = DB.ppdb_registrations.find(r => 
        String(r.registration_number).toLowerCase() === String(regQuery).toLowerCase() ||
        String(r.id).toLowerCase() === String(regQuery).toLowerCase()
      );
      if (!reg) {
        return { success: false, message: 'Nomor pendaftaran tidak ditemukan', error: { code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'Nomor pendaftaran tidak ditemukan' } };
      }
      if (reg.status === 'converted') {
        const linkedStudent = DB.students.find(s => (reg.student_id && s.id === reg.student_id) || (reg.converted_nis && s.nis === reg.converted_nis) || (s.full_name === (reg.child_name || reg.full_name)));
        if (linkedStudent) {
          reg.converted_nis = linkedStudent.nis;
          reg.class_name = linkedStudent.class_name;
          reg.student_id = linkedStudent.id;
        }
      }
      return {
        success: true,
        data: {
          ...reg,
          registration: reg
        }
      };
    }

    if (action === 'get') {
      const deny = rbacDeny(params, ['super_admin', 'admin_tu']);
      if (deny) return deny;
      const reg = DB.ppdb_registrations.find(r => r.id === params.id || r.registration_number === params.id);
      if (!reg) return { success: false, message: 'Pendaftar tidak ditemukan' };
      return { success: true, data: reg };
    }

    if (action === 'list') {
      const deny = rbacDeny(params, ['super_admin', 'admin_tu']);
      if (deny) return deny;
      let list = [...DB.ppdb_registrations];
      if (params.status) {
        if (params.status === 'pending') {
          list = list.filter(r => r.status === 'pending' || r.status === 'submitted' || r.status === 'waiting_verification');
        } else if (params.status === 'approved') {
          list = list.filter(r => r.status === 'approved' || r.status === 'accepted' || r.status === 'verified');
        } else if (params.status === 'rejected') {
          list = list.filter(r => r.status === 'rejected');
        } else if (params.status === 'converted') {
          list = list.filter(r => r.status === 'converted');
        }
      }
      if (params.search || params.q) {
        const q = String(params.search || params.q).toLowerCase();
        list = list.filter(r => 
          (r.full_name && r.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
          (r.child_name && r.child_name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
          (r.registration_number && r.registration_number.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
          (r.parent_name && r.parent_name.toLowerCase().includes(q))
        );
      }
      if (params.form_type) {
        list = list.filter(r => String(r.form_type).toLowerCase() === String(params.form_type).toLowerCase() || String(r.level).toLowerCase() === String(params.form_type).toLowerCase());
      }
      return {
        success: true,
        data: {
          items: list,
          total: list.length,
          pagination: { page: 1, pageSize: 50, total: list.length }
        }
      };
    }

    if (action === 'accept' || action === 'approve') {
      const deny = rbacDeny(params, ['super_admin', 'admin_tu']);
      if (deny) return deny;
      const reg = DB.ppdb_registrations.find(r => r.id === params.id || r.registration_number === params.id);
      if (reg) {
        reg.status = 'approved';
        if (params.notes) reg.notes = params.notes;
      }
      return { success: true, data: { message: 'Pendaftar berhasil disetujui', registration: reg } };
    }

    if (action === 'reject') {
      const deny = rbacDeny(params, ['super_admin', 'admin_tu']);
      if (deny) return deny;
      const reg = DB.ppdb_registrations.find(r => r.id === params.id || r.registration_number === params.id);
      if (reg) {
        reg.status = 'rejected';
        reg.notes = params.reason || params.notes || 'Persyaratan administrasi belum terpenuhi';
      }
      return { success: true, data: { message: 'Pendaftar ditolak', registration: reg } };
    }

    if (action === 'update') {
      const deny = rbacDeny(params, ['super_admin', 'admin_tu']);
      if (deny) return deny;
      const reg = DB.ppdb_registrations.find(r => r.id === params.id || r.registration_number === params.id);
      if (!reg) return { success: false, message: 'Pendaftar tidak ditemukan' };

      if (params.full_name || params.child_name || params.student_name) {
        const sName = params.full_name || params.child_name || params.student_name;
        reg.full_name = sName; reg.child_name = sName; reg.student_name = sName;
      }
      if (params.nickname !== undefined) reg.nickname = params.nickname;
      if (params.gender !== undefined) { reg.gender = params.gender; reg.student_gender = params.gender; }
      if (params.birth_place !== undefined) { reg.birth_place = params.birth_place; reg.student_birth_place = params.birth_place; }
      if (params.birth_date !== undefined) { reg.birth_date = params.birth_date; reg.student_birth_date = params.birth_date; }
      if (params.address !== undefined) reg.address = params.address;
      if (params.parent_name !== undefined) reg.parent_name = params.parent_name;
      if (params.father_name !== undefined) reg.father_name = params.father_name;
      if (params.mother_name !== undefined) reg.mother_name = params.mother_name;
      if (params.phone !== undefined || params.parent_phone !== undefined) {
        const p = params.phone || params.parent_phone;
        reg.phone = p; reg.parent_phone = p; reg.father_phone = p;
      }
      if (params.email !== undefined || params.parent_email !== undefined) {
        const e = params.email || params.parent_email;
        reg.email = e; reg.parent_email = e;
      }
      if (params.level !== undefined) { reg.level = params.level; reg.form_type = params.level === 'TPQ' ? 'tpq' : 'paud'; }
      if (params.notes !== undefined) reg.notes = params.notes;
      reg.updated_at = new Date().toISOString();

      return { success: true, message: 'Data pendaftar berhasil diperbarui', data: reg };
    }

    if (action === 'delete') {
      const deny = rbacDeny(params, ['super_admin', 'admin_tu']);
      if (deny) return deny;
      const idx = DB.ppdb_registrations.findIndex(r => r.id === params.id || r.registration_number === params.id);
      if (idx === -1) return { success: false, message: 'Pendaftar tidak ditemukan' };
      const deleted = DB.ppdb_registrations.splice(idx, 1)[0];
      return { success: true, message: 'Data pendaftar berhasil dihapus', data: deleted };
    }

    if (action === 'convert') {
      const deny = rbacDeny(params, ['super_admin', 'admin_tu']);
      if (deny) return deny;
      const reg = DB.ppdb_registrations.find(r => r.id === params.id || r.registration_number === params.id);
      if (!reg) return { success: false, message: 'Pendaftar tidak ditemukan' };

      // 1. Guard Idempotensi: Cegah konversi ganda untuk pendaftar yang sama
      if (reg.status === 'converted') {
        return { success: false, message: 'Pendaftar ini sudah pernah dikonversi menjadi siswa aktif.' };
      }

      const nextSeq = DB.students.length + 1;
      const targetNis = params.nis || ('2026' + String(nextSeq).padStart(3, '0'));

      // 2. Guard Keunikan NIS: Pastikan NIS belum terdaftar di data siswa aktif
      const isNisTaken = DB.students.some(s => String(s.nis).trim() === String(targetNis).trim());
      if (isNisTaken) {
        return { success: false, message: `NIS ${targetNis} sudah digunakan oleh siswa aktif lain. Silakan gunakan nomor NIS lain.` };
      }

      const targetClass = DB.classes.find(c => c.id === (params.class_id || 'CLS-001')) || {};
      const studentName = reg.child_name || reg.full_name || reg.student_name || 'Santri Baru';
      const studentBirthDate = reg.birth_date || reg.student_birth_date || '2020-01-01';

      const newStu = {
        id: 'STU-' + String(nextSeq).padStart(3, '0'),
        nis: targetNis,
        full_name: studentName,
        name: studentName,
        child_name: studentName,
        nickname: reg.nickname || '',
        gender: reg.gender || reg.student_gender || 'L',
        class_id: params.class_id || 'CLS-001',
        class_name: targetClass.name || 'PAUD A - Bintang Kecil',
        birth_place: reg.birth_place || reg.student_birth_place || 'Samarinda',
        birth_date: studentBirthDate,
        parent_id: 'PAR-' + String(nextSeq).padStart(3, '0'),
        parent_name: reg.parent_name || reg.father_name || reg.mother_name || '-',
        parent_email: reg.parent_email || reg.email || '',
        parent_phone: reg.parent_phone || reg.phone || reg.father_phone || '',
        phone: reg.phone || reg.parent_phone || '',
        photo_url: reg.photo_url || reg.child_photo_url || '',
        address: reg.address || '',
        status: 'active'
      };
      DB.students.push(newStu);

      // Sinkronisasi akun user portal jika belum ada
      const existingUser = DB.users.find(u => u.nis === targetNis || (u.student_id && u.student_id === newStu.id));
      if (!existingUser) {
        DB.users.push({
          id: 'USR-' + targetNis,
          email: newStu.parent_email || `${targetNis}@siswa.yayasan.sch.id`,
          nis: targetNis,
          password_hash: studentBirthDate.replace(/\D/g, ''),
          full_name: studentName,
          name: studentName,
          phone: newStu.parent_phone,
          role: 'siswa_aktif',
          role_label: 'Siswa Aktif',
          status: 'active',
          student_id: newStu.id
        });
      }

      reg.status = 'converted';
      reg.converted_nis = targetNis;
      reg.student_id = newStu.id;
      reg.class_name = newStu.class_name;
      const defaultPass = studentBirthDate ? studentBirthDate.replace(/\D/g, '') : ('ortu' + String(nextSeq).padStart(3, '0'));
      const initialCredentials = {
        student_name: newStu.full_name,
        username: targetNis,
        nis: targetNis,
        default_password: defaultPass,
        password_hint: defaultPass,
        login_url: '/login.html'
      };
      return {
        success: true,
        message: 'Pendaftar berhasil dikonversi menjadi siswa aktif.',
        data: {
          message: 'Pendaftar berhasil dikonversi menjadi siswa aktif.',
          registration: reg,
          student: newStu,
          nis: targetNis,
          class_name: newStu.class_name,
          parent_user: {
            email: newStu.parent_email,
            full_name: newStu.parent_name
          },
          initial_credentials: initialCredentials
        }
      };
    }

    if (action === 'period' || action === 'periods') {
      const activePeriod = (DB.ppdb_periods && DB.ppdb_periods[0]) || {
        id: 'PPDPER-2026',
        name: 'PPDB 2026/2027 Gelombang 1',
        academic_year: '2026/2027',
        status: 'open',
        online_registration_dates: '01 Feb - 31 Mar 2026',
        verification_dates: '01 - 05 Apr 2026',
        announcement_dates: '10 Apr 2026',
        re_registration_dates: '12 - 20 Apr 2026',
        notes: 'Pendaftaran Peserta Didik Baru Gelombang 1 Resmi Dibuka. Silakan mengisi formulir pendaftaran secara online.'
      };
      return {
        success: true,
        data: action === 'period' ? activePeriod : { items: DB.ppdb_periods, periods: DB.ppdb_periods, period: activePeriod }
      };
    }

    if (action === 'update_period') {
      const deny = rbacDeny(params, ['super_admin', 'admin_tu']);
      if (deny) return deny;
      if (!DB.ppdb_periods || !DB.ppdb_periods[0]) {
        DB.ppdb_periods = [{ id: 'PPDPER-2026' }];
      }
      Object.assign(DB.ppdb_periods[0], params);
      return { success: true, message: 'Status gelombang PPDB berhasil diperbarui', data: DB.ppdb_periods[0] };
    }
  }

  // 2B. SISWA PORTAL (get_profile, update_biodata, get_academic, get_spp, get_documents, upload_document)
  if (module === 'siswa') {
    const findStudent = () => {
      const studentId = params.student_id || params.id;
      if (studentId) {
        const found = DB.students.find(s => s.id === studentId || s.nis === studentId);
        if (found) return found;
      }
      if (params.nis) {
        const found = DB.students.find(s => s.nis === params.nis);
        if (found) return found;
      }
      if (params._user_id) {
        const found = DB.students.find(s => s.id === params._user_id || ('USR-' + s.nis) === params._user_id);
        if (found) return found;
      }
      if (params._user_email) {
        const emailLower = params._user_email.toLowerCase();
        const found = DB.students.find(s => (s.parent_email && s.parent_email.toLowerCase() === emailLower) || (s.email && s.email.toLowerCase() === emailLower));
        if (found) return found;
      }
      return DB.students[0];
    };

    const targetStudent = findStudent();

    if (action === 'get_profile') {
      if (!targetStudent) {
        return { success: false, message: 'Data siswa tidak ditemukan', error: { code: 'NOT_FOUND' } };
      }
      const parent = DB.parents ? DB.parents.find(p => p.id === targetStudent.parent_id) : null;
      const profileData = {
        ...targetStudent,
        name: targetStudent.full_name || targetStudent.name,
        full_name: targetStudent.full_name || targetStudent.name,
        child_name: targetStudent.child_name || targetStudent.full_name || targetStudent.name,
        kelas_sekarang: targetStudent.class_name || targetStudent.kelas_sekarang || 'TK B Terpadu',
        semester_sekarang: targetStudent.semester_sekarang || 'Semester 1 • TA 2026/2027',
        status_kenaikan: targetStudent.status_kenaikan || 'Aktif',
        father_name: targetStudent.father_name || (parent ? parent.father_name || parent.name : 'Bapak Muhammad Fauzi'),
        father_phone: targetStudent.father_phone || (parent ? parent.phone : '081234567893'),
        mother_name: targetStudent.mother_name || (parent ? parent.mother_name : 'Ibu Siti Aminah'),
        address: targetStudent.address || (parent ? parent.address : 'Jl. Ahmad Yani No. 88, Samarinda'),
        religion: targetStudent.religion || 'Islam',
        nik: targetStudent.nik || '6472010101210001',
        previous_school: targetStudent.previous_school || 'KB Al-Ihsan',
        blood_type: targetStudent.blood_type || 'O',
        hobbies: targetStudent.hobbies || 'Mewarnai & Bersepeda',
