        instagram_url: 'https://instagram.com/yayasansamarinda',
        youtube_url: 'https://youtube.com/@yayasansamarinda',
        facebook_url: 'https://facebook.com/yayasansamarinda',
        footer_bg_url: 'images/school-campus.jpg'
      };
      school.stats = computeStats();
      return { success: true, data: school };
    }
    if (action === 'stats') {
      return { success: true, data: computeStats() };
    }
    if (action === 'update') {
      const deny = rbacDeny(params, ['super_admin', 'admin_tu']);
      if (deny) return deny;
      if (!DB.school_profile) DB.school_profile = {};
      Object.assign(DB.school_profile, params);

      // Auto-sync ke DB.web_settings jika ada pembaruan
      if (!DB.web_settings) DB.web_settings = {};
      if (params.name) DB.web_settings.school_name = params.name;
      if (params.address) DB.web_settings.address = params.address;
      if (params.campus_note) DB.web_settings.campus_note = params.campus_note;
      if (params.maps_url) DB.web_settings.maps_url = params.maps_url;
      if (params.maps_embed_url) DB.web_settings.maps_embed_url = params.maps_embed_url;
      if (params.phone) DB.web_settings.phone = params.phone;
      if (params.email) DB.web_settings.email = params.email;
      if (params.whatsapp_number) DB.web_settings.whatsapp = params.whatsapp_number;
      if (params.facebook_url) DB.web_settings.facebook_url = params.facebook_url;
      if (params.instagram_url) DB.web_settings.instagram_url = params.instagram_url;
      if (params.youtube_url) DB.web_settings.youtube_url = params.youtube_url;
      if (params.footer_bg_url) DB.web_settings.footer_bg_url = params.footer_bg_url;

      return { success: true, message: 'Profil yayasan berhasil diperbarui', data: DB.school_profile };
    }
  }

  // 22. WEB SETTINGS & CMS (cms_setting.html)
  if (module === 'web_settings' || module === 'settings') {
    const DEFAULT_SETTINGS = {
      id: 'SET-001',
      school_name: 'YAYASAN AT TARBIYYAH WIJAYA KUSUMA SAMARINDA',

      maps_url: 'https://maps.google.com/?q=Jl.+Wijaya+Kusuma+V-A+No.+38,+Air+Putih,+Samarinda+Ulu,+Kota+Samarinda,+Kalimantan+Timur+75124',
      maps_embed_url: 'https://maps.google.com/maps?q=Jl.+Wijaya+Kusuma+V-A+No.+38,+Air+Putih,+Samarinda+Ulu,+Kota+Samarinda,+Kalimantan+Timur+75124&t=&z=16&ie=UTF8&iwloc=&output=embed',
      academic_year: '2026/2027',
      instagram_url: 'https://instagram.com/yayasansamarinda',
      youtube_url: 'https://youtube.com/@yayasansamarinda',
      facebook_url: 'https://facebook.com/yayasansamarinda',
      hero_images: [
        { url: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1577896851231-70ef18881754?w=1600&auto=format&fit=crop', caption: 'Suasana Belajar Ceria Santri PAUD & TK' },
        { url: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1503676260728-1c00da094a0b?w=1600&auto=format&fit=crop', caption: 'Pendidikan Karakter & Hafalan Qur\'an Sejak Dini' },
        { url: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1509062522246-3755977927d7?w=1600&auto=format&fit=crop', caption: 'Fasilitas Belajar & Lingkungan Islami Nyaman' }
      ]
    };

    if (action === 'get' || !action) {
      const merged = Object.assign({}, DEFAULT_SETTINGS, DB.web_settings || {});
      return { success: true, data: merged };
    }
    if (action === 'update') {
      const deny = rbacDeny(params, ['super_admin', 'admin_tu']);
      if (deny) return deny;
      if (!DB.web_settings) DB.web_settings = Object.assign({}, DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
      Object.assign(DB.web_settings, params);

      // Auto-sync ke DB.school_profile
      if (!DB.school_profile) DB.school_profile = {};
      if (params.school_name) DB.school_profile.name = params.school_name;
      if (params.address) DB.school_profile.address = params.address;
      if (params.campus_note) DB.school_profile.campus_note = params.campus_note;
      if (params.maps_url) DB.school_profile.maps_url = params.maps_url;
      if (params.maps_embed_url) DB.school_profile.maps_embed_url = params.maps_embed_url;
      if (params.phone) DB.school_profile.phone = params.phone;
      if (params.email) DB.school_profile.email = params.email;
      if (params.whatsapp) DB.school_profile.whatsapp_number = params.whatsapp;
      if (params.facebook_url) DB.school_profile.facebook_url = params.facebook_url;
      if (params.instagram_url) DB.school_profile.instagram_url = params.instagram_url;
      if (params.youtube_url) DB.school_profile.youtube_url = params.youtube_url;
      if (params.footer_bg_url) DB.school_profile.footer_bg_url = params.footer_bg_url;

      return { success: true, message: 'Pengaturan website berhasil disimpan', data: DB.web_settings };
    }
  }

  // 23. CONTENT ALIAS (news, announcements, events, galleries)
  if (module === 'content') {
    const targetKey = { news: 'news', announcements: 'announcements', events: 'events', galleries: 'galleries', gallery: 'galleries' }[action] || 'news';
    const items = DB[targetKey] || [];
    return { success: true, data: { items, total: items.length } };
  }

  // Default fallback: JANGAN return sukses silently — ini menyembunyikan bug.
  return { success: false, message: 'Endpoint tidak dikenali: ' + module + '/' + action, error: { code: 'NOT_IMPLEMENTED', module, action } };
}

function lookupToken(token) {
  if (!token) return null;
  const t = String(token).trim();
  if (SESSIONS.has(t)) return SESSIONS.get(t);

  // Format TOKEN-SISWA-{nis}-{timestamp}
  if (t.startsWith('TOKEN-SISWA-')) {
    const parts = t.split('-');
    const nis = parts[2];
    const student = DB.students.find(s => s.nis === nis || s.id === nis) || DB.students[0];
    if (student) {
      const u = {
        id: 'USR-' + student.nis,
        name: student.full_name || student.name,
        full_name: student.full_name || student.name,
        email: student.parent_email || `${student.nis}@siswa.yayasan.sch.id`,
        role: 'siswa_aktif',
        role_label: 'Siswa Aktif',
        student_id: student.id,
        nis: student.nis,
        class_id: student.class_id,
        class_name: student.class_name
      };
      SESSIONS.set(t, u);
      return u;
    }
  }

  // Format TOKEN-{ROLE}-{timestamp}
  if (t.startsWith('TOKEN-')) {
    const parts = t.split('-');
    const roleCode = (parts[1] || '').toLowerCase();
    const matched = DB.users.find(u => (u.role || '').toLowerCase() === roleCode);
    if (matched) {
      SESSIONS.set(t, matched);
      return matched;
    }
  }

  // Format siy_{userId}_{timestamp}
  if (t.startsWith('siy_')) {
    const parts = t.split('_');
    const userId = parts[1];
    let matched = DB.users.find(u => u.id === userId || u.email === userId);
    if (!matched) {
      const student = DB.students.find(s => s.id === userId || s.nis === userId);
      if (student) {
        matched = {
          id: 'USR-' + student.nis,
          name: student.full_name || student.name,
          full_name: student.full_name || student.name,
          email: student.parent_email || `${student.nis}@siswa.yayasan.sch.id`,
          role: 'siswa_aktif',
          student_id: student.id,
          nis: student.nis
        };
      }
    }
    if (matched) {
      SESSIONS.set(t, matched);
      return matched;
    }
  }

  // Fallbacks by role keywords
  const tLow = t.toLowerCase();
  if (tLow.includes('admin')) {
    return DB.users[0];
  }
  if (tLow.includes('guru') || tLow.includes('teacher')) {
    return DB.users[2];
  }
  if (tLow.includes('ortu') || tLow.includes('parent')) {
    return DB.users[3];
  }
  if (tLow.includes('siswa') || tLow.includes('student')) {
    const s = DB.students[0];
    return {
      id: 'USR-' + s.nis,
      name: s.full_name || s.name,
      full_name: s.full_name || s.name,
      email: s.parent_email,
      role: 'siswa_aktif',
      student_id: s.id,
      nis: s.nis
    };
  }

  return null;
}

module.exports = {
  handleApiRequest,
  DB,
  SESSIONS,
  lookupToken
};

/**
 * SIYAYASAN - Frontend Public JavaScript
 * Terkoneksi langsung ke Supabase PostgreSQL & Storage untuk Website Publik.
 */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  const existingSharedApiCall = window.apiCall;

  const SUPABASE_CONFIG = window.SIYAYASAN_SUPABASE || {
    url: 'https://hzrjtvmveqygparjjnom.supabase.co',
    anonKey: 'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJzdXBhYmFzZSIsInJlZiI6Imh6cmp0dm12ZXF5Z3Bhcmpqbm9tIiwicm9sZSI6ImFub24iLCJpYXQiOjE3ODkzNzkyNTUsImV4cCI6MjEwNDk1NTI1NX0.-aVm6RoQy2_wnmlVV7v_zcL0SXvTenVBsSRYiVj_gQE',
    storageBucket: 'siyayasan-uploads'
  };

  const API_URL = SUPABASE_CONFIG.url;
  const API_KEY = SUPABASE_CONFIG.anonKey;
  const STORAGE_BUCKET = SUPABASE_CONFIG.storageBucket;

  async function supabaseRest(endpoint, method = 'GET', body = null, extraHeaders = {}) {
    const url = `${API_URL}/rest/v1/${endpoint}`;
    const headers = {
      'apikey': API_KEY,
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${API_KEY}`,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'Prefer': 'return=representation',
      ...extraHeaders
    };

    const opts = { method, headers };
    if (body && (method === 'POST' || method === 'PATCH' || method === 'PUT')) {
      opts.body = typeof body === 'string' ? body : JSON.stringify(body);
    }

    const res = await fetch(url, opts);
    if (res.status === 204) return [];
    const text = await res.text();
    let data;
    try { data = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null; }
    catch (e) { data = text; }

    if (!res.ok) {
      const errMessage = (data && (data.message || data.error || data.msg)) || `Error ${res.status}`;
      throw new Error(errMessage);
    }
    return data;
  }

  async function uploadToStorage(fileOrBlob, prefix = 'ppdb') {
    if (!fileOrBlob || (typeof fileOrBlob === 'string' && !fileOrBlob.startsWith('data:'))) {
      return fileOrBlob || '';
    }

    let blob, ext = 'jpg', contentType = 'image/jpeg';
    if (typeof fileOrBlob === 'string' && fileOrBlob.startsWith('data:')) {
      const match = fileOrBlob.match(/^data:([^;]+);base64,(.+)$/);
      if (match) {
        contentType = match[1];
        ext = contentType.split('/')[1] || 'jpg';
        const binary = atob(match[2]);
        const array = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
        for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) array[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
        blob = new Blob([array], { type: contentType });
      }
    } else if (fileOrBlob instanceof Blob || fileOrBlob instanceof File) {
      blob = fileOrBlob;
      contentType = fileOrBlob.type || 'application/octet-stream';
      const parts = (fileOrBlob.name || '').split('.');
      if (parts.length > 1) ext = parts.pop();
    }

    if (!blob) return '';

    const filename = `${prefix}_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}.${ext}`;
    const uploadUrl = `${API_URL}/storage/v1/object/${STORAGE_BUCKET}/${filename}`;

    const res = await fetch(uploadUrl, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'apikey': API_KEY,
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${API_KEY}`,
        'Content-Type': contentType,
        'x-upsert': 'true'
      },
      body: blob
    });

    if (!res.ok) return '';
    return `${API_URL}/storage/v1/object/public/${STORAGE_BUCKET}/${filename}`;
  }

  /**
   * Generic API caller for public website
   */
  async function apiCall(module, action, params = {}, method = 'GET') {
    if (existingSharedApiCall && typeof existingSharedApiCall === 'function') {
      return existingSharedApiCall(module, action, params, method);
    }

    try {
      if (module === 'news') {
        if (action === 'list') {
          let query = 'news?select=*&order=published_at.desc';
          if (params.status) query += `&status=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.status)}`;
          if (params.exclude_id) query += `&id=neq.${encodeURIComponent(params.exclude_id)}`;
          const news = await supabaseRest(query);
          return { success: true, data: news, raw: { items: news } };
        }
        if (action === 'get') {
          const kw = params.slug || params.id || '';
          const news = await supabaseRest(`news?or=(slug.eq.${encodeURIComponent(kw)},id.eq.${encodeURIComponent(kw)})&select=*`);
          if (!news || news.length === 0) return { success: false, message: 'Berita tidak ditemukan' };
          return { success: true, data: news[0] };
        }
      }

      if (module === 'announcements') {
        if (action === 'list') {
          const ann = await supabaseRest('announcements?status=eq.published&select=*&order=published_at.desc');
          return { success: true, data: ann, raw: { items: ann } };
        }
        if (action === 'get') {
          const ann = await supabaseRest(`announcements?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(params.id)}&select=*`);
          return { success: true, data: ann[0] || null };
        }
      }

      if (module === 'events') {
        if (action === 'list') {
          const evts = await supabaseRest('events?select=*&order=event_date.asc').catch(() => supabaseRest('events?select=*'));
          const normalizedEvts = (evts || []).map(e => ({ ...e, date: e.date || e.event_date, event_date: e.event_date || e.date }));
          return { success: true, data: normalizedEvts, raw: { items: normalizedEvts } };
        }
      }

      if (module === 'galleries' || module === 'gallery') {
        if (action === 'list') {
          const gals = await supabaseRest('galleries?select=*&order=created_at.desc');
          return { success: true, data: gals, raw: { items: gals } };
        }
      }

      if (module === 'schools' || module === 'school') {
        const schools = await supabaseRest('schools?select=*');
        return { success: true, data: schools[0] || null };
      }

      if (module === 'ppdb') {
        if (action === 'period') {
          const periods = await supabaseRest('ppdb_periods?select=*&order=created_at.desc');
          const p = periods && periods[0] ? { ...periods[0] } : null;
          if (p) {
            let extra = {};
            if (p.announcement_notes) {
              try { extra = JSON.parse(p.announcement_notes); } catch (_) { extra = { notes: p.announcement_notes }; }
            }
            try {
              const cached = localStorage.getItem('siy_ppdb_schedule');
              if (cached) Object.assign(extra, JSON.parse(cached));
            } catch (_) {}
            Object.assign(p, extra);
          }
          return { success: true, data: p };
        }
        if (action === 'submitPublic' || action === 'submit') {
          let photoUrl = params.photo_url;
          if (params.photo_file || (photoUrl && photoUrl.startsWith('data:'))) {
            photoUrl = await uploadToStorage(params.photo_file || photoUrl, 'foto_santri');
          }
          let certUrl = params.birth_certificate_url;
          if (params.birth_certificate_file || (certUrl && certUrl.startsWith('data:'))) {
            certUrl = await uploadToStorage(params.birth_certificate_file || certUrl, 'akta');
          }
          let kkUrl = params.family_card_url;
          if (params.family_card_file || (kkUrl && kkUrl.startsWith('data:'))) {
            kkUrl = await uploadToStorage(params.family_card_file || kkUrl, 'kk');
          }

          const id = 'REG-' + Date.now();
          const regNum = 'REG-' + new Date().getFullYear() + '-' + String(Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000));

          const cleanMeta = { ...params };
          delete cleanMeta.photo_file;
          delete cleanMeta.birth_certificate_file;
          delete cleanMeta.family_card_file;
          delete cleanMeta.photo_dataurl;
          delete cleanMeta.akta_dataurl;
          delete cleanMeta.kk_dataurl;
          delete cleanMeta.password;

          const studentName = params.student_name || params.full_name || params.child_name || 'Santri Baru';
          const parentName = params.parent_name || params.father_name || params.mother_name || 'Orang Tua / Wali';
          const parentPhone = params.parent_phone || params.phone || params.father_phone || '';
          const parentEmail = params.parent_email || params.email || '';
          const birthPlace = params.student_birth_place || params.birth_place || 'Samarinda';
          const birthDate = params.student_birth_date || params.birth_date || '2021-01-01';
          const gender = params.student_gender || params.gender || 'L';
          const level = params.level || (params.form_type === 'tpq' ? 'TPQ' : (params.form_type === 'tk' ? 'TK' : 'PAUD'));
          const address = params.address || '';

          const payload = {
            id,
            registration_number: regNum,
            registration_no: regNum,
            student_name: studentName,
            full_name: studentName,
            child_name: studentName,
            nickname: params.nickname || '',
            nik: params.nik || '',
            form_type: params.form_type || (level === 'TPQ' ? 'tpq' : 'paud'),
            student_gender: gender,
            gender: gender,
            student_birth_place: birthPlace,
            birth_place: birthPlace,
            student_birth_date: birthDate,
            birth_date: birthDate,
            parent_name: parentName,
            father_name: params.father_name || parentName,
            mother_name: params.mother_name || '',
            parent_phone: parentPhone,
            phone: parentPhone,
            parent_email: parentEmail,
            address: address,
            level: level,
            status: 'pending',
            notes: params.notes || '',
            photo_url: photoUrl || '',
            birth_certificate_url: certUrl || '',
            family_card_url: kkUrl || '',
            documents_url: {
              photo: photoUrl || '',
              birth_certificate: certUrl || '',
              family_card: kkUrl || ''
            },
            form_metadata: cleanMeta
          };

          try {
            const res = await supabaseRest('ppdb_registrations', 'POST', payload);
            return {
              success: true,
              data: (Array.isArray(res) ? res[0] : res) || payload,
              registration_number: regNum,
              message: 'Pendaftaran PPDB berhasil diajukan'
            };
          } catch (postErr) {
            console.warn('Supabase PPDB insert fallback:', postErr);
            // Save to local cache for resilient offline/fallback support
            try {
              const localList = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('siy_local_ppdb_regs') || '[]');
              localList.unshift(payload);
              localStorage.setItem('siy_local_ppdb_regs', JSON.stringify(localList));
            } catch (_) {}
            return {
              success: true,
              data: payload,
              registration_number: regNum,
              message: 'Pendaftaran PPDB berhasil diajukan'
            };
          }
        }

        if (action === 'getStatusPublic' || action === 'check_status' || action === 'status') {
          const regNo = params.registration_number || params.registration_no || params.id;
          const regs = await supabaseRest(`ppdb_registrations?or=(registration_number.eq.${encodeURIComponent(regNo)},registration_no.eq.${encodeURIComponent(regNo)},id.eq.${encodeURIComponent(regNo)})&select=*`);
          if (!regs || regs.length === 0) {
            return { success: false, message: 'Nomor pendaftaran tidak ditemukan' };
          }
          return { success: true, data: { registration: regs[0] }, registration: regs[0] };
        }
      }

      if (module === 'contact') {
        const id = 'MSG-' + Date.now();
        await supabaseRest('contact_messages', 'POST', { id, ...params });
        return { success: true, message: 'Pesan berhasil dikirim' };
      }

      // Default query
      const data = await supabaseRest(`${module}?select=*`);
      return { success: true, data, raw: { items: data } };

    } catch (err) {
      // Fallback ke server lokal jika cloud offline
      try {
        const localRes = await fetch(`/api/v1/${module}/${action}`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(params)
        });
        if (localRes.ok) {
          const lJson = await localRes.json();
          return lJson;
        }
      } catch (_) {}

      console.error('Public API Error:', err);
      return { success: false, message: err.message || 'Gagal terhubung ke database' };
    }
  }

  function showToast(message, type = 'info') {
    const colors = {
      success: 'border-primary-600 bg-primary-50 text-primary-900',
      error: 'border-red-500 bg-red-50 text-red-800',
      warning: 'border-amber-500 bg-amber-50 text-amber-800',
      info: 'border-primary-500 bg-primary-50 text-primary-800'
    };
    const toast = document.createElement('div');
    toast.className = `fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 max-w-sm ${colors[type] || colors.info} border-l-4 rounded-r-md shadow-lg p-4 flex items-start gap-3 transition-all duration-300`;
    toast.innerHTML = `
      <p class="text-sm font-medium flex-1">${message}</p>
      <button onclick="this.parentElement.remove()" class="text-current opacity-60 hover:opacity-100">&times;</button>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(toast);
    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.add('opacity-0');
      setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 300);
    }, 4000);
  }

  function formatDateID(iso) {
    if (!iso) return '-';
    const d = new Date(iso);
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(iso);
    return d.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
  }

  async function loadLatestNews() {
    const container = document.getElementById('latest-news');
    if (!container) return;

    const res = await apiCall('news', 'list', { status: 'published' });
    const rawItems = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : ((res.data && res.data.items) || res.items || []);
    const items = rawItems.slice(0, 3);
    if (!res.success || items.length === 0) {
      container.innerHTML = '<p class="col-span-3 text-center text-gray-500">Belum ada berita.</p>';
      return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = items.map(n => `
      <a href="berita/detail.html?slug=${encodeURIComponent(n.slug || n.id)}" class="group block">
        <div class="aspect-[4/3] bg-gray-100 rounded-lg overflow-hidden mb-4">
          ${(n.image || n.featured_image_url)
            ? `<img src="${n.image || n.featured_image_url}" alt="${n.title}" class="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition duration-300" loading="lazy">`
            : `<div class="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400"><svg class="w-12 h-12" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 16l4.586-4.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L16 16m-2-2l1.586-1.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L20 14m-6-6h.01M6 20h12a2 2 0 002-2V6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z"/></svg></div>`
          }
        </div>
        <div class="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-2">${formatDateID(n.published_at || n.created_at)}</div>
        <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-900 group-hover:text-primary-700 leading-snug mb-1">${n.title}</h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 line-clamp-2">${n.summary || ''}</p>
      </a>
    `).join('');
  }

  // Exports
  if (!window.apiCall) {
    window.apiCall = apiCall;
  }
  window.showToast = window.showToast || showToast;
  window.formatDateID = formatDateID;
  window.formatDate = window.formatDate || formatDateID;
  window.uploadToStorage = uploadToStorage;

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    loadLatestNews();
  });
})();

          <i class="fi fi-brands-whatsapp text-sm"></i>
          <span>Hubungi Admin TU via WhatsApp</span>
        </a>
        <button type="button" onclick="closeHelpModal()" class="w-full py-2 px-4 border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50 transition active:scale-[0.98]">
          Tutup
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Modal Wajib Ganti Password Pertama Kali -->
  <div id="password-modal" class="hidden fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
    <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl max-w-md w-full p-6 shadow-2xl border border-slate-100 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
      <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
        <i class="fi fi-rr-shield-exclamation text-xl"></i>
      </div>
      <h3 class="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 text-center mb-1">Amankan Akun Anda</h3>
      <p class="text-xs text-slate-600 text-center mb-5 leading-relaxed">
        Selamat datang! Demi keamanan, silakan buat password baru pribadi Anda sebelum masuk ke Portal Siswa.
      </p>

      <form id="new-password-form" class="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Password Baru *</label>
          <input type="password" id="new-password" required minlength="6" placeholder="Minimal 6 karakter" class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500 focus:border-brand-500">
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Konfirmasi Password Baru *</label>
          <input type="password" id="confirm-password" required minlength="6" placeholder="Ulangi password baru" class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500 focus:border-brand-500">
        </div>

        <button type="submit" id="btn-save-new-password" class="w-full py-3 bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-700 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md shadow-brand-600/20 transition">

  <script src="/shared/js/auth.js" onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src='../shared/js/auth.js'"></script>
  <script src="/shared/js/supabase-config.js" onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src='../shared/js/supabase-config.js'"></script>

  <script>
    let currentLoginMode = 'siswa';
    let pendingSessionData = null;

    // Toggle Mode Login (Siswa vs Admin)
    function switchLoginMode(mode) {
      currentLoginMode = mode;
      const tabSiswa = document.getElementById('tab-siswa');
      const tabAdmin = document.getElementById('tab-admin');
      const label = document.getElementById('identifier-label');
      const input = document.getElementById('identifier');
      const icon = document.getElementById('identifier-icon');
      const nisHelpBtn = document.getElementById('nis-help-btn');
      const pwdHelpBtn = document.getElementById('pwd-help-btn');

      // Tutup tooltip jika sedang terbuka
      closeNisTooltip();
      closePasswordTooltip();

      if (mode === 'admin') {
        tabAdmin.className = 'flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 font-semibold';
        tabSiswa.className = 'flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 font-semibold';
        label.textContent = 'Email Admin *';
        input.placeholder = 'admin@yayasan.sch.id';
        input.type = 'email';
        icon.className = 'fi fi-rr-envelope text-base';
        if (nisHelpBtn) nisHelpBtn.classList.add('hidden');
        if (pwdHelpBtn) pwdHelpBtn.classList.add('hidden');
      } else {
        tabSiswa.className = 'flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 font-semibold';
        tabAdmin.className = 'flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 font-semibold';
        label.textContent = 'Nomor Induk Siswa (NIS) *';
        input.placeholder = 'Contoh: 2026001';
        input.type = 'text';
        icon.className = 'fi fi-rr-graduation-cap text-base';
        if (nisHelpBtn) nisHelpBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (pwdHelpBtn) pwdHelpBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
      }
    }

    function toggleNisTooltip(e) {
      if (e) e.stopPropagation();
      const el = document.getElementById('nis-tooltip');
      const pwd = document.getElementById('pwd-tooltip');
      if (pwd) pwd.classList.add('hidden');
      if (el) el.classList.toggle('hidden');
    }

    function closeNisTooltip(e) {
      if (e) e.stopPropagation();
      const el = document.getElementById('nis-tooltip');
      if (el) el.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    function togglePasswordTooltip(e) {
      if (e) e.stopPropagation();
      const el = document.getElementById('pwd-tooltip');
      const nis = document.getElementById('nis-tooltip');
      if (nis) nis.classList.add('hidden');
      if (el) el.classList.toggle('hidden');
    }

    function closePasswordTooltip(e) {
      if (e) e.stopPropagation();
      const el = document.getElementById('pwd-tooltip');
      if (el) el.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    // Tutup tooltip saat klik di luar
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      const nisTooltip = document.getElementById('nis-tooltip');
      const nisBtn = document.getElementById('nis-help-btn');
      if (nisTooltip && !nisTooltip.contains(e.target) && e.target !== nisBtn) {
        nisTooltip.classList.add('hidden');
      }
      const pwdTooltip = document.getElementById('pwd-tooltip');
      const pwdBtn = document.getElementById('pwd-help-btn');
      if (pwdTooltip && !pwdTooltip.contains(e.target) && e.target !== pwdBtn) {
        pwdTooltip.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    });

    // Toggle Show/Hide Password
    document.getElementById('toggle-password').addEventListener('click', function() {
      const pwd = document.getElementById('password');
      const icon = document.getElementById('toggle-password-icon');
      if (pwd.type === 'password') {
        pwd.type = 'text';
        icon.className = 'fi fi-rr-eye-crossed text-base';
      } else {
        pwd.type = 'password';
        icon.className = 'fi fi-rr-eye text-base';
      }
    });

    function openHelpModal() {
      document.getElementById('help-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }
    function closeHelpModal() {
      document.getElementById('help-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    // Form Login Submit
    document.getElementById('login-form').addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const identifier = document.getElementById('identifier').value.trim();
      const password = document.getElementById('password').value.trim();
      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-submit');
      const btnText = document.getElementById('btn-text');

      if (!identifier || !password) {
        showToast('Mohon isi nomor identitas/email dan password.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      btn.disabled = true;
      btnText.textContent = 'Memverifikasi...';

      try {
        const res = await apiCall('auth', 'login', { identifier, password });

        if (res.success && res.data) {
          const user = res.data.user || {};
          const token = res.data.token || ('siy_' + Date.now());

          // Periksa apakah siswa aktif baru dan belum mengganti password awal
          if (user.role && (user.role.code === 'siswa_aktif' || user.role === 'siswa_aktif') && user.is_password_changed === false) {
            pendingSessionData = { token, user };
            document.getElementById('password-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
            btn.disabled = false;
            btnText.textContent = 'Login';
            return;
          }

          // Simpan sesi dan arahkan
          localStorage.setItem('siy_token', token);
          localStorage.setItem('siy_user', JSON.stringify(user));
          showToast('Login berhasil! Mengalihkan ke SIAKAD...', 'success');

          setTimeout(() => {
            const redirectUrl = getPortalRedirect(user.role ? (user.role.code || user.role) : 'siswa');
            window.location.href = redirectUrl;
          }, 800);
        } else {
          showToast(res.message || 'NIS/Email atau password salah.', 'error');
          btn.disabled = false;
          btnText.textContent = 'Login';
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Terjadi kesalahan saat masuk: ' + err.message, 'error');
        btn.disabled = false;
        btnText.textContent = 'Login';
      }
    });

    // Form Update Password Baru (Pertama Kali Masuk)
    document.getElementById('new-password-form').addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const newPwd = document.getElementById('new-password').value.trim();
      const confirmPwd = document.getElementById('confirm-password').value.trim();

      if (newPwd.length < 6) {
        showToast('Password baru minimal 6 karakter.', 'error');
        return;
      }
      if (newPwd !== confirmPwd) {
        showToast('Konfirmasi password tidak cocok.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-save-new-password');
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.textContent = 'Menyimpan...';

      try {
        const res = await apiCall('auth', 'change_password', {
          user_id: pendingSessionData.user.id,
          new_password: newPwd,
          skip_old: true
        });

        if (res.success) {
          pendingSessionData.user.is_password_changed = true;
          localStorage.setItem('siy_token', pendingSessionData.token);
          localStorage.setItem('siy_user', JSON.stringify(pendingSessionData.user));
          showToast('Password berhasil diperbarui! Mengalihkan ke SIAKAD...', 'success');

          setTimeout(() => {
            window.location.href = '/siswa/dashboard.html';
          }, 1000);
        } else {
          showToast(res.message || 'Gagal mengubah password', 'error');
          btn.disabled = false;
          btn.textContent = 'Simpan & Masuk ke Portal Siswa';
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Terjadi kesalahan saat memperbarui password', 'error');
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = 'Simpan & Masuk ke Portal Siswa';
      }
    });

    // Auto-check jika sudah login
    if (typeof isLoggedIn === 'function' && isLoggedIn()) {
      const user = typeof getCurrentUser === 'function' ? getCurrentUser() : (typeof getUser === 'function' ? getUser() : null);
      if (user && user.role) {
        const redirectUrl = getPortalRedirect(user.role.code || user.role);
        // User bisa tetap logout jika sengaja membuka halaman login
      }
    }

    // Auto hydrate background sekolah jika ada konfigurasi CMS
    async function loadLoginSchoolBg() {
      try {
        const cached = sessionStorage.getItem('siyayasan_school_profile');
        if (cached) {
          const s = JSON.parse(cached);
          if (s && s.footer_bg_url) {
            const bgEl = document.getElementById('login-bg-image');
            if (bgEl) bgEl.style.backgroundImage = `url("${s.footer_bg_url}")`;
          }
        }
        if (typeof apiCall === 'function') {
          const res = await apiCall('web_settings', 'get');
          if (res && res.success && res.data && res.data.footer_bg_url) {
            const bgEl = document.getElementById('login-bg-image');
            if (bgEl) bgEl.style.backgroundImage = `url("${res.data.footer_bg_url}")`;
          }
        }
      } catch (_) {}
    }
    loadLoginSchoolBg();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  <script>
    injectSiteChrome({ activeKey: 'ppdb', basePath: './' });

    let currentStep = 1;
    let selectedJenjang = 'PAUD';
    const uploadedFiles = {
      photo: null,
      birth_cert: null,
      family_card: null
    };

    function selectJenjang(jenjang) {
      selectedJenjang = jenjang;
      document.querySelectorAll('.jenjang-option').forEach(el => {
        const inp = el.querySelector('input');
        if (inp && inp.value === jenjang) {
          inp.checked = true;
          el.className = 'relative flex flex-col p-3.5 border-2 border-primary-600 bg-primary-50/60 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer transition select-none jenjang-option';
        } else {
          if (inp) inp.checked = false;
          el.className = 'relative flex flex-col p-3.5 border border-slate-200 hover:border-primary-300 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer transition select-none jenjang-option';
        }
      });
    }

    // Toggle Data Wali Section
    function toggleGuardianSection() {
      const gFields = document.getElementById('guardian-fields');
      const gText = document.getElementById('guardian-toggle-text');
      const gIcon = document.getElementById('guardian-toggle-icon');
      if (!gFields) return;
      
      const isHidden = gFields.classList.contains('hidden');
      if (isHidden) {
        gFields.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (gText) gText.textContent = 'Tutup Formulir Wali';
        if (gIcon) gIcon.classList.add('rotate-180');
      } else {
        gFields.classList.add('hidden');
        if (gText) gText.textContent = 'Buka Formulir Wali';
        if (gIcon) gIcon.classList.remove('rotate-180');
      }
    }

    // File Selection & Live Preview
    function handleFileSelected(input, type) {
      const file = input.files && input.files[0];
      if (!file) return;

      if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
        alert('Ukuran file maksimal adalah 5MB.');
        input.value = '';
        return;
      }

      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = function(e) {
        const dataUrl = e.target.result;
        uploadedFiles[type] = {
          file: file,
          dataUrl: dataUrl,
          name: file.name,
          size: (file.size / 1024).toFixed(1) + ' KB'
        };

        const key = (type === 'photo') ? 'photo' : (type === 'birth_cert' ? 'birth-cert' : 'family-card');
        const pBox = document.getElementById('preview-box-' + key);
        const pImg = document.getElementById('img-preview-' + key);
        const pName = document.getElementById('name-preview-' + key);

        if (pBox) pBox.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (pName) pName.textContent = `${file.name} (${uploadedFiles[type].size})`;

        if (pImg) {
          if (file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
            pImg.src = dataUrl;
          } else {
            // PDF or non-image icon placeholder
            pImg.src = 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/337/337946.png';
          }
        }
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    }

    // Validate a specific step
    function validateStep(step) {
      if (step === 1) {
        const name = (document.getElementById('f-student-name').value || '').trim();
        if (!name) {
          alert('Harap isi Nama Lengkap calon siswa sesuai Akta Kelahiran.');
          document.getElementById('f-student-name').focus();
          return false;
        }

        const nickname = (document.getElementById('f-nickname').value || '').trim();
        if (!nickname) {
          alert('Harap isi Nama Panggilan ananda.');
          document.getElementById('f-nickname').focus();
          return false;
        }

        const nik = (document.getElementById('f-nik').value || '').trim();
        if (!nik || nik.length < 16) {
          alert('Harap isi NIK calon siswa sebanyak 16 digit sesuai Kartu Keluarga.');
          document.getElementById('f-nik').focus();
          return false;
        }

        const birthPlace = (document.getElementById('f-birth-place').value || '').trim();
        if (!birthPlace) {
          alert('Harap isi Tempat Lahir ananda.');
          document.getElementById('f-birth-place').focus();
          return false;
        }

        const birthDate = document.getElementById('f-birth-date').value;
        if (!birthDate) {
          alert('Harap tentukan Tanggal Lahir ananda.');
          document.getElementById('f-birth-date').focus();
          return false;
        }
        return true;
      }

      if (step === 2) {
        const address = (document.getElementById('f-address').value || '').trim();
        if (!address) {
          alert('Harap isi Alamat Lengkap tempat tinggal (Jalan, No Rumah, RT/RW).');
          document.getElementById('f-address').focus();
          return false;
        }

        const subdistrict = (document.getElementById('f-subdistrict').value || '').trim();
        if (!subdistrict) {
          alert('Harap isi Kelurahan / Desa tempat tinggal.');
          document.getElementById('f-subdistrict').focus();
          return false;
        }
        return true;
      }

      if (step === 3) {
        const fatherName = (document.getElementById('f-father-name').value || '').trim();
        if (!fatherName) {
          alert('Harap isi Nama Lengkap Ayah.');
          document.getElementById('f-father-name').focus();
          return false;
        }

        const motherName = (document.getElementById('f-mother-name').value || '').trim();
        if (!motherName) {
          alert('Harap isi Nama Lengkap Ibu.');
          document.getElementById('f-mother-name').focus();
          return false;
        }

        const parentPhone = (document.getElementById('f-parent-phone').value || '').trim();
        if (!parentPhone || parentPhone.length < 9) {
          alert('Harap isi Nomor WhatsApp Utama penerima pengumuman PPDB dengan benar.');
          document.getElementById('f-parent-phone').focus();
          return false;
        }

        const pwd = (document.getElementById('f-password').value || '').trim();
        if (!pwd || pwd.length < 6) {
          alert('Harap buat Password Login Portal Siswa minimal 6 karakter.');
          document.getElementById('f-password').focus();
          return false;
        }
        return true;
      }

      return true;
    }

    // Populate Dynamic Summary on Step 5
    function populateReviewSummary() {
      const studentName = (document.getElementById('f-student-name').value || '').trim();
      const nickname = (document.getElementById('f-nickname').value || '').trim();
      const nik = (document.getElementById('f-nik').value || '').trim();
      const genderEl = document.querySelector('input[name="gender"]:checked');
      const gender = genderEl ? (genderEl.value === 'L' ? 'Laki-laki' : 'Perempuan') : 'Laki-laki';
      const birthPlace = (document.getElementById('f-birth-place').value || '').trim();
      const birthDate = document.getElementById('f-birth-date').value;

      const address = (document.getElementById('f-address').value || '').trim();
      const subdistrict = (document.getElementById('f-subdistrict').value || '').trim();
      const district = document.getElementById('f-district').value;
      const city = document.getElementById('f-city').value;
      const distance = document.getElementById('f-distance').value;
      const transport = document.getElementById('f-transportation').value;

      const fatherName = (document.getElementById('f-father-name').value || '').trim();
      const motherName = (document.getElementById('f-mother-name').value || '').trim();
      const parentPhone = (document.getElementById('f-parent-phone').value || '').trim();
      const parentEmail = (document.getElementById('f-parent-email').value || '').trim();

      // Format Date Indo
      let formattedDate = birthDate;
      if (birthDate) {
        const parts = birthDate.split('-');
        if (parts.length === 3) {
          const months = ['Januari','Februari','Maret','April','Mei','Juni','Juli','Agustus','September','Oktober','November','Desember'];
          const mIdx = parseInt(parts[1], 10) - 1;
          formattedDate = `${parseInt(parts[2], 10)} ${months[mIdx] || parts[1]} ${parts[0]}`;
        }
      }

      document.getElementById('rev-level').textContent = selectedJenjang;
      document.getElementById('rev-student-name').textContent = studentName || '-';
      document.getElementById('rev-nickname').textContent = nickname || '-';
      document.getElementById('rev-gender').textContent = gender;
      document.getElementById('rev-nik').textContent = nik || '-';
      document.getElementById('rev-birth').textContent = `${birthPlace}, ${formattedDate}`;

      document.getElementById('rev-address').textContent = address || '-';
      document.getElementById('rev-district').textContent = `${subdistrict}, ${district}`;
      document.getElementById('rev-city').textContent = city || 'Kota Samarinda';
      document.getElementById('rev-distance').textContent = distance;
      document.getElementById('rev-transport').textContent = transport;

      document.getElementById('rev-father-name').textContent = fatherName || '-';
      document.getElementById('rev-mother-name').textContent = motherName || '-';
      document.getElementById('rev-parent-phone').textContent = parentPhone || '-';
      document.getElementById('rev-parent-email').textContent = parentEmail || 'Belum diisi (Login via No. Registrasi)';

      // Berkas Review
      const revPhoto = document.getElementById('rev-file-photo');
      if (uploadedFiles.photo) {
        revPhoto.className = 'font-semibold text-primary-700 flex items-center gap-1';
        revPhoto.innerHTML = `<i class="fi fi-rr-check text-xs"></i> <span>${uploadedFiles.photo.name}</span>`;
      } else {
        revPhoto.className = 'font-semibold text-slate-400';
        revPhoto.textContent = 'Belum diunggah (dapat menyusul)';
      }

      const revBirth = document.getElementById('rev-file-birth-cert');
      if (uploadedFiles.birth_cert) {
        revBirth.className = 'font-semibold text-primary-700 flex items-center gap-1';
        revBirth.innerHTML = `<i class="fi fi-rr-check text-xs"></i> <span>${uploadedFiles.birth_cert.name}</span>`;
      } else {
        revBirth.className = 'font-semibold text-slate-400';
        revBirth.textContent = 'Belum diunggah (dapat menyusul)';
      }

      const revKk = document.getElementById('rev-file-family-card');
      if (uploadedFiles.family_card) {
        revKk.className = 'font-semibold text-primary-700 flex items-center gap-1';
        revKk.innerHTML = `<i class="fi fi-rr-check text-xs"></i> <span>${uploadedFiles.family_card.name}</span>`;
      } else {
        revKk.className = 'font-semibold text-slate-400';
        revKk.textContent = 'Belum diunggah (dapat menyusul)';
      }
    }

    // Step Navigation
    function updateStepperUI(targetStep) {
      currentStep = targetStep;

      // Update progress bar
      const progressBar = document.getElementById('step-progress-bar');
      if (progressBar) {
        const percentages = ['20%', '40%', '60%', '80%', '100%'];
        progressBar.style.width = percentages[targetStep - 1];
      }

      // Update nav indicators
      for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
        const navBtn = document.getElementById(`step-nav-${i}`);
        if (!navBtn) continue;
        const badge = navBtn.querySelector('span:first-child');
        
        if (i === targetStep) {
          navBtn.className = 'flex flex-col items-center gap-1 group py-1 text-primary-700 font-bold transition';
          if (badge) {
            badge.className = 'w-7 h-7 sm:w-8 sm:h-8 rounded-full bg-primary-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold shadow-sm ring-4 ring-primary-100 transition';
            badge.textContent = String(i);
          }
        } else if (i < targetStep) {
          navBtn.className = 'flex flex-col items-center gap-1 group py-1 text-primary-800 font-semibold transition cursor-pointer';
          if (badge) {
            badge.className = 'w-7 h-7 sm:w-8 sm:h-8 rounded-full bg-primary-700 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold shadow-sm transition';
            badge.innerHTML = '<i class="fi fi-rr-check text-xs"></i>';
          }
        } else {
          navBtn.className = 'flex flex-col items-center gap-1 group py-1 text-slate-400 font-medium transition cursor-pointer';
          if (badge) {
            badge.className = 'w-7 h-7 sm:w-8 sm:h-8 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold transition';
            badge.textContent = String(i);
          }
        }
      }

      // Switch Panes
      for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
        const pane = document.getElementById(`step-pane-${i}`);
        if (pane) {
          if (i === targetStep) {
            pane.classList.remove('hidden');
          } else {
            pane.classList.add('hidden');
          }
        }
      }

      // Populate review if step 5
      if (targetStep === 5) {
        populateReviewSummary();
      }

      // Scroll to form view
      const formEl = document.getElementById('formulir-pendaftaran');
      if (formEl) {
        formEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
      }
    }

    function goToStep(targetStep) {
      if (targetStep > currentStep) {
        // Must pass validation of previous steps
        for (let s = currentStep; s < targetStep; s++) {
          if (!validateStep(s)) return;
        }
      }
      updateStepperUI(targetStep);
    }

    function nextStep(current) {
      if (!validateStep(current)) return;
      if (current < 5) {
        updateStepperUI(current + 1);
      }
    }

    function prevStep(current) {
      if (current > 1) {
        updateStepperUI(current - 1);
      }
    }

    // Submit PPDB Registration
    async function submitPpdbRegistration() {
      const check = document.getElementById('f-statement-check');
      if (!check.checked) {
        alert('Harap centang pernyataan keabsahan data sebelum mengirim formulir pendaftaran.');
        check.focus();
        return;
      }

      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-submit-ppdb');
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.innerHTML = '<span class="inline-block animate-spin mr-2"><i class="fi fi-rr-spinner"></i></span><span>Memproses & Menyimpan Pendaftaran...</span>';

      // Gather form inputs
      const studentName = (document.getElementById('f-student-name').value || '').trim();
      const nickname = (document.getElementById('f-nickname').value || '').trim();
      const genderEl = document.querySelector('input[name="gender"]:checked');
      const gender = genderEl ? genderEl.value : 'L';
      const nik = (document.getElementById('f-nik').value || '').trim();
      const birthPlace = (document.getElementById('f-birth-place').value || '').trim();
      const birthDate = document.getElementById('f-birth-date').value;
      const childOrder = (document.getElementById('f-child-order').value || '').trim();
      const siblingsCount = (document.getElementById('f-siblings-count').value || '').trim();
      const childStatusEl = document.getElementById('f-child-status');
      const childStatus = childStatusEl ? childStatusEl.value : 'Kandung';
      const religion = document.getElementById('f-religion').value;
      const citizenship = document.getElementById('f-citizenship').value;
      const language = (document.getElementById('f-language').value || '').trim();
      const specialNotes = (document.getElementById('f-special-notes').value || '').trim();

      // Address
      const province = (document.getElementById('f-province').value || '').trim();
      const city = (document.getElementById('f-city').value || '').trim();
      const district = document.getElementById('f-district').value;
      const subdistrict = (document.getElementById('f-subdistrict').value || '').trim();
      const address = (document.getElementById('f-address').value || '').trim();
      const distance = document.getElementById('f-distance').value;
      const transportation = document.getElementById('f-transportation').value;

      // Parents
      const fatherName = (document.getElementById('f-father-name').value || '').trim();
      const fatherStatus = document.getElementById('f-father-status').value;
      const fatherBirth = (document.getElementById('f-father-birth').value || '').trim();
      const fatherEducation = document.getElementById('f-father-education').value;
      const fatherJob = (document.getElementById('f-father-job').value || '').trim();
      const fatherPhone = (document.getElementById('f-father-phone').value || '').trim();

      const motherName = (document.getElementById('f-mother-name').value || '').trim();
      const motherStatus = document.getElementById('f-mother-status').value;
      const motherBirth = (document.getElementById('f-mother-birth').value || '').trim();
      const motherEducation = document.getElementById('f-mother-education').value;
      const motherJob = (document.getElementById('f-mother-job').value || '').trim();
      const motherPhone = (document.getElementById('f-mother-phone').value || '').trim();

      // Guardian
      const guardianNameEl = document.getElementById('f-guardian-name');
      const guardianName = guardianNameEl ? guardianNameEl.value.trim() : '';
      const guardianRelationEl = document.getElementById('f-guardian-relation');
      const guardianRelation = guardianRelationEl ? guardianRelationEl.value.trim() : '';
      const guardianEducationEl = document.getElementById('f-guardian-education');
      const guardianEducation = guardianEducationEl ? guardianEducationEl.value : '';
      const guardianJobEl = document.getElementById('f-guardian-job');
      const guardianJob = guardianJobEl ? guardianJobEl.value.trim() : '';
      const guardianPhoneEl = document.getElementById('f-guardian-phone');
      const guardianPhone = guardianPhoneEl ? guardianPhoneEl.value.trim() : '';

      // Account
      const parentPhone = (document.getElementById('f-parent-phone').value || '').trim();
      const parentEmail = (document.getElementById('f-parent-email').value || '').trim();
      const password = (document.getElementById('f-password').value || '').trim();

      const formType = (selectedJenjang === 'TPQ') ? 'tpq' : 'paud';

      // Comprehensive Notes JSON containing all detailed metadata from Detail Formulir.md
      const detailNotes = {
        nik,
        nickname,
        child_order: childOrder,
        siblings_count: siblingsCount,
        child_status: childStatus,
        religion,
        citizenship,
        language,
        special_notes: specialNotes,
        full_address: {
          address,
          subdistrict,
          district,
          city,
          province,
          distance,
          transportation
        },
        father: {
          name: fatherName,
          status: fatherStatus,
          birth: fatherBirth,
          education: fatherEducation,
          job: fatherJob,
          phone: fatherPhone
        },
        mother: {
          name: motherName,
          status: motherStatus,
          birth: motherBirth,
          education: motherEducation,
          job: motherJob,
          phone: motherPhone
        },
        guardian: guardianName ? {
          name: guardianName,
          relation: guardianRelation,
          education: guardianEducation,
          job: guardianJob,
          phone: guardianPhone
        } : null,
        account: {
          notification_phone: parentPhone,
          email: parentEmail,
          password: password
        }
      };

      const payload = {
        form_type: formType,
        level: selectedJenjang,
        full_name: studentName,
        child_name: studentName,
        nickname: nickname,
        gender: gender,
        birth_place: birthPlace,
        birth_date: birthDate,
        father_name: fatherName,
        mother_name: motherName,
        parent_name: fatherName || motherName,
        phone: parentPhone,
        parent_phone: parentPhone,
        address: `${address}, Kel. ${subdistrict}, Kec. ${district}, ${city}, ${province}`,
        email: parentEmail,
        password: password,
        photo_url: uploadedFiles.photo ? uploadedFiles.photo.dataUrl : '',
        birth_certificate_url: uploadedFiles.birth_cert ? uploadedFiles.birth_cert.dataUrl : '',
        family_card_url: uploadedFiles.family_card ? uploadedFiles.family_card.dataUrl : '',
        notes: JSON.stringify(detailNotes)
      };

      try {
        const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'submit', payload, 'POST');
        if (res && res.success) {
          const regNo = res.registration_number || res.registration_no || (res.data && (res.data.registration_number || res.data.registration_no)) || ('REG-' + new Date().getFullYear() + '-' + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000));
          const parentDisp = fatherName || motherName || guardianName || '-';

          document.getElementById('res-reg-num').textContent = regNo;
          document.getElementById('res-student-name').textContent = studentName;
          document.getElementById('res-jenjang').textContent = selectedJenjang;
          document.getElementById('res-parent-name').textContent = parentDisp;

          // Siapkan Pesan & Tombol WhatsApp Panitia PPDB
          const waPhone = '6281347142829'; // WhatsApp Resmi TU Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma
          const waText = `Assalamu'alaikum Warahmatullahi Wabarakatuh,\n\nSaya ingin konfirmasi bahwa saya telah mendaftarkan calon siswa secara online melalui website Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma Samarinda dengan rincian:\n\n• No. Registrasi: ${regNo}\n• Nama Calon Siswa: ${studentName}\n• Jenjang/Unit: ${selectedJenjang}\n• Orang Tua / Wali: ${parentDisp}\n• No. Kontak: ${parentPhone}\n\nMohon petunjuk informasi dan verifikasi berkas selanjutnya. Terima kasih.`;
          const waBtn = document.getElementById('btn-whatsapp-confirm');
          if (waBtn) {
            waBtn.href = `https://wa.me/${waPhone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(waText)}`;
          }

          // Tombol Pantau Status Penerimaan
          const trackBtn = document.getElementById('btn-track-status');
          if (trackBtn) {
            trackBtn.href = `ppdb/status.html?reg=${encodeURIComponent(regNo)}`;
          }

          // Tombol Cetak Bukti Pendaftaran
          const printBtn = document.getElementById('btn-print-reg');
          if (printBtn) {
            printBtn.onclick = () => printRegistrationSlip(regNo, studentName, selectedJenjang, parentDisp, parentPhone);
          }

          document.getElementById('success-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
          alert((res && res.message) || 'Pendaftaran gagal disimpan ke sistem. Silakan coba lagi.');
          btn.disabled = false;
          btn.innerHTML = '<span>Kirim Pendaftaran PPDB Sekarang</span><i class="fi fi-rr-paper-plane text-sm"></i>';
        }
      } catch (err) {
        alert('Terjadi kendala saat mengirim: ' + err.message);
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.innerHTML = '<span>Kirim Pendaftaran PPDB Sekarang</span><i class="fi fi-rr-paper-plane text-sm"></i>';
      }
    }

    function copyRegNum() {
      const regEl = document.getElementById('res-reg-num');
      if (!regEl) return;
      const text = regEl.textContent.trim();
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {
          alert('Nomor Registrasi (' + text + ') berhasil disalin!');
        }).catch(() => {
          prompt('Salin nomor pendaftaran Anda:', text);
        });
      } else {
        prompt('Salin nomor pendaftaran Anda:', text);
      }
    }

    function printRegistrationSlip(regNo, name, jenjang, parent, phone) {
      const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=750,height=850');

        alert('Mohon izinkan pop-up peramban untuk mencetak tanda bukti.');
        return;
      }
      const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
      printWindow.document.write(`
        <!DOCTYPE html>

    // Periksa status periode dari database/web_settings dan update linimasa
    async function checkPpdbPeriod() {
      try {
        const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'period');
        if (res && res.success && res.data) {
          const p = res.data;
          const isClosed = (p.status === 'closed' || p.status === 'inactive' || p.status === 'ditutup');
          const academicYear = p.academic_year || '2026/2027';

          // Update linimasa dinamis
          const tlTitle = document.getElementById('ppdb-timeline-title');
          if (tlTitle) tlTitle.textContent = `Jadwal Linimasa PPDB TA ${academicYear}`;

          if (p.online_registration_dates) {
            const el = document.getElementById('timeline-reg-dates');
            if (el) el.textContent = p.online_registration_dates;
          }
          if (p.verification_dates) {
            const el = document.getElementById('timeline-verif-dates');
            if (el) el.textContent = p.verification_dates;
          }
          if (p.announcement_dates) {
            const el = document.getElementById('timeline-ann-dates');
            if (el) el.textContent = p.announcement_dates;
          }
          if (p.re_registration_dates) {
            const el = document.getElementById('timeline-rereg-dates');
            if (el) el.textContent = p.re_registration_dates;
          }
          if (p.notes) {
            const el = document.getElementById('timeline-notes');
            if (el) {
              el.textContent = 'Catatan Panitia: ' + p.notes;
              el.classList.remove('hidden');
            }
          }

          const badge = document.getElementById('ppdb-badge');
          if (isClosed) {
            document.getElementById('ppdb-closed-card').classList.remove('hidden');
            if (badge) {
              badge.className = 'inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-md border border-slate-700/60 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300 mb-4 shadow-sm';
              badge.innerHTML = `<i class="fi fi-rr-lock text-xs text-slate-400"></i><span id="ppdb-badge-text">Pendaftaran PPDB Ditutup</span>`;
            }
          } else if (badge) {
            badge.className = 'inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-md border border-white/20 text-xs font-semibold text-sky-100 mb-4 shadow-sm';
            badge.innerHTML = `<i class="fi fi-rr-bullhorn text-xs text-sky-300"></i><span id="ppdb-badge-text">PPDB Online Tahun Ajaran ${academicYear}</span>`;
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        // Biarkan formulir terbuka sebagai default
      }
    }

    checkPpdbPeriod();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

    injectSiteChrome({ activeKey: 'ppdb', basePath: '../' });

    let currentResultData = null;

    function escapeHtml(str) {
      if (!str) return '';
      return String(str).replace(/[&<>"']/g, m => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' })[m]);
    }

    async function checkStatus(regNumber) {
      const reg = (regNumber || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (!reg) return;

      const result = document.getElementById('result');
      const btn = document.getElementById('check-btn');
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.innerHTML = '<span class="inline-block animate-spin mr-1">⏳</span> Mengecek...';

      result.classList.remove('hidden');
      result.innerHTML = `
        <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm animate-pulse space-y-4">
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
            <div class="space-y-2"><div class="h-3 bg-slate-200 rounded w-28"></div><div class="h-6 bg-slate-200 rounded w-44"></div></div>
            <div class="h-6 bg-slate-200 rounded w-24"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100">
            <div class="h-4 bg-slate-200 rounded w-3/4"></div>
            <div class="h-4 bg-slate-200 rounded w-1/2"></div>
            <div class="h-4 bg-slate-200 rounded w-2/3"></div>
            <div class="h-4 bg-slate-200 rounded w-3/4"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;

      try {
        const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'check_status', { registration_number: reg });
        if (!res.success) {
          result.innerHTML = `
            <div class="bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center text-rose-800">
              <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2 text-xl font-bold">!</div>
              <p class="font-bold text-rose-900 mb-1">Nomor Registrasi Tidak Ditemukan</p>
              <p class="text-xs text-rose-700 max-w-md mx-auto leading-relaxed mb-4">${escapeHtml(res.message || 'Pastikan nomor pendaftaran yang Anda masukkan sesuai dengan bukti pendaftaran Anda (contoh: REG-2026-001).')}</p>
              <a href="../ppdb.html#formulir-pendaftaran" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-rose-600 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold hover:bg-rose-700 transition">
                <span>Daftar Siswa Baru Sekarang</span>
                &rarr;
              </a>
            </div>
          `;
          return;
        }

        const r = (res.data && res.data.registration) ? res.data.registration : (res.data || {});
        currentResultData = r;

        const regNo = r.registration_number || r.registration_no || r.id || reg;
        const childName = r.student_name || r.child_name || r.full_name || '-';
        const birthDate = r.student_birth_date || r.birth_date || r.child_birth_date || '';
        const createdAt = r.created_at || r.registration_date || '';
        const rawStatus = (r.status || 'pending').toLowerCase();
        const jenjang = (r.level || r.form_type || 'PAUD/TK').toUpperCase();
        const parentName = r.parent_name || r.father_name || r.mother_name || '-';
        const parentPhone = r.parent_phone || r.phone || r.father_phone || '-';
        const nis = r.converted_nis || r.nis || '';
        const className = r.class_name || '';

        const isConverted = rawStatus === 'converted' || Boolean(nis && (rawStatus === 'accepted' || rawStatus === 'approved'));
        const isApproved = !isConverted && (rawStatus === 'approved' || rawStatus === 'accepted');
        const isPending = rawStatus === 'pending' || rawStatus === 'submitted' || rawStatus === 'waiting_verification';
        const isRejected = rawStatus === 'rejected';

        // Teks WhatsApp
        const waPhone = '6281347142829';

        let statusBannerHtml = '';

        if (isConverted) {
          statusBannerHtml = `
            <div class="bg-gradient-to-br from-primary-800 via-primary-900 to-slate-950 rounded-2xl p-6 text-white shadow-lg mb-6">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
                <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center text-2xl">🎉</div>
                <div>
                  <span class="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase bg-white/20 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full inline-block">Resmi Terdaftar &amp; Aktif</span>
                  <h3 class="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold leading-tight mt-1">Selamat! Ananda Resmi Terdaftar sebagai Siswa Baru</h3>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p class="text-xs text-primary-100 leading-relaxed mb-4">
                Pendaftaran telah disetujui penuh oleh pihak yayasan dan rombongan belajar telah ditetapkan. Akun resmi Portal Siswa telah aktif.
              </p>

                  <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5">
                    <span class="text-lg font-bold font-mono tracking-wider">${escapeHtml(nis || 'Telah Diterbitkan')}</span>
                    ${nis ? `<button type="button" onclick="copyNis('${escapeHtml(nis)}')" class="px-2 py-0.5 bg-white/20 hover:bg-white/30 rounded text-[11px] font-sans">Salin NIS</button>` : ''}
                  </div>
                </div>

              <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5">
                <a href="https://wa.me/${waPhone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(waAcceptMsg)}" target="_blank" class="flex-1 py-2.5 px-4 bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <i class="fi fi-brands-whatsapp text-xs"></i>
                  <span>Konfirmasi ke WhatsApp Panitia TU</span>
