                <button type="button" onclick="printOfficialLetter()" class="py-2.5 px-4 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-800 font-semibold text-xs rounded-xl transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <i class="fi fi-rr-print text-xs"></i>
                  <span>Cetak Surat Pengumuman</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        } else if (isPending) {
          const waPendingMsg = `Assalamu'alaikum Panitia PPDB Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma Samarinda,\n\nSaya ingin menanyakan progres verifikasi pendaftaran online atas nama ${childName} dengan No. Registrasi: ${regNo}. Terima kasih.`;
          statusBannerHtml = `
            <div class="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-2xl p-6 text-amber-950 mb-6">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-bold">⏳</div>
                <div>
                  <span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-700 uppercase tracking-wide bg-amber-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full inline-block">Proses: Menunggu Verifikasi</span>
                  <h3 class="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-amber-900 mt-0.5">Berkas Sedang Diverifikasi oleh Panitia TU</h3>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p class="text-xs text-amber-800 leading-relaxed mb-4">
                Formulir pendaftaran dan kelengkapan dokumen telah masuk ke sistem. Panitia PPDB memerlukan waktu 1–2 hari kerja untuk memvalidasi berkas administrasi. Pastikan nomor kontak WhatsApp Anda aktif.
              </p>
              <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5">
                <a href="https://wa.me/${waPhone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(waPendingMsg)}" target="_blank" class="flex-1 py-2.5 px-4 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <i class="fi fi-brands-whatsapp text-xs"></i>
                  <span>Tanya Panitia via WhatsApp</span>
                </a>
                <button type="button" onclick="printOfficialLetter()" class="py-2.5 px-4 bg-white border border-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-50 text-amber-900 font-semibold text-xs rounded-xl transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <i class="fi fi-rr-print text-xs"></i>
                  <span>Cetak Bukti Pendaftaran</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        } else if (isRejected) {
          const waRejectMsg = `Assalamu'alaikum Panitia PPDB,\n\nSaya ingin konsultasi mengenai status pendaftaran ${regNo} atas nama ${childName}. Terima kasih.`;
          statusBannerHtml = `
            <div class="bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-2xl p-6 text-rose-950 mb-6">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-bold">✕</div>
                <div>
                  <span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-rose-700 uppercase tracking-wide bg-rose-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full inline-block">Hasil Seleksi</span>
                  <h3 class="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-rose-900 mt-0.5">Mohon Maaf, Pendaftaran Belum Dapat Diterima</h3>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p class="text-xs text-rose-800 leading-relaxed mb-4">
                Kuota kelas untuk unit yang dipilih telah terpenuhi atau kelengkapan berkas belum memenuhi kualifikasi tahun ajaran ini.
              </p>
              <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5">
                <a href="https://wa.me/${waPhone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(waRejectMsg)}" target="_blank" class="flex-1 py-2.5 px-4 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <i class="fi fi-brands-whatsapp text-xs"></i>
                  <span>Konsultasi ke Sekretariat Yayasan</span>
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        }

        const statusBadges = {
          converted: '<span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-primary-100 text-primary-800 border border-primary-200">Siswa Aktif Terdaftar</span>',
          approved: '<span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-primary-100 text-primary-800 border border-primary-200">Diterima (Lolos Seleksi)</span>',
          accepted: '<span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-primary-100 text-primary-800 border border-primary-200">Diterima (Lolos Seleksi)</span>',
          pending: '<span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200">Menunggu Verifikasi TU</span>',
          submitted: '<span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200">Menunggu Verifikasi TU</span>',
          waiting_verification: '<span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200">Menunggu Verifikasi TU</span>',
          rejected: '<span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 border border-rose-200">Belum Diterima</span>'
        };

        result.innerHTML = `
          ${statusBannerHtml}

          <!-- Rincian Formulir Pendaftar -->
          <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
            <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between pb-4 mb-5 border-b border-slate-100 gap-3">
              <div>
                <span class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">Nomor Registrasi PPDB</span>
                <div class="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-mono tracking-wider tabular-nums">${escapeHtml(regNo)}</div>
              </div>
              <div>
                ${statusBadges[rawStatus] || `<span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-slate-100 text-slate-800">${escapeHtml(r.status)}</span>`}
              </div>
            </div>

            <dl class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs mb-5">
              <div><dt class="text-slate-500 mb-0.5">Nama Lengkap Calon Siswa</dt><dd class="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm">${escapeHtml(childName)}</dd></div>
              <div><dt class="text-slate-500 mb-0.5">Jenjang / Unit Tujuan</dt><dd class="font-bold text-primary-700 uppercase">${escapeHtml(jenjang)}</dd></div>
              <div><dt class="text-slate-500 mb-0.5">Tanggal Lahir</dt><dd class="font-medium text-slate-800">${birthDate || '-'}</dd></div>
              <div><dt class="text-slate-500 mb-0.5">Tanggal Mendaftar</dt><dd class="font-medium text-slate-800">${createdAt ? createdAt.split('T')[0] : '-'}</dd></div>
              <div><dt class="text-slate-500 mb-0.5">Nama Orang Tua / Wali</dt><dd class="font-semibold text-slate-800">${escapeHtml(parentName)}</dd></div>
              <div><dt class="text-slate-500 mb-0.5">Kontak WhatsApp Terdaftar</dt><dd class="font-mono text-slate-800">${escapeHtml(parentPhone)}</dd></div>
            </dl>

            ${r.notes ? `
              <div class="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 text-xs mb-4">
                <span class="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Catatan Resmi Panitia PPDB:</span>
                <p class="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">${escapeHtml(r.notes)}</p>
              </div>` : ''}

            <div class="pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs text-slate-500">
              <span>Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma &bull; Sekretariat PPDB Terpadu</span>
              <button type="button" onclick="window.scrollTo({top: 0, behavior: 'smooth'})" class="text-primary-600 hover:text-primary-800 font-semibold">Cari Nomor Lain &uarr;</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      } catch (err) {
        result.innerHTML = `
          <div class="bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center text-xs text-rose-800">
            Terjadi gangguan koneksi saat memeriksa data. Silakan coba kembali beberapa saat lagi.
          </div>
        `;
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fi fi-rr-search text-xs"></i><span>Periksa Status</span>';
      }
    }

    function copyNis(nis) {
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(nis).then(() => {
          alert('NIS (' + nis + ') berhasil disalin!');
        });
      } else {
        prompt('Salin NIS Anda:', nis);
      }
    }

    function printOfficialLetter() {
      if (!currentResultData) return;
      const r = currentResultData;
      const regNo = r.registration_number || r.registration_no || r.id;
      const childName = r.child_name || r.full_name || '-';
      const jenjang = (r.level || r.form_type || 'PAUD/TK').toUpperCase();
      const parentName = r.father_name || r.mother_name || r.parent_name || '-';
      const nis = r.converted_nis || r.nis || '-';
      const className = r.class_name || jenjang;
      const rawStatus = (r.status || 'pending').toLowerCase();
      const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });

      let statusDesc = 'DALAM PROSES VERIFIKASI ADMINISTRASI';
      if (rawStatus === 'converted') statusDesc = 'RESMI DITERIMA & TERDAFTAR SEBAGAI SISWA';
      else if (rawStatus === 'approved' || rawStatus === 'accepted') statusDesc = 'DINYATAKAN DITERIMA (LOLOS SELEKSI)';
      else if (rawStatus === 'rejected') statusDesc = 'BELUM DAPAT DITERIMA';

      const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=750,height=850');
      if (!printWindow) {
        alert('Mohon izinkan pop-up peramban untuk mencetak surat.');
        return;
      }

      printWindow.document.write(`
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html lang="id">
        <head>
          <meta charset="UTF-8">
          <title>Surat Keterangan Status PPDB - ${regNo}</title>
          <style>
            body { font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif; padding: 32px; color: #0f172a; line-height: 1.6; }
            .kop { text-align: center; border-bottom: 3px double #0284c7; padding-bottom: 12px; margin-bottom: 24px; }
            .kop h1 { margin: 0; color: #0369a1; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.5px; }
            .kop p { margin: 3px 0 0; font-size: 12px; color: #64748b; }
            .title { text-align: center; margin-bottom: 20px; }
            .title h2 { margin: 0; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; text-transform: uppercase; color: #1e293b; }
            .badge { display: inline-block; background: #e0f2fe; color: #0369a1; border: 1px solid #7dd3fc; padding: 6px 14px; font-weight: 700; font-size: 15px; border-radius: 6px; font-family: monospace; margin-top: 6px; }
            table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 13px; }
            td { padding: 9px 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; }
            td.lbl { width: 34%; font-weight: 600; color: #64748b; }
            td.val { font-weight: 600; color: #0f172a; }
            .box { background: #f8fafc; border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; border-radius: 8px; padding: 14px; font-size: 12px; color: #334155; }
            .ttd-wrap { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin-top: 36px; font-size: 12px; }
            .ttd { text-align: center; width: 220px; }
            @media print { .no-print { display: none !important; } }
          </style>
        </head>
        <body>
          <div class="kop">
            <h1>YAYASAN AT TARBIYYAH WIJAYA KUSUMA SAMARINDA</h1>
            <p>Pendidikan Terpadu: PAUD, Taman Kanak-Kanak (TK A &amp; TK B) dan TPQ</p>
            <p>Jl. Wijaya Kusuma V-A No. 38, Samarinda Ulu | WhatsApp Resmi: 0813-4714-2829</p>
          </div>
          <div class="title">
            <h2>Surat Keterangan Hasil Seleksi PPDB</h2>
            <div class="badge">${regNo}</div>
          </div>
          <table>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Nama Lengkap Siswa</td><td class="val">${childName}</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Jenjang / Unit</td><td class="val">${jenjang}</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Nama Orang Tua / Wali</td><td class="val">${parentName}</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Status Hasil Seleksi</td><td class="val" style="color: #0369a1;">${statusDesc}</td></tr>
            ${nis !== '-' ? `<tr><td class="lbl">Nomor Induk Siswa (NIS)</td><td class="val font-mono">${nis}</td></tr>` : ''}
            ${className ? `<tr><td class="lbl">Kelas / Rombel</td><td class="val">${className}</td></tr>` : ''}
          </table>
          <div class="box">
            <strong>Keterangan Resmi Yayasan:</strong>
            <p style="margin: 6px 0 0;">
              Dokumen ini diterbitkan secara otomatis oleh sistem administrasi Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma Samarinda sebagai bukti sah status penerimaan calon siswa baru. Untuk pengaktifan akun Portal Siswa, gunakan username berupa NIS dan password awal berupa Tanggal Lahir siswa.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div class="ttd-wrap">
            <div class="ttd">
              <p>Orang Tua / Wali,</p>
              <br><br><br>
              <p><strong>(${parentName})</strong></p>
            </div>
            <div class="ttd">
              <p>Samarinda, ${today}<br>Panitia PPDB Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma,</p>
              <br><br>
              <p><strong>( Bagian Administrasi &amp; TU )</strong></p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="no-print" style="text-align: center; margin-top: 30px;">
            <button onclick="window.print()" style="padding: 10px 24px; background: #0284c7; color: white; border: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold; cursor: pointer;">Cetak Sekarang</button>
          </div>
          <script>window.onload = function() { window.print(); };<\/script>
        </body>
        </html>
      `);
      printWindow.document.close();
    }

    document.getElementById('check-form').addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const reg = document.getElementById('reg-number').value.trim();
      checkStatus(reg);
    });

    // Auto-check if URL has query parameter `?reg=...`
    const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const regParam = urlParams.get('reg');
    if (regParam) {
      document.getElementById('reg-number').value = regParam;
      checkStatus(regParam);
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  <script src="../../shared/js/shell.js"></script>
  <script>
    requireAuth('super_admin,admin_tu');
    const user = getCurrentUser();

    const MENU = Shell.ADMIN_MENU;

    Shell.init({
      menu: MENU,
      active: 'dashboard.html',
      portalLabel: 'Portal Admin',
      title: 'Dashboard',
      subtitle: user ? 'Selamat datang kembali, ' + (user.full_name || user.name || 'Admin') + '.' : 'Selamat datang kembali.',
      showRefresh: true,
      onRefresh: loadDashboard
    });

    // Tanggal hari ini untuk label dashboard
    (function setTodayLabel() {
      // sengaja kosong - label tanggal dihapus karena bukan domain admin
    })();

    function showSkeleton() {
      const ids = ['stat-students', 'stat-classes', 'stat-teachers', 'stat-parents',
                   'stat-students-active', 'stat-teachers-active'];
      ids.forEach(id => Shell.skeleton(document.getElementById(id)));
    }

    async function loadDashboard() {
      Shell.hideError();
      showSkeleton();
      Shell.startRefresh();
      try {
        const res = await apiCall('dashboard', 'index');
        if (!res.success) {
          Shell.showError(res.message || 'Periksa koneksi Anda lalu coba lagi.');
          return;
        }
        // Backend kembalikan flat object {total_students, total_students_active, ...}
        const d = res.data || {};
        Shell.setText('stat-students', d.total_students != null ? d.total_students : 0);
        Shell.setText('stat-students-active', d.total_students_active != null ? d.total_students_active : (d.total_students != null ? d.total_students : 0));
        Shell.setText('stat-classes', d.total_classes != null ? d.total_classes : 0);
        Shell.setText('stat-teachers', d.total_teachers != null ? d.total_teachers : 0);
        Shell.setText('stat-teachers-active', d.total_teachers_active != null ? d.total_teachers_active : (d.total_teachers != null ? d.total_teachers : 0));
        Shell.setText('stat-parents', d.total_parents != null ? d.total_parents : 0);

        Shell.setText('ppdb-total', d.ppdb_total != null ? d.ppdb_total : (d.ppdb_pending != null ? d.ppdb_pending : 0));
        Shell.setText('ppdb-pending', d.ppdb_pending != null ? d.ppdb_pending : 0);
        Shell.setText('ppdb-approved', d.ppdb_accepted != null ? d.ppdb_accepted : 0);
        Shell.setText('ppdb-month', d.ppdb_this_month != null ? d.ppdb_this_month : 0);

        const sppTarget = d.spp_target != null ? d.spp_target : (d.fin_unpaid_amount || 0);
        const sppCollected = d.spp_collected != null ? d.spp_collected : (d.fin_paid_amount || 0);
        const pendingVerify = d.payments_pending_verification != null ? d.payments_pending_verification : (d.fin_pending != null ? d.fin_pending : 0);

        Shell.setText('fin-invoice', formatIDR(sppTarget));
        Shell.setText('fin-paid', formatIDR(sppCollected));
        Shell.setText('fin-outstanding', formatIDR(Math.max(0, sppTarget - sppCollected)));
        Shell.setText('fin-pending', pendingVerify);

        renderActivityFeed();
      } catch (err) {
        Shell.showError('Tidak dapat terhubung ke server.');
      }
    }

    async function renderActivityFeed() {
      const feed = document.getElementById('activity-feed');
      if (!feed) return;
      try {
        const res = await apiCall('logs', 'list', { per_page: 5 });
        const items = (res.data && (res.data.items || res.data)) || [];
        if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) {
          feed.innerHTML = '<div class="text-xs text-gray-500">Belum ada aktivitas terbaru.</div>';
          return;
        }
        feed.innerHTML = items.slice(0, 5).map(it => {
          const action = it.description || it.action || 'Aktivitas';
          const target = it.module || '';
          const who = (it.user_name || 'Sistem');
          const when = (it.created_at || '');
          const whenStr = when ? new Date(when).toLocaleString('id-ID', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) : '';
          return '<div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 py-1.5 border-b border-gray-100 last:border-0">' +
                   '<div class="min-w-0 flex-1">' +
                     '<div class="text-sm text-gray-900 truncate"><span class="font-medium">' + escapeHtml(who) + '</span> <span class="text-gray-600">' + escapeHtml(action) + '</span></div>' +
                   '</div>' +
                   '<div class="text-xs text-gray-500 shrink-0">' + escapeHtml(whenStr) + '</div>' +
                 '</div>';
        }).join('');
      } catch (e) {
        feed.innerHTML = '<div class="text-xs text-gray-500">Aktivitas tidak dapat dimuat.</div>';
      }
    }

    function escapeHtml(s) {
      if (s == null) return '';
      return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&':'&amp;', '<':'&lt;', '>':'&gt;', '"':'&quot;', "'":'&#39;' }[c]));
    }
    loadDashboard();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

            <label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-1">Catatan Tambahan</label>
            <input type="text" id="edit-notes" class="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500">
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-gray-200">
          <button type="button" onclick="closeEditModal()" class="px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50">Batal</button>
          <button id="btn-save-edit" type="submit" class="px-5 py-2 bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg shadow-sm">Simpan Perubahan</button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script src="../../shared/js/auth.js"></script>
  <script src="../../shared/js/api.js"></script>
  <script src="../../shared/js/shell.js"></script>
  <script>
    requireAuth('super_admin,admin_tu');

    const MENU = Shell.ADMIN_MENU;

    Shell.init({
      menu: MENU,
      active: 'ppdb.html',
      portalLabel: 'Portal Admin',
      title: 'Manajemen PPDB',
      subtitle: 'Verifikasi, kelola calon siswa, & atur periode pendaftaran',
      showRefresh: true,
      onRefresh: () => { loadPpdbPeriod(); loadPpdb(); }
    });

    let currentTab = 'all';
    let currentPeriod = null;

    async function loadPpdbPeriod() {
      try {
        const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'period');
        if (res && res.success && res.data) {
          currentPeriod = res.data;
          const isOpen = currentPeriod.status === 'open';
          const icon = document.getElementById('admin-ppdb-status-icon');
          const badge = document.getElementById('admin-ppdb-status-badge');
          const text = document.getElementById('admin-ppdb-period-text');

          if (isOpen) {
            icon.className = 'w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-lg font-bold bg-green-100 text-green-700';
            icon.textContent = '✓';
            badge.className = 'px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-green-100 text-green-800 uppercase';
            badge.textContent = 'BUKA (PENDAFTARAN AKTIF)';
          } else {
            icon.className = 'w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-lg font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-700';
            icon.textContent = '✕';
            badge.className = 'px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 uppercase';
            badge.textContent = 'TUTUP (FORMULIR DISEMBUNYIKAN)';
          }
          text.textContent = `Tahun Ajaran ${currentPeriod.academic_year || '2026/2027'} • Linimasa jadwal aktif di website publik`;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Gagal memuat status periode PPDB:', err);
      }
    }

    function openPeriodModal() {
      if (currentPeriod) {
        document.getElementById('p-status').value = currentPeriod.status || 'open';
        document.getElementById('p-academic-year').value = currentPeriod.academic_year || '2026/2027';
        document.getElementById('p-reg-dates').value = currentPeriod.online_registration_dates || '';
        document.getElementById('p-verif-dates').value = currentPeriod.verification_dates || '';
        document.getElementById('p-ann-dates').value = currentPeriod.announcement_dates || '';
        document.getElementById('p-rereg-dates').value = currentPeriod.re_registration_dates || '';
        document.getElementById('p-notes').value = currentPeriod.notes || '';
      }
      document.getElementById('period-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closePeriodModal() {
      document.getElementById('period-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    document.getElementById('period-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-save-period');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Menyimpan...';

      const payload = {
        status: document.getElementById('p-status').value,
        academic_year: document.getElementById('p-academic-year').value.trim() || '2026/2027',
        online_registration_dates: document.getElementById('p-reg-dates').value.trim(),
        verification_dates: document.getElementById('p-verif-dates').value.trim(),
        announcement_dates: document.getElementById('p-ann-dates').value.trim(),
        re_registration_dates: document.getElementById('p-rereg-dates').value.trim(),
        notes: document.getElementById('p-notes').value.trim()
      };

      try {
        const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'update_period', payload);
        showToast(res.message || 'Pengaturan PPDB berhasil disimpan', res.success ? 'success' : 'error');
        if (res.success) {
          closePeriodModal();
          loadPpdbPeriod();
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Gagal menyimpan pengaturan PPDB', 'error');
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Simpan Pengaturan';
      }
    });

    async function loadPpdb() {
      Shell.hideError();
      const stopSpin = Shell.startRefresh();
      const params = { per_page: 50 };
      if (currentTab !== 'all') params.status = currentTab;
      const search = document.getElementById('filter-search').value;
      const type = document.getElementById('filter-type').value;
      if (search) params.search = search;
      if (type) params.form_type = type;

      const tbody = document.getElementById('ppdb-tbody');
      tbody.innerHTML = Shell.skeletonRows(7, 4);

      try {
        const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'list', params);
        if (!res.success) {
          Shell.showError(res.message || 'Gagal memuat data PPDB');
          tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-4 py-6 text-center text-sm text-red-600">${res.message || 'Gagal'}</td></tr>`;
          return;
        }
        const items = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : ((res.data && (res.data.items || res.data.registrations)) || []);
        if (items.length === 0) {
          tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-4 py-6 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">Tidak ada data pendaftaran</td></tr>';
          return;
        }
        tbody.innerHTML = items.map(r => {
          const childName = r.student_name || r.child_name || r.full_name || '-';
          const parentName = r.parent_name || r.father_name || r.mother_name || '-';
          const formType = (r.level || r.form_type || '-').toUpperCase();
          const regNo = r.registration_number || r.registration_no || r.id || '-';
          const dateCreated = r.created_at || r.registration_date || '';

          return `
          <tr>
            <td class="px-4 py-3 text-sm font-mono tabular-nums text-gray-700 whitespace-nowrap">${regNo}</td>
            <td class="px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-900 min-w-0"><span class="truncate block max-w-xs">${childName}</span></td>
            <td class="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-600 font-semibold">${formType}</td>
            <td class="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-600 truncate">${parentName}</td>
            <td class="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-600 tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap">${formatDate(dateCreated)}</td>
            <td class="px-4 py-3"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 text-xs rounded font-medium ${ppdbStatus(r.status)}">${r.status}</span></td>
            <td class="px-4 py-3 text-sm whitespace-nowrap space-x-1.5">
              <button onclick="viewDetail('${r.id}')" class="text-primary-700 hover:text-primary-900 font-medium">Lihat</button>
              <button onclick="openEditModal('${r.id}')" class="text-amber-700 hover:text-amber-900 font-medium">Edit</button>
              ${r.status === 'pending' ? `<button onclick="approveReg('${r.id}')" class="text-green-700 hover:text-green-900 font-medium">Setujui</button><button onclick="rejectReg('${r.id}')" class="text-red-700 hover:text-red-900 font-medium">Tolak</button>` : ''}
              ${r.status === 'approved' || r.status === 'accepted' ? `<button onclick="convertReg('${r.id}')" class="text-blue-700 hover:text-blue-900 font-bold">Convert ke Siswa</button>` : ''}
              ${r.status === 'converted' ? `<span class="inline-block px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 rounded border border-blue-200">Siswa Aktif</span>` : ''}
              <button onclick="deleteReg('${r.id}')" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-red-700 font-medium text-xs ml-1" title="Hapus berkas">&times; Hapus</button>
            </td>
          </tr>`;
        }).join('');
      } catch (e) {
        Shell.showError('Tidak dapat terhubung ke server.');
      } finally {
        stopSpin();
      }
    }

    function ppdbStatus(status) {
      return { pending: 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700', approved: 'bg-green-100 text-green-700', accepted: 'bg-green-100 text-green-700', rejected: 'bg-red-100 text-red-700', converted: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' }[status] || 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700';
    }

    function switchTab(tab) {
      currentTab = tab;
      document.querySelectorAll('.ppdb-tab').forEach(b => {
        b.classList.remove('border-primary-600','text-primary-700');
        b.classList.add('border-transparent','text-gray-600');
      });
      const active = document.querySelector(`[data-tab="${tab}"]`);
      if (active) {
        active.classList.add('border-primary-600','text-primary-700');
        active.classList.remove('border-transparent','text-gray-600');
      }
      loadPpdb();
    }

    async function viewDetail(id) {
      const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'get', { id });
      if (!res.success) { showToast(res.message, 'error'); return; }
      const r = res.data;
      const childName = r.student_name || r.child_name || r.full_name || '-';
      const parentName = r.parent_name || r.father_name || r.mother_name || '-';
      const formType = (r.level || r.form_type || 'PAUD').toUpperCase();
      const birthDate = r.student_birth_date || r.birth_date || r.child_birth_date || '-';
      const birthPlace = r.student_birth_place || r.birth_place || '-';
      const genderCode = r.student_gender || r.child_gender || r.gender || 'L';
      const gender = genderCode === 'L' ? 'Laki-laki (L)' : 'Perempuan (P)';
      const phone = r.parent_phone || r.phone || r.father_phone || '-';
      const email = r.parent_email || r.email || '-';
      const photo = r.photo_url || r.child_photo_url || (r.documents_url && r.documents_url.photo) || '';
      const birthCert = r.birth_certificate_url || (r.documents_url && r.documents_url.birth_certificate) || '';
      const familyCard = r.family_card_url || (r.documents_url && r.documents_url.family_card) || '';

      document.getElementById('ppdb-detail-body').innerHTML = `
        <dl class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
          <div><dt class="text-gray-500 text-xs">No. Pendaftaran</dt><dd class="font-mono font-bold text-gray-900">${r.registration_number || r.id}</dd></div>
          <div><dt class="text-gray-500 text-xs">Status Penerimaan</dt><dd><span class="px-2 py-0.5 text-xs rounded font-medium ${ppdbStatus(r.status)}">${r.status}</span></dd></div>
          <div><dt class="text-gray-500 text-xs">Nama Calon Siswa</dt><dd class="font-bold text-gray-900">${childName}</dd></div>
          <div><dt class="text-gray-500 text-xs">Jenjang Pendidikan</dt><dd class="font-semibold text-primary-700">${formType}</dd></div>
          <div><dt class="text-gray-500 text-xs">Tempat &amp; Tgl Lahir</dt><dd class="font-mono">${birthPlace}, ${birthDate}</dd></div>
          <div><dt class="text-gray-500 text-xs">Jenis Kelamin</dt><dd>${gender}</dd></div>
          <div><dt class="text-gray-500 text-xs">Nama Orang Tua / Wali</dt><dd class="font-medium">${parentName}</dd></div>
          <div><dt class="text-gray-500 text-xs">No. WhatsApp / HP</dt><dd class="font-mono font-semibold text-gray-900">${phone}</dd></div>
          <div class="col-span-2"><dt class="text-gray-500 text-xs">Email Orang Tua</dt><dd>${email}</dd></div>
          <div class="col-span-2"><dt class="text-gray-500 text-xs">Alamat Lengkap</dt><dd class="text-gray-800">${r.address || '-'}</dd></div>
          ${photo ? `
            <div class="col-span-2 border-t border-gray-100 pt-3">
              <dt class="text-gray-500 text-xs mb-1 font-semibold">Foto Calon Santri:</dt>
              <dd><a href="${photo}" target="_blank" title="Klik untuk perbesar"><img src="${photo}" class="w-28 h-28 object-cover rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm hover:opacity-90 transition"></a></dd>
            </div>` : ''}
          ${(birthCert || familyCard) ? `
            <div class="col-span-2 border-t border-gray-100 pt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              ${birthCert ? `<a href="${birthCert}" target="_blank" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-sky-50 text-sky-700 border border-sky-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-sky-100 transition"><svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z"/></svg>Lihat Akta Kelahiran</a>` : ''}
              ${familyCard ? `<a href="${familyCard}" target="_blank" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-100 transition"><svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z"/></svg>Lihat Kartu Keluarga</a>` : ''}
            </div>` : ''}
          ${r.notes ? `<div class="col-span-2 border-t border-gray-100 pt-3"><dt class="text-gray-500 text-xs font-semibold mb-0.5">Catatan Pendaftar:</dt><dd class="text-xs text-gray-700 bg-gray-50 p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 whitespace-pre-wrap">${r.notes}</dd></div>` : ''}
        </dl>
      `;
      document.getElementById('ppdb-detail-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    async function openEditModal(id) {
      try {
        const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'get', { id });
        if (!res.success || !res.data) { showToast(res.message || 'Gagal memuat data pendaftar', 'error'); return; }
        const r = res.data;

        document.getElementById('edit-reg-id').value = r.id || id;
        document.getElementById('edit-student-name').value = r.student_name || r.full_name || r.child_name || '';
        document.getElementById('edit-nickname').value = r.nickname || '';
        document.getElementById('edit-gender').value = (r.student_gender || r.gender || 'L').toUpperCase();
        document.getElementById('edit-birth-place').value = r.student_birth_place || r.birth_place || '';
        document.getElementById('edit-birth-date').value = r.student_birth_date || r.birth_date || '';
        document.getElementById('edit-level').value = r.level || (r.form_type === 'tpq' ? 'TPQ' : 'PAUD');
        document.getElementById('edit-parent-name').value = r.parent_name || r.father_name || r.mother_name || '';
        document.getElementById('edit-parent-phone').value = r.parent_phone || r.phone || '';
        document.getElementById('edit-parent-email').value = r.parent_email || r.email || '';
        document.getElementById('edit-address').value = r.address || '';
        document.getElementById('edit-notes').value = r.notes || '';

        document.getElementById('ppdb-edit-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Gagal membuka form edit', 'error');
      }
    }

    function closeEditModal() {
      document.getElementById('ppdb-edit-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    document.getElementById('ppdb-edit-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-save-edit');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Menyimpan...';

      const id = document.getElementById('edit-reg-id').value;
      const payload = {
        id,
        student_name: document.getElementById('edit-student-name').value.trim(),
        full_name: document.getElementById('edit-student-name').value.trim(),
        child_name: document.getElementById('edit-student-name').value.trim(),
        nickname: document.getElementById('edit-nickname').value.trim(),
        gender: document.getElementById('edit-gender').value,
        birth_place: document.getElementById('edit-birth-place').value.trim(),
        birth_date: document.getElementById('edit-birth-date').value,
        level: document.getElementById('edit-level').value,
        parent_name: document.getElementById('edit-parent-name').value.trim(),
        parent_phone: document.getElementById('edit-parent-phone').value.trim(),
        phone: document.getElementById('edit-parent-phone').value.trim(),
        parent_email: document.getElementById('edit-parent-email').value.trim(),
        email: document.getElementById('edit-parent-email').value.trim(),
        address: document.getElementById('edit-address').value.trim(),
        notes: document.getElementById('edit-notes').value.trim()
      };

      try {
        const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'update', payload);
        if (res.success) {
          showToast(res.message || 'Data pendaftar berhasil diperbarui', 'success');
          closeEditModal();
          loadPpdb();
        } else {
          showToast(res.message || 'Gagal menyimpan perubahan', 'error');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Terjadi kesalahan saat menyimpan data', 'error');
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Simpan Perubahan';
      }
    });

    async function deleteReg(id) {
      if (!confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus berkas pendaftaran ini dari sistem?')) return;
      try {
        const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'delete', { id });
        if (res.success) {
          showToast(res.message || 'Berkas pendaftaran berhasil dihapus', 'success');
          loadPpdb();
        } else {
          showToast(res.message || 'Gagal menghapus berkas', 'error');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Gagal menghapus berkas pendaftaran', 'error');
      }
    }

    async function approveReg(id) {
      const notes = prompt('Catatan verifikasi (opsional):');
      const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'approve', { id, notes: notes || '' });
      showToast(res.message, res.success ? 'success' : 'error');
      if (res.success) loadPpdb();
    }

    async function rejectReg(id) {
      const reason = prompt('Alasan penolakan:');
      if (!reason) return;
      const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'reject', { id, reason });
      showToast(res.message, res.success ? 'success' : 'error');
      if (res.success) loadPpdb();
    }

    async function convertReg(id) {
      // Ambil daftar kelas untuk dropdown tujuan
      let classes = [];
      try {
        const cr = await apiCall('classes', 'list', { per_page: 100 });
        const raw = cr.data && (cr.data.classes || cr.data.items || (Array.isArray(cr.data) ? cr.data : []));
        classes = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : [];
      } catch (_) { classes = []; }

      if (classes.length === 0) {
        showToast('Tidak ada kelas tersedia. Buat kelas terlebih dahulu di menu Kelas.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      const defaultNis = '2026' + String(Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000));
      const optionsHtml = classes.map(c => `<option value="${c.id}">${c.name} (${c.level || ''})</option>`).join('');

      const modal = document.createElement('div');
      modal.className = 'fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-gray-900 bg-opacity-50 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4';
      modal.innerHTML = `
        <div class="bg-white rounded-xl w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl overflow-hidden animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-200">
          <div class="px-5 py-4 border-b border-gray-200 flex justify-between items-center bg-gray-50">
            <h3 class="text-base font-bold text-gray-900">Aktivasi Siswa Baru (PPDB)</h3>
            <button id="cm-close" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-xl font-bold">&times;</button>
          </div>
          <div class="p-5 space-y-4">
            <p class="text-xs text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">
              Pendaftar akan diaktifkan menjadi <strong>Siswa Aktif</strong>. Akun Portal Siswa otomatis aktif dengan username berupa <strong>NIS</strong> dan kata sandi awal berupa <strong>Tanggal Lahir</strong>.
            </p>
            <div>
              <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Nomor Induk Siswa (NIS) *</label>
              <input type="text" id="cm-nis" value="${defaultNis}" class="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm font-mono font-bold text-gray-900 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500">
              <span class="text-[11px] text-gray-400">NIS ini digunakan siswa untuk login ke Portal Siswa.</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Kelas Tujuan *</label>
              <select id="cm-class" class="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500">${optionsHtml}</select>
            </div>
            <div class="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-gray-200">
              <button id="cm-cancel" class="px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50">Batal</button>
              <button id="cm-ok" class="px-5 py-2 bg-primary-600 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-primary-700 shadow-md shadow-primary-600/20">Aktivasi Siswa</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(modal);

      const close = () => modal.remove();
      modal.querySelector('#cm-close').onclick = close;
      modal.querySelector('#cm-cancel').onclick = close;
      modal.querySelector('#cm-ok').onclick = async () => {
        const classId = modal.querySelector('#cm-class').value;
        const nis = modal.querySelector('#cm-nis').value.trim();
        if (!classId) { showToast('Pilih kelas terlebih dahulu', 'warning'); return; }
        if (!nis) { showToast('Masukkan NIS siswa', 'warning'); return; }

        const okBtn = modal.querySelector('#cm-ok');
        okBtn.disabled = true; okBtn.textContent = 'Mengaktifkan...';

        try {
          const res = await apiCall('ppdb', 'convert', { id, class_id: classId, nis });
          if (res.success && res.data) {
            close();
            showConvertSuccessModal(res.data);
            loadPpdb();
          } else {
            showToast(res.message || 'Gagal mengaktifkan siswa', 'error');
            okBtn.disabled = false; okBtn.textContent = 'Aktivasi Siswa';
          }
        } catch (e) {
          showToast('Gagal mengaktifkan siswa', 'error');
          okBtn.disabled = false; okBtn.textContent = 'Aktivasi Siswa';
        }
      };
    }

    function showConvertSuccessModal(data) {
      const s = data.student || {};
      const nis = data.nis || s.nis || '-';
      const className = data.class_name || s.class_name || '-';
      const studentName = s.full_name || s.name || '-';
      const creds = data.initial_credentials || {};
      const copyText = `AKSES PORTAL SISWA YAYASAN AT TARBIYYAH WIJAYA KUSUMA SAMARINDA\nNama Siswa: ${studentName}\nNIS (Username): ${nis}\nPassword Awal: ${defaultPassword}\nLink Portal: ${loginUrl}\n\n*Catatan: Segera buat kata sandi baru saat pertama kali login.`;

      const succModal = document.createElement('div');
      succModal.className = 'fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-gray-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4';
      succModal.innerHTML = `
        <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl overflow-hidden animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-200">
          <div class="p-6 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-700 text-white">
            <div class="w-10 h-10 bg-white/20 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
              <svg class="w-6 h-6 text-white" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"/></svg>
            </div>
            <h3 class="text-lg font-bold">Siswa Berhasil Diaktifkan!</h3>
            <p class="text-xs text-emerald-100 mt-0.5">Siswa resmi terdaftar di database yayasan dan dapat mengakses Portal Siswa.</p>
          </div>

          <div class="p-6 space-y-4 text-xs">
            <div class="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 space-y-2.5 font-mono">
              <div class="flex justify-between items-center"><span class="text-slate-500 font-sans">Nama Siswa:</span><span class="font-bold text-slate-900">${studentName}</span></div>
              <div class="flex justify-between items-center"><span class="text-slate-500 font-sans">NIS (Login ID):</span><span class="font-bold text-primary-700 bg-primary-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-primary-200">${nis}</span></div>
              <div class="flex justify-between items-center"><span class="text-slate-500 font-sans">Kelas Masuk:</span><span class="font-bold text-slate-800 font-sans">${className}</span></div>
              <div class="flex justify-between items-center border-t border-slate-200 pt-2"><span class="text-slate-500 font-sans">Password Awal:</span><span class="font-bold text-emerald-700 font-sans">${defaultPassword}</span></div>
            </div>

            <p class="text-slate-600 text-xs leading-relaxed">
              Saat pertama kali login di Portal Siswa, siswa/wali murid diwajibkan untuk membuat kata sandi baru demi keamanan akun.
            </p>

            <div class="space-y-2 pt-2">
              <button id="btn-copy-creds" type="button" class="w-full py-2.5 px-4 bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-xl text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm transition">
                <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M8 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2h10a2 2 0 002-2v-1M8 5a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2M8 5a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2m0 0h2a2 2 0 012 2v3m2 4H10m0 0l3-3m-3 3l3 3"/></svg>
                <span id="copy-btn-text">Salin Rincian Akun (1-Klik)</span>
              </button>
              <button id="btn-close-succ" type="button" class="w-full py-2 px-4 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-medium rounded-xl text-xs transition">
                Tutup Jendela
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(succModal);

      succModal.querySelector('#btn-close-succ').onclick = () => succModal.remove();
      succModal.querySelector('#btn-copy-creds').onclick = () => {
        if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
          navigator.clipboard.writeText(copyText).then(() => {
            const txt = succModal.querySelector('#copy-btn-text');
            if (txt) txt.textContent = '✓ Berhasil Disalin ke Clipboard!';
            setTimeout(() => { if (txt) txt.textContent = 'Salin Rincian Akun (1-Klik)'; }, 2500);
          }).catch(() => {
            showToast('Gagal menyalin info akun', 'error');
          });
        } else {
          showToast('Fitur clipboard tidak didukung di browser ini', 'warning');
        }
      };
    }

    loadPpdbPeriod();
    loadPpdb();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  <div id="proof-modal" class="hidden fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-gray-950/80 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4" onclick="closeProofModal()">
    <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 max-w-lg w-full shadow-2xl border border-gray-800/10" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center pb-3 mb-3 border-b border-gray-100">
        <div>
          <span class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400">Bukti Pembayaran Masuk</span>
          <h4 id="proof-student-title" class="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">-</h4>
        </div>
        <button onclick="closeProofModal()" class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 text-xl font-bold">&times;</button>
      </div>
      <div class="bg-gray-100 rounded-xl overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center min-h-[240px] max-h-[60vh] border border-gray-200">
        <img id="proof-img" src="" alt="Bukti Transfer" class="max-h-[58vh] w-auto max-w-full object-contain">
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3 flex justify-between items-center text-xs text-gray-500">
        <span id="proof-inv-info">-</span>
        <a id="proof-dl-link" href="#" target="_blank" class="text-primary-600 font-semibold hover:underline">Buka Gambar Asli &nearr;</a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script src="../../shared/js/auth.js"></script>
  <script src="../../shared/js/api.js"></script>
  <script src="../../shared/js/shell.js"></script>
  <script>
    requireAuth('super_admin,admin_tu');

    const MENU = Shell.ADMIN_MENU;

    Shell.init({
      menu: MENU,
      active: 'payments.html',
      portalLabel: 'Portal Admin',
      title: 'Pembayaran &amp; Rekap SPP',
      subtitle: 'Kelola tagihan SPP bulanan, invoice massal & validasi bukti transfer siswa',
      showRefresh: true,
      onRefresh: refreshAll
    });

    let currentTab = 'invoices';
    let invoicesCache = [];
    let paymentsCache = [];
    let classesCache = [];
    let studentsCache = [];

    async function refreshAll() {
      Shell.hideError();
      const stopSpin = Shell.startRefresh();
      try {
        await Promise.all([loadKpis(), loadInvoices(), loadVerifications(), loadClasses(), loadStudentsList()]);
      } catch (e) {
        Shell.showError('Tidak dapat terhubung ke server database.');
      } finally {
        stopSpin();
      }
    }

    async function loadKpis() {
      const res = await apiCall('payments', 'summary');
      if (res.success && res.data) {
        document.getElementById('kpi-total').textContent = formatIDR(res.data.total_invoice || 0);
        document.getElementById('kpi-paid').textContent = formatIDR(res.data.total_paid || 0);
        document.getElementById('kpi-outstanding').textContent = formatIDR(res.data.total_outstanding || 0);
        
        const pendingCount = res.data.pending_verification || 0;
        document.getElementById('kpi-pending').textContent = pendingCount;
        
        const badge = document.getElementById('badge-verif-count');
        if (pendingCount > 0) {
          badge.textContent = pendingCount;
          badge.classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
          badge.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      }
    }

    async function loadClasses() {
      const res = await apiCall('classes', 'list', { per_page: 100 });
      const items = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : ((res.data && (res.data.items || res.data.classes)) || []);
      if (res.success && items.length > 0) {
        classesCache = items;
        const selBulk = document.getElementById('bulk-class');
        if (selBulk) selBulk.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Siswa Aktif Seluruh Kelas</option>' + classesCache.map(c => `<option value="${c.id}">${c.name}</option>`).join('');

        const selFilter = document.getElementById('filter-inv-class');
        if (selFilter) selFilter.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Rombongan Belajar (Kelas)</option>' + classesCache.map(c => `<option value="${c.id}">${c.name}</option>`).join('');
      }
    }

    async function loadStudentsList() {
      const res = await apiCall('students', 'list', { per_page: 100, status: 'active' });
      const items = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : ((res.data && (res.data.items || res.data.students)) || []);
      if (res.success && items.length > 0) {
        studentsCache = items;
        const sel = document.getElementById('single-student');
        sel.innerHTML = studentsCache.map(s => `<option value="${s.id}">${s.full_name} (${s.nis || 'NIS -'}) - ${s.kelas_sekarang || s.class_name || '-'}</option>`).join('');
      }
    }

    async function loadInvoices() {
      const tbody = document.getElementById('invoices-tbody');
      tbody.innerHTML = Shell.skeletonRows(6, 4);

      const res = await apiCall('invoices', 'list', { per_page: 200 });

      if (!res.success) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium">${res.message || 'Gagal'}</td></tr>`;
        return;
      }

      invoicesCache = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : ((res.data && (res.data.items || res.data.invoices)) || []);

      // Populate opsi periode / bulan secara dinamis
      const selMonth = document.getElementById('filter-inv-month');
      if (selMonth && selMonth.options.length <= 1) {
        const uniqueMonths = [...new Set(invoicesCache.map(i => i.period || i.month || i.description || '').filter(Boolean))];
        uniqueMonths.forEach(m => {
          const opt = document.createElement('option');
          opt.value = m;
          opt.textContent = m;
          selMonth.appendChild(opt);
        });
      }

      applyInvoicesFilter();
    }

    function applyInvoicesFilter() {
      const search = (document.getElementById('filter-inv-search')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const statusFilter = (document.getElementById('filter-inv-status')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const classFilter = document.getElementById('filter-inv-class')?.value || '';
      const monthFilter = (document.getElementById('filter-inv-month')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();

      let list = [...invoicesCache];

      // Filter URL student_id jika datang dari link profil siswa
      const urlStudentId = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('student_id');
      if (urlStudentId && !search) {
        list = list.filter(i => (i.student_id === urlStudentId));
      }

      // 1. Realtime Search (Nama, NIS, No Tagihan, Keterangan)
      if (search) {
        list = list.filter(i => 
          (i.invoice_number && i.invoice_number.toLowerCase().includes(search)) ||
          (i.student_name && i.student_name.toLowerCase().includes(search)) ||
          (i.description && i.description.toLowerCase().includes(search)) ||
          (i.item_name && i.item_name.toLowerCase().includes(search))
        );
      }

      // 2. Filter Status Tagihan
      if (statusFilter) {
        list = list.filter(i => (i.status || '').toLowerCase() === statusFilter);
      }

      // 3. Filter Kelas
      if (classFilter) {
        const studentIdsInClass = studentsCache.filter(s => s.class_id === classFilter).map(s => s.id);
        list = list.filter(i => studentIdsInClass.includes(i.student_id));
      }

      // 4. Filter Periode / Bulan
      if (monthFilter) {
        list = list.filter(i => 
          (i.period && i.period.toLowerCase().includes(monthFilter)) ||
          (i.month && i.month.toLowerCase().includes(monthFilter)) ||
          (i.description && i.description.toLowerCase().includes(monthFilter)) ||
          (i.item_name && i.item_name.toLowerCase().includes(monthFilter))
        );
      }

      // Update counter info
      const countEl = document.getElementById('filter-count-info');
      if (countEl) {
        countEl.innerHTML = `Menampilkan <strong class="text-gray-900 font-bold">${list.length}</strong> dari total ${invoicesCache.length} tagihan`;
      }

      renderInvoicesTable(list);
    }

    function resetInvoicesFilter() {
      document.getElementById('filter-inv-search').value = '';
      document.getElementById('filter-inv-status').value = '';
      document.getElementById('filter-inv-class').value = '';
      document.getElementById('filter-inv-month').value = '';
      applyInvoicesFilter();
      showToast('Seluruh filter tagihan telah di-reset.', 'info');
    }

    function renderInvoicesTable(list) {
      const tbody = document.getElementById('invoices-tbody');
      if (list.length === 0) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" class="px-4 py-12 text-center text-xs text-gray-500">Tidak ada data tagihan yang sesuai dengan kriteria filter.</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      tbody.innerHTML = list.map(i => {
        let badge = 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200';
        let label = 'Belum Lunas';
        if (i.status === 'paid') {
          badge = 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200';
          label = 'Lunas';
        } else if (i.status === 'menunggu_verifikasi') {
          badge = 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-800 border border-blue-200';
          label = 'Menunggu Verifikasi';
        }

        const isUnpaid = i.status === 'unpaid' || i.status === 'menunggu_verifikasi';

        return `
        <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50/80 transition">
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap text-xs font-mono font-bold text-gray-800">${i.invoice_number || '-'}</td>
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap">
            <div class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-900">${i.student_name || '-'}</div>
          </td>
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap text-xs text-gray-600">${i.description || i.month || '-'}</td>
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap text-xs font-mono font-bold text-gray-900">${formatIDR(i.amount)}</td>
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap">
            <span class="px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] rounded-lg font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${badge}">
              ${label}
            </span>
          </td>
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap text-center space-x-1.5">
            ${isUnpaid ? `
              <button onclick="markPaidOffline('${i.id}')" title="Tandai lunas offline (pembayaran tunai di kantor TU)" class="px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-100 transition">
                ✓ Lunas Tunai
              </button>
              <button onclick="sendWaInvoice('${i.id}')" title="Kirim pengingat tagihan via WhatsApp ke orang tua" class="px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-100 transition inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                <svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12.031 6.172c-3.181 0-5.767 2.586-5.768 5.766-.001 1.298.38 2.27 1.019 3.287l-.582 2.128 2.182-.573c.978.58 1.911.928 3.145.929 3.178 0 5.767-2.587 5.768-5.766.001-3.187-2.575-5.77-5.764-5.771zm3.392 8.244c-.144.405-.837.774-1.17.824-.299.045-.677.063-1.092-.069-.252-.08-.575-.187-.988-.365-1.739-.751-2.874-2.502-2.961-2.617-.087-.116-.708-.94-.708-1.793s.448-1.273.607-1.446c.159-.173.346-.217.462-.217l.332.006c.106.005.249-.04.39.298.144.347.491 1.2.534 1.287.043.087.072.188.014.304-.058.116-.087.188-.173.289l-.26.304c-.087.086-.177.18-.076.354.101.174.449.741.964 1.201.662.591 1.221.774 1.394.86s.275.072.376-.043c.101-.116.433-.506.549-.68.116-.173.231-.145.39-.087s1.011.477 1.184.564.289.13.332.202c.045.072.045.419-.099.824z"/></svg>
                <span>Kirim WA</span>
              </button>
            ` : `
              <button onclick="printAdminReceipt('${i.id}')" title="Cetak Kuitansi Resmi" class="px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-100 transition inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                <svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M17 17h2a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2h2m2 4h6a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2zm8-12V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4h10z"/></svg>
                <span>Kuitansi</span>
              </button>
            `}
            <button onclick="deleteInvoice('${i.id}')" title="Hapus tagihan" class="p-1 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-600 transition">
              <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"/></svg>
            </button>
          </td>
        </tr>`;
      }).join('');
    }

    async function loadVerifications() {
      const tbody = document.getElementById('payments-tbody');
      tbody.innerHTML = Shell.skeletonRows(7, 3);

      const res = await apiCall('payments', 'list', { per_page: 50, status: 'pending' });

      if (!res.success) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium">${res.message || 'Gagal'}</td></tr>`;
        return;
      }

      paymentsCache = res.data || [];
      applyVerificationsFilter();
    }

    function applyVerificationsFilter() {
      const q = (document.getElementById('filter-verif-search')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const tbody = document.getElementById('payments-tbody');

      let list = [...paymentsCache];
      if (q) {
        list = list.filter(p => 
          (p.student_name && p.student_name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
          (p.invoice_number && p.invoice_number.toLowerCase().includes(q))
        );
      }

      if (list.length === 0) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-4 py-12 text-center text-xs text-gray-500">Tidak ada bukti transfer yang cocok atau menunggu verifikasi.</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      tbody.innerHTML = list.map(p => {
        const hasProof = !!p.proof_image_url;
        return `
        <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50/80 transition">
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap text-xs text-gray-600">${formatDate(p.payment_date || p.submitted_at)}</td>
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap text-xs font-bold text-gray-900">${p.student_name || '-'}</td>
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap text-xs font-mono text-gray-600">${p.invoice_number || '-'}</td>
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap text-xs font-mono font-bold text-gray-900">${formatIDR(p.amount)}</td>
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap text-xs text-gray-600 capitalize">${p.method || 'Transfer'}</td>
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap text-center">
            ${hasProof ? `
              <button onclick="openProofModal('${p.id}')" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-primary-50 text-primary-700 border border-primary-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold hover:bg-primary-100 transition">
                <span>📷 Lihat Bukti Foto</span>
              </button>
            ` : '<span class="text-xs text-gray-400">Tidak ada foto</span>'}
          </td>
          <td class="px-4 py-3.5 whitespace-nowrap text-center space-x-2">
            <button onclick="verifyPayment('${p.id}', true)" class="px-3 py-1 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg shadow-sm transition">
              ✓ Setujui (Lunas)
            </button>
            <button onclick="verifyPayment('${p.id}', false)" class="px-3 py-1 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-rose-50 text-gray-600 hover:text-rose-700 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg border border-gray-200 transition">
              Tolak
            </button>
          </td>
        </tr>`;
      }).join('');
    }

    function switchTab(tab) {
      currentTab = tab;
      document.querySelectorAll('.pay-tab').forEach(b => {
        b.classList.remove('border-primary-600', 'text-primary-700');
        b.classList.add('border-transparent', 'text-gray-500');
      });
      document.querySelector(`[data-tab="${tab}"]`).classList.add('border-primary-600', 'text-primary-700');
      document.querySelector(`[data-tab="${tab}"]`).classList.remove('border-transparent', 'text-gray-500');

      document.getElementById('panel-invoices').classList.toggle('hidden', tab !== 'invoices');
      document.getElementById('panel-verifications').classList.toggle('hidden', tab !== 'verifications');
      if (tab === 'verifications') loadVerifications();
    }

    // Modal Single Invoice
    function openSingleInvoiceModal() {
      document.getElementById('single-inv-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
      document.getElementById('single-due').value = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    }
    function closeSingleInvModal() {
      document.getElementById('single-inv-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    document.getElementById('single-inv-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const studentId = document.getElementById('single-student').value;
      const s = studentsCache.find(x => x.id === studentId);
      const studentName = s ? s.full_name : '';

      const data = {
        student_id: studentId,
        student_name: studentName,
        item_name: document.getElementById('single-item').value.trim(),
        description: document.getElementById('single-item').value.trim(),
        amount: parseFloat(document.getElementById('single-amount').value),
        due_date: document.getElementById('single-due').value
      };

      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-save-single-inv');
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.textContent = 'Menerbitkan...';

      try {
        const res = await apiCall('invoices', 'create', data);
        showToast(res.message || 'Tagihan berhasil diterbitkan', res.success ? 'success' : 'error');
        if (res.success) {
          closeSingleInvModal();
          loadInvoices();
          loadKpis();
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Gagal membuat tagihan', 'error');
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = 'Terbitkan Tagihan';
      }
    });

    // Modal Bulk Invoice
    function openBulkInvoiceModal() {
      document.getElementById('bulk-inv-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
      document.getElementById('bulk-due').value = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    }
    function closeBulkInvModal() {
      document.getElementById('bulk-inv-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    document.getElementById('bulk-inv-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const classId = document.getElementById('bulk-class').value;
      const month = document.getElementById('bulk-month').value.trim();
      const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('bulk-amount').value);
      const dueDate = document.getElementById('bulk-due').value;

      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-save-bulk-inv');
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.textContent = 'Menerbitkan Massal...';

      try {
        const res = await apiCall('invoices', 'create_bulk', {
          class_id: classId,
          month: month,
          item_name: `SPP ${month}`,
          amount: amount,
          due_date: dueDate
        });
        showToast(res.message || 'Tagihan massal berhasil diterbitkan', res.success ? 'success' : 'error');
        if (res.success) {
          closeBulkInvModal();
          loadInvoices();
          loadKpis();
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Gagal menerbitkan tagihan massal', 'error');
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = 'Terbitkan Massal Sekarang';
      }
    });

    // Verifikasi Pembayaran
    async function verifyPayment(id, isApproved) {
      if (!isApproved && !confirm('Tolak pembayaran ini? Tagihan siswa akan dikembalikan ke status Belum Lunas.')) return;
      const action = isApproved ? 'verify' : 'reject';
      const res = await apiCall('payments', action, { id });
      showToast(res.message || 'Pembayaran diperbarui', res.success ? 'success' : 'error');
      if (res.success) {
        loadVerifications();
        loadInvoices();
        loadKpis();
      }
    }

    async function markPaidOffline(invoiceId) {
      if (!confirm('Tandai tagihan ini sebagai LUNAS (Pembayaran Tunai di Kantor TU)?')) return;
      const res = await apiCall('invoices', 'mark_paid', { id: invoiceId });
      showToast(res.message || 'Tagihan lunas', res.success ? 'success' : 'error');
      if (res.success) {
        loadInvoices();
        loadKpis();
      }
    }

    async function deleteInvoice(id) {
      if (!confirm('Hapus tagihan ini?')) return;
      const res = await apiCall('invoices', 'delete', { id });
      showToast(res.message || 'Tagihan dihapus', res.success ? 'success' : 'error');
      if (res.success) {
        loadInvoices();
        loadKpis();
      }
    }

    function openProofModal(payId) {
      const p = paymentsCache.find(x => x.id === payId);
      if (!p || !p.proof_image_url) {
        showToast('Bukti transfer tidak tersedia', 'error');
        return;
      }
      document.getElementById('proof-img').src = p.proof_image_url;
      document.getElementById('proof-dl-link').href = p.proof_image_url;
      document.getElementById('proof-student-title').textContent = `${p.student_name} (${p.invoice_number || 'Tagihan'})`;
      document.getElementById('proof-inv-info').textContent = `Jumlah: ${formatIDR(p.amount)} • Tgl: ${formatDate(p.payment_date || p.submitted_at)}`;
      document.getElementById('proof-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeProofModal() {
      document.getElementById('proof-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    function sendWaInvoice(invId) {
      const inv = invoicesCache.find(x => x.id === invId);
      if (!inv) return;
      const s = studentsCache.find(x => x.id === inv.student_id) || {};
      const parentPhone = inv.parent_phone || inv.parent_whatsapp || s.father_phone || s.mother_phone || s.parent_phone || s.phone || s.emergency_phone || '081234567890';
      const cleanPhone = String(parentPhone).replace(/\D/g, '').replace(/^0/, '62');
      const studentName = inv.student_name || s.full_name || '-';
      const nis = s.nis || '-';
      const month = inv.description || inv.month || inv.period || 'SPP';
      const amount = formatIDR(inv.amount || 150000);
      const dueDate = inv.due_date ? formatDate(inv.due_date) : 'Tgl 10';

      const msg = `Assalamu'alaikum Warahmatullahi Wabarakatuh,\n\nYth. Bapak/Ibu Orang Tua / Wali dari ananda:\n• Nama Siswa: ${studentName}\n• NIS: ${nis}\n\nKami dari Tata Usaha Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma Samarinda menginformasikan kewajiban iuran bulanan sbb:\n• Tagihan: ${month}\n• Nominal: ${amount}\n• Jatuh Tempo: ${dueDate}\n\nPembayaran dapat dilakukan melalui transfer rekening resmi yayasan:\n- Bank Syariah Indonesia (BSI): 7123456789\n- Bank Kaltimtara: 0012345678\na.n. Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma\n\nAtau pembayaran tunai langsung di kantor Tata Usaha sekolah. Setelah transfer, bukti dapat diunggah melalui Portal Siswa atau dikirimkan via WhatsApp ini.\n\nTerima kasih atas perhatian dan kerjasamanya.`;

      window.open(`https://wa.me/${cleanPhone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(msg)}`, '_blank');
    }

    function printAdminReceipt(invId) {
      const inv = invoicesCache.find(x => x.id === invId);
      if (!inv) return;
      const s = studentsCache.find(x => x.id === inv.student_id) || {};
      const studentName = inv.student_name || s.full_name || '-';
      const nis = s.nis || '-';
      const className = s.class_name || s.kelas_sekarang || 'PAUD / TK Terpadu';
      const invNum = inv.invoice_number || ('INV/' + new Date().getFullYear() + '/SPP');
      const month = inv.description || inv.month || inv.period || 'SPP Bulanan';
      const amount = inv.amount || 150000;
      const paidAt = inv.paid_at ? formatDate(inv.paid_at) : new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
      const paidBy = inv.paid_by || 'Tunai / Kasir TU Yayasan';

      const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=750,height=800');
      if (!printWindow) {
        alert('Mohon izinkan pop-up peramban untuk mencetak kuitansi.');
        return;
      }

      printWindow.document.write(`
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html lang="id">
        <head>
          <meta charset="UTF-8">
          <title>Kuitansi Pembayaran SPP - ${invNum}</title>
          <style>
            body { font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif; padding: 36px; color: #0f172a; line-height: 1.5; }
            .kop { text-align: center; border-bottom: 3px double #0284c7; padding-bottom: 12px; margin-bottom: 24px; }
            .kop h1 { margin: 0; color: #0369a1; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.5px; }
            .kop p { margin: 3px 0 0; font-size: 12px; color: #64748b; }
            .kuitansi-title { text-align: center; margin-bottom: 20px; }
            .kuitansi-title h2 { margin: 0; font-size: 17px; font-weight: 800; text-transform: uppercase; color: #1e293b; letter-spacing: 1px; }
            .kuitansi-num { font-size: 12px; color: #64748b; font-family: monospace; font-weight: 600; margin-top: 4px; }
            .badge-lunas { display: inline-block; background: #ecfdf5; color: #065f46; border: 1.5px solid #a7f3d0; padding: 4px 14px; font-weight: 800; font-size: 13px; border-radius: 9999px; margin-top: 6px; }
            table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 24px; font-size: 13px; }
            td { padding: 9px 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; }
            td.lbl { width: 32%; font-weight: 600; color: #64748b; }
            td.val { font-weight: 700; color: #0f172a; }
            .nominal-box { background: #f0f9ff; border: 2px dashed #0284c7; border-radius: 12px; padding: 14px 20px; margin-bottom: 24px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; }
            .nominal-label { font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; color: #0369a1; text-transform: uppercase; }
            .nominal-val { font-size: 22px; font-weight: 800; color: #0284c7; font-family: monospace; }
            .terbilang { font-size: 12px; color: #475569; font-style: italic; }
            .ttd-wrap { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin-top: 36px; font-size: 12px; }
            .ttd { text-align: center; width: 220px; }
            @media print { .no-print { display: none !important; } }
          </style>
        </head>
        <body>
          <div class="kop">
            <h1>YAYASAN AT TARBIYYAH WIJAYA KUSUMA SAMARINDA</h1>
            <p>Pendidikan Anak Usia Dini (PAUD), TK Terpadu &amp; TPQ ULINNUHA</p>
            <p>Jl. Wijaya Kusuma V-A No. 38, Kel. Air Putih, Kec. Samarinda Ulu, Kota Samarinda | Telp/WA: 0813-4714-2829</p>
          </div>
          <div class="kuitansi-title">
            <h2>Kuitansi Resmi Pembayaran SPP</h2>
            <div class="kuitansi-num">No. Invoice: ${invNum}</div>
            <div class="badge-lunas">&#10003; LUNAS (PEMBAYARAN SAH)</div>
          </div>
          <table>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Telah Diterima Dari</td><td class="val">${studentName}</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Nomor Induk Siswa (NIS)</td><td class="val" style="font-family:monospace;">${nis}</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Kelas / Rombel</td><td class="val">${className}</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Untuk Pembayaran</td><td class="val">${month}</td></tr>
