    function printSppSummaryReport() {
      const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
      const totalInv = invoicesCache.reduce((s, i) => s + Number(i.amount || 0), 0);
      const paidInvs = invoicesCache.filter(i => i.status === 'paid');
      const unpaidInvs = invoicesCache.filter(i => i.status !== 'paid');
      const totalPaid = paidInvs.reduce((s, i) => s + Number(i.amount || 0), 0);
      const totalUnpaid = unpaidInvs.reduce((s, i) => s + Number(i.amount || 0), 0);
      const percent = totalInv > 0 ? Math.round((totalPaid / totalInv) * 100) : 0;

      const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=850,height=900');
      if (!printWindow) {
        alert('Mohon izinkan pop-up peramban untuk mencetak rekap.');
        return;
      }

      const rowsHtml = invoicesCache.map((inv, idx) => `
        <tr>
          <td style="padding: 6px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; font-family: monospace;">${idx + 1}</td>
          <td style="padding: 6px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; font-family: monospace;">${inv.invoice_number || '-'}</td>
          <td style="padding: 6px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; font-weight: 600;">${inv.student_name || '-'}</td>
          <td style="padding: 6px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0;">${inv.description || inv.month || '-'}</td>
          <td style="padding: 6px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; text-align: right; font-family: monospace; font-weight: 600;">${formatIDR(inv.amount)}</td>
          <td style="padding: 6px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; color: ${inv.status === 'paid' ? '#059669' : '#dc2626'};">${inv.status === 'paid' ? 'LUNAS' : 'BELUM'}</td>
        </tr>
      `).join('');

      printWindow.document.write(`
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html lang="id">
        <head>
          <meta charset="UTF-8">
          <title>Laporan Rekapitulasi SPP - Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma Samarinda</title>
          <style>
            body { font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif; padding: 32px; color: #0f172a; line-height: 1.4; font-size: 12px; }
            .kop { text-align: center; border-bottom: 3px double #0284c7; padding-bottom: 10px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
            .kop h1 { margin: 0; color: #0369a1; font-size: 18px; font-weight: 800; }
            .kop p { margin: 2px 0 0; font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; }
            .title { text-align: center; margin-bottom: 16px; }
            .title h2 { margin: 0; font-size: 15px; font-weight: 800; text-transform: uppercase; color: #1e293b; }
            .kpi-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr); gap: 10px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
            .kpi-card { background: #f8fafc; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; padding: 10px; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; }
            .kpi-lbl { font-size: 10px; font-weight: 700; color: #64748b; text-transform: uppercase; }
            .kpi-val { font-size: 15px; font-weight: 800; color: #0f172a; margin-top: 3px; font-family: monospace; }
            table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 24px; font-size: 11px; }
            th { background: #f1f5f9; padding: 8px; text-align: left; font-weight: 700; color: #475569; border-bottom: 2px solid #cbd5e1; }
            .ttd-wrap { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin-top: 32px; font-size: 11px; }
            .ttd { text-align: center; width: 220px; }
            @media print { .no-print { display: none !important; } }
          </style>
        </head>
        <body>
          <div class="kop">
            <h1>YAYASAN AT TARBIYYAH WIJAYA KUSUMA SAMARINDA</h1>
            <p>Pendidikan Terpadu: PAUD, Taman Kanak-Kanak (TK A &amp; TK B) dan TPQ ULINNUHA</p>
            <p>Jl. Wijaya Kusuma V-A No. 38, Samarinda Ulu | WhatsApp Resmi: 0813-4714-2829</p>
          </div>
          <div class="title">
            <h2>Laporan Rekapitulasi Pembayaran SPP Siswa</h2>
            <p style="margin: 3px 0 0; color: #64748b;">Per Tanggal: ${today}</p>
          </div>
          <div class="kpi-grid">
            <div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-lbl">Total Tagihan</div><div class="kpi-val">${formatIDR(totalInv)}</div></div>
            <div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-lbl">Total Lunas</div><div class="kpi-val" style="color: #059669;">${formatIDR(totalPaid)}</div></div>
            <div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-lbl">Total Tunggakan</div><div class="kpi-val" style="color: #dc2626;">${formatIDR(totalUnpaid)}</div></div>
            <div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-lbl">Realisasi Kas</div><div class="kpi-val">${percent}%</div></div>
          </div>
          <table>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th style="width: 30px;">No</th>
                <th>No. Tagihan</th>
                <th>Nama Siswa</th>
                <th>Keterangan / Bulan</th>
                <th style="text-align: right;">Nominal</th>
                <th style="text-align: center;">Status</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>${rowsHtml}</tbody>
          </table>
          <div class="ttd-wrap">
            <div class="ttd">
              <p>Mengetahui,<br>Ketua Yayasan,</p>
              <br><br><br>
              <p><strong>( Makhrus )</strong></p>
            </div>
            <div class="ttd">
              <p>Samarinda, ${today}<br>Bendahara Yayasan,</p>
              <br><br><br>
              <p><strong>( Sri Pragawati )</strong></p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="no-print" style="text-align: center; margin-top: 30px;">
            <button onclick="window.print()" style="padding: 10px 24px; background: #0284c7; color: white; border: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold; cursor: pointer;">Cetak Laporan Rekap Ini</button>
          </div>
          <script>window.onload = function() { window.print(); };<\/script>
        </body>
        </html>
      `);
      printWindow.document.close();
    }

    (async () => {
      await refreshAll();
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

        <div class="md:col-span-7 space-y-3">
          <!-- Toggle Pilihan Metode: File vs URL -->
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 p-1 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl w-fit border border-gray-200">
            <button type="button" id="tab-footer-file" onclick="setFooterMode('file')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all bg-white text-gray-800 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 16l4.586-4.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L16 16m-2-2l1.586-1.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L20 14m-6-6h.01M6 20h12a2 2 0 002-2V6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z"/></svg>
              <span>Unggah File Komputer</span>
            </button>
            <button type="button" id="tab-footer-url" onclick="setFooterMode('url')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M13.828 10.172a4 4 0 00-5.656 0l-4 4a4 4 0 105.656 5.656l1.102-1.101m-.758-4.899a4 4 0 005.656 0l4-4a4 4 0 00-5.656-5.656l-1.1 1.1"/></svg>
              <span>Gunakan Link / URL Foto</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          <!-- Mode A: File Upload Komputer -->
          <div id="footer-file-container" class="space-y-2">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
              <input type="file" id="footer-bg-file" accept="image/*" class="hidden" onchange="handleFooterBgUpload(event)">
              <button type="button" onclick="document.getElementById('footer-bg-file').click()" class="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold bg-primary-50 hover:bg-primary-100 text-primary-700 rounded-xl transition border border-primary-200 flex items-center gap-2 shadow-sm">
                <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 16l4.586-4.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L16 16m-2-2l1.586-1.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L20 14m-6-6h.01M6 20h12a2 2 0 002-2V6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z"/></svg>
                <span>Pilih Foto dari Perangkat...</span>
              </button>
              <span id="footer-file-name" class="text-xs text-gray-500 italic">Belum ada file dipilih</span>
            </div>
            <p class="text-[11px] text-gray-400">Pilih foto gedung atau lingkungan sekolah berformat JPG/PNG.</p>
          </div>

          <!-- Mode B: URL Link Foto -->
          <div id="footer-url-container" class="hidden space-y-2">
            <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase">Link URL Foto</label>
            <input type="text" id="set-footer-bg" oninput="updateFooterBgPreview()" class="block w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-sm border border-gray-300 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 font-mono text-xs" placeholder="https://images.unsplash.com/... atau images/school-campus.jpg">
            <p class="text-[11px] text-gray-400">Pastikan URL berawalan http:// atau https:// dan dapat diakses publik.</p>
          </div>

          <div class="pt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
            <button type="button" onclick="resetFooterBgToDefault()" class="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg transition border border-gray-200">
              Reset ke Foto Bawaan Kampus
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Preview Card -->
        <div class="md:col-span-5">
          <div class="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5">Pratinjau Tampilan Footer (Gradasi 50%)</div>
          <div class="relative h-28 w-full rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-200 shadow-inner bg-slate-950 flex flex-col justify-end p-3">
            <div id="footer-preview-bg" class="absolute inset-0 bg-cover bg-center transition-all duration-300" style="background-image: url('../../public/images/school-campus.jpg');"></div>
            <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-slate-950 via-slate-950/80 to-slate-900/60 pointer-events-none"></div>
            <div class="relative z-10 text-white">
              <div class="text-xs font-extrabold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-sky-400"></span>
                <span>YAYASAN AT TARBIYYAH WIJAYA KUSUMA SAMARINDA</span>
              </div>
              <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-300">&copy; 2026 Hak Cipta Dilindungi</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

  </main>

  <!-- Modal Tambah Slide Carousel -->
  <div id="slide-modal" class="hidden fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-gray-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
    <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 shadow-2xl border border-gray-200 animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-200">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-gray-100 mb-4">
        <div>
          <h3 class="text-base font-bold text-gray-900">Tambah Slide Hero Beranda</h3>
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Pilih salah satu metode: unggah foto dari komputer ATAU gunakan link URL gambar.</p>
        </div>
        <button type="button" onclick="closeAddSlideModal()" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-2xl leading-none">&times;</button>
      </div>

      <form id="slide-form" class="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase mb-1">Judul / Keterangan Banner *</label>
          <input type="text" id="slide-caption" required class="block w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-xs border border-gray-300 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500" placeholder="Contoh: Suasana Belajar Ceria Santri PAUD & TK">
        </div>

        <!-- Tab Pemisah Terarah: Upload File VS Link URL -->
        <div>
          <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase mb-2">Pilih Sumber Gambar</label>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 p-1 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl border border-gray-200">
            <button type="button" id="tab-slide-file" onclick="setSlideInputMode('file')" class="py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-white text-gray-900 shadow-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
              <svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-primary-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 16l4.586-4.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L16 16m-2-2l1.586-1.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L20 14m-6-6h.01M6 20h12a2 2 0 002-2V6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z"/></svg>
              <span>1. Upload dari Galeri/PC</span>
            </button>
            <button type="button" id="tab-slide-url" onclick="setSlideInputMode('url')" class="py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-900 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
              <svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M13.828 10.172a4 4 0 00-5.656 0l-4 4a4 4 0 105.656 5.656l1.102-1.101m-.758-4.899a4 4 0 005.656 0l4-4a4 4 0 00-5.656-5.656l-1.1 1.1"/></svg>
              <span>2. Masukkan Link/URL Foto</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Mode Input A: File Picker -->
        <div id="slide-mode-file" class="space-y-2">
          <label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">Pilih Berkas Foto Gambar</label>
          <div class="border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200 hover:border-primary-400 rounded-xl p-4 text-center cursor-pointer transition bg-gray-50/50" onclick="document.getElementById('slide-file').click()">
            <input type="file" id="slide-file" accept="image/*" class="hidden" onchange="onSlideFileSelected(event)">
            <svg class="w-8 h-8 mx-auto text-gray-400 mb-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M7 16a4 4 0 01-.88-7.903A5 5 0 1115.9 6L16 6a5 5 0 011 9.9M15 13l-3-3m0 0l-3 3m3-3v12"/></svg>
            <div id="slide-file-label" class="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">Klik untuk mencari foto dari komputer</div>
            <div class="text-[11px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5">Format didukung: JPG, PNG, WEBP (Maks 5MB)</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Mode Input B: Link URL Text -->
        <div id="slide-mode-url" class="hidden space-y-2">
          <label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">Tautan Gambar (Direct Image URL)</label>
          <input type="url" id="slide-url" oninput="onSlideUrlChanged()" class="block w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-xs border border-gray-300 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 font-mono" placeholder="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-...">
          <p class="text-[11px] text-gray-400">Masukkan link gambar langsung dari web atau CDN cloud.</p>
        </div>

        <!-- Live Image Preview Box -->
        <div class="space-y-1.5">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span class="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-500">Pratinjau Foto Slide</span>
            <button type="button" id="btn-clear-slide-preview" onclick="clearSlidePreview()" class="hidden text-[11px] text-red-600 font-semibold hover:underline">Hapus / Reset</button>
          </div>
          <div class="h-32 w-full rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-gray-100 border border-gray-200 relative flex items-center justify-center">
            <img id="slide-preview-img" src="" alt="Pratinjau Slide" class="hidden w-full h-full object-cover">
            <div id="slide-preview-placeholder" class="text-xs text-gray-400 text-center px-4">
              Foto belum dipilih. Pratinjau akan muncul di sini.
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="flex justify-end gap-2.5 pt-3 border-t border-gray-100">
          <button type="button" onclick="closeAddSlideModal()" class="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-xl">Batal</button>
          <button type="submit" id="btn-save-slide" class="px-5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 rounded-xl shadow-sm transition">
            Tambahkan Slide
          </button>
        </div>

    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Scripts -->
  <script src="../../shared/js/auth.js"></script>
  <script src="../../shared/js/api.js"></script>
  <script src="../../shared/js/shell.js"></script>

  <script>
    requireAuth('super_admin,admin_tu');

    const MENU = Shell.ADMIN_MENU;

    Shell.init({
      menu: MENU,
      active: 'cms_setting.html',
      portalLabel: 'Portal Admin',
      title: 'Pengaturan Website & Tampilan',
      subtitle: 'CMS Banner Hero & Media Sosial Yayasan'
    });

    let currentHeroImages = [];
    let slideInputMode = 'file'; // 'file' atau 'url'
    let selectedSlideFile = null;
    let selectedSlideDataUrl = '';

    function showAddSlideModal() {
      document.getElementById('slide-form')?.reset();
      selectedSlideFile = null;
      selectedSlideDataUrl = '';
      setSlideInputMode('file');
      clearSlidePreview();
      document.getElementById('slide-modal')?.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeAddSlideModal() {
      document.getElementById('slide-modal')?.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    // Toggle Metode Footer Background
    function setFooterMode(mode) {
      const tabFile = document.getElementById('tab-footer-file');
      const tabUrl = document.getElementById('tab-footer-url');
      const contFile = document.getElementById('footer-file-container');
      const contUrl = document.getElementById('footer-url-container');

      if (mode === 'file') {
        tabFile.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all bg-white text-gray-800 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1.5';
        tabUrl.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5';
        contFile.classList.remove('hidden');
        contUrl.classList.add('hidden');
      } else {
        tabUrl.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all bg-white text-gray-800 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1.5';
        tabFile.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5';
        contUrl.classList.remove('hidden');
        contFile.classList.add('hidden');
        // Reset file input label saat beralih ke URL
        const fileIn = document.getElementById('footer-bg-file');
        if (fileIn) fileIn.value = '';
        const nameLbl = document.getElementById('footer-file-name');
        if (nameLbl) nameLbl.textContent = 'Mode Tautan URL aktif';
      }
    }

    // Toggle Sumber Input Slide Hero (Pembatas Eksklusif)
    function setSlideInputMode(mode) {
      slideInputMode = mode;
      const tabFile = document.getElementById('tab-slide-file');
      const tabUrl = document.getElementById('tab-slide-url');
      const modeFile = document.getElementById('slide-mode-file');
      const modeUrl = document.getElementById('slide-mode-url');

      if (mode === 'file') {
        tabFile.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-white text-gray-900 shadow-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5';
        tabUrl.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-900 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5';
        modeFile.classList.remove('hidden');
        modeUrl.classList.add('hidden');

        // Batas tegas: Kosongkan input URL jika beralih ke file
        document.getElementById('slide-url').value = '';
        if (selectedSlideDataUrl) {
          showSlidePreview(selectedSlideDataUrl);
        } else {
          hideSlidePreview();
        }
      } else {
        tabUrl.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-white text-gray-900 shadow-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5';
        tabFile.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-900 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5';
        modeUrl.classList.remove('hidden');
        modeFile.classList.add('hidden');

        // Batas tegas: Kosongkan input file jika beralih ke URL
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('slide-file');
        if (fileInput) fileInput.value = '';
        selectedSlideFile = null;
        selectedSlideDataUrl = '';
        document.getElementById('slide-file-label').textContent = 'Klik untuk mencari foto dari komputer';

        onSlideUrlChanged();
      }
    }

    function onSlideFileSelected(e) {
      const file = e.target.files && e.target.files[0];
      if (!file) return;

      if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
        alert('Harap pilih file gambar (JPG, PNG, atau WEBP)');
        e.target.value = '';
        return;
      }

      selectedSlideFile = file;
      document.getElementById('slide-file-label').textContent = `${file.name} (${(file.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB)`;

      // Baca sebagai data URL untuk live image preview
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = function(evt) {
        selectedSlideDataUrl = evt.target.result;
        showSlidePreview(selectedSlideDataUrl);
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    }

    function onSlideUrlChanged() {
      const url = document.getElementById('slide-url').value.trim();
      if (url && (url.startsWith('http://') || url.startsWith('https://') || url.startsWith('images/') || url.startsWith('/images/'))) {
        showSlidePreview(url);
      } else {
        hideSlidePreview();
      }
    }

    function showSlidePreview(src) {
      const img = document.getElementById('slide-preview-img');
      const placeholder = document.getElementById('slide-preview-placeholder');
      const clearBtn = document.getElementById('btn-clear-slide-preview');

      img.src = src;
      img.classList.remove('hidden');
      img.onerror = () => {
        img.classList.add('hidden');
        placeholder.classList.remove('hidden');
        placeholder.textContent = 'Gambar tidak dapat dimuat dari link ini.';
      };
      img.onload = () => {
        placeholder.classList.add('hidden');
      };
      placeholder.classList.add('hidden');
      if (clearBtn) clearBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function hideSlidePreview() {
      const img = document.getElementById('slide-preview-img');
      const placeholder = document.getElementById('slide-preview-placeholder');
      const clearBtn = document.getElementById('btn-clear-slide-preview');

      img.src = '';
      img.classList.add('hidden');
      placeholder.classList.remove('hidden');
      placeholder.textContent = 'Foto belum dipilih. Pratinjau akan muncul di sini.';
      if (clearBtn) clearBtn.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    function clearSlidePreview() {
      const fileIn = document.getElementById('slide-file');
      if (fileIn) fileIn.value = '';
      const urlIn = document.getElementById('slide-url');
      if (urlIn) urlIn.value = '';
      selectedSlideFile = null;
      selectedSlideDataUrl = '';
      document.getElementById('slide-file-label').textContent = 'Klik untuk mencari foto dari komputer';
      hideSlidePreview();
    }

    async function loadSettings() {
      try {
        const res = await apiCall('web_settings', 'get');
        if (res && res.success && res.data) {
          const d = res.data;
          document.getElementById('set-school-name').value = d.school_name || 'YAYASAN AT TARBIYYAH WIJAYA KUSUMA SAMARINDA';
          document.getElementById('set-tagline').value = d.tagline || 'Membina Generasi Qurani, Berakhlak Mulia & Berprestasi';
          document.getElementById('set-whatsapp').value = d.whatsapp || '081347142829';
          document.getElementById('set-phone').value = d.phone || '081347142829';
          document.getElementById('set-email').value = d.email || 'info@yayasan.sch.id';
          document.getElementById('set-address').value = d.address || 'Jl. Wijaya Kusuma V-A No. 38, Kel. Air Putih, Kec. Samarinda Ulu, Kota Samarinda, Kalimantan Timur 75124';
          document.getElementById('set-campus-note').value = d.campus_note || 'Kampus Utama Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma';
          document.getElementById('set-maps-url').value = d.maps_url || 'https://maps.google.com/?q=Jl.+Wijaya+Kusuma+V-A+No.+38,+Air+Putih,+Samarinda+Ulu,+Kota+Samarinda,+Kalimantan+Timur+75124';
          document.getElementById('set-maps-embed-url').value = d.maps_embed_url || 'https://maps.google.com/maps?q=Jl.+Wijaya+Kusuma+V-A+No.+38,+Air+Putih,+Samarinda+Ulu,+Kota+Samarinda,+Kalimantan+Timur+75124&t=&z=16&ie=UTF8&iwloc=&output=embed';
          document.getElementById('set-facebook').value = d.facebook_url || 'https://facebook.com/yayasansamarinda';
          document.getElementById('set-instagram').value = d.instagram_url || 'https://instagram.com/yayasansamarinda';
          document.getElementById('set-youtube').value = d.youtube_url || 'https://youtube.com/@yayasansamarinda';
          document.getElementById('set-footer-bg').value = d.footer_bg_url || '/images/school-campus.jpg';
          updateFooterBgPreview();
          updateMapsPreview();
          updateMapsLinkButton();

          // Hero images
          currentHeroImages = Array.isArray(d.hero_images) ? d.hero_images : [];
          renderSlides();
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error loading web settings:', e);
      }
    }

    function updateMapsLinkButton() {
      const url = document.getElementById('set-maps-url')?.value.trim();
      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-test-maps-url');
      if (btn) btn.href = url || '#';
    }

    function updateMapsPreview() {
      const embedUrl = document.getElementById('set-maps-embed-url')?.value.trim();
      const iframe = document.getElementById('maps-preview-iframe');
      if (iframe) {
        iframe.src = embedUrl || 'https://maps.google.com/maps?q=Jl.+Wijaya+Kusuma+V-A+No.+38,+Air+Putih,+Samarinda+Ulu,+Kota+Samarinda,+Kalimantan+Timur+75124&t=&z=16&ie=UTF8&iwloc=&output=embed';
      }
    }

    function resetMapsToDefault() {
      const defaultAddr = 'Jl. Wijaya Kusuma V-A No. 38, Kel. Air Putih, Kec. Samarinda Ulu, Kota Samarinda, Kalimantan Timur 75124';
      const defaultNote = 'Kampus Utama Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma';
      const defaultMapsUrl = 'https://maps.google.com/?q=Jl.+Wijaya+Kusuma+V-A+No.+38,+Air+Putih,+Samarinda+Ulu,+Kota+Samarinda,+Kalimantan+Timur+75124';
      const defaultEmbed = 'https://maps.google.com/maps?q=Jl.+Wijaya+Kusuma+V-A+No.+38,+Air+Putih,+Samarinda+Ulu,+Kota+Samarinda,+Kalimantan+Timur+75124&t=&z=16&ie=UTF8&iwloc=&output=embed';

      document.getElementById('set-address').value = defaultAddr;
      document.getElementById('set-campus-note').value = defaultNote;
      document.getElementById('set-maps-url').value = defaultMapsUrl;
      document.getElementById('set-maps-embed-url').value = defaultEmbed;
      updateMapsPreview();
      updateMapsLinkButton();
      showToast('Alamat dan peta berhasil di-reset ke Kampus Wijaya Kusuma.', 'info');
    }

    function updateFooterBgPreview() {
      const val = document.getElementById('set-footer-bg')?.value.trim();
      const el = document.getElementById('footer-preview-bg');
      if (el) {
        el.style.backgroundImage = `url('${val || '/images/school-campus.jpg'}')`;
      }
    }

    function resetFooterBgToDefault() {
      const input = document.getElementById('set-footer-bg');
      if (input) input.value = '/images/school-campus.jpg';
      const fileIn = document.getElementById('footer-bg-file');
      if (fileIn) fileIn.value = '';
      const nameLbl = document.getElementById('footer-file-name');
      if (nameLbl) nameLbl.textContent = 'Foto bawaan kampus';
      updateFooterBgPreview();
      showToast('Foto latar footer dikembalikan ke foto bawaan kampus.', 'info');
    }

    async function handleFooterBgUpload(e) {
      const file = e.target.files && e.target.files[0];
      if (!file) return;

      const nameLbl = document.getElementById('footer-file-name');
      if (nameLbl) nameLbl.textContent = `${file.name} (${(file.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB)`;

      try {
        showToast('Mengunggah foto footer...', 'info');
        const url = await uploadToSupabaseStorage(file, 'footer_bg');
        if (url) {
          document.getElementById('set-footer-bg').value = url;
          updateFooterBgPreview();
          showToast('Foto latar footer berhasil dipilih! Tekan "Simpan Semua Pengaturan" di atas.', 'success');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Gagal memproses foto footer', 'error');
      }
    }

    function renderSlides() {
      const container = document.getElementById('slides-grid');
      if (!container) return;
      if (!currentHeroImages || currentHeroImages.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full py-6 text-center text-xs text-gray-400">Belum ada slide gambar hero.</div>';
        return;
      }

      container.innerHTML = currentHeroImages.map((s, idx) => `
        <div class="border border-gray-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-gray-50 flex flex-col justify-between group shadow-sm">
          <div class="h-36 w-full overflow-hidden bg-gray-200 relative">
            <img src="${s.url}" alt="${s.caption || 'Slide'}" class="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-300" onerror="this.src='/images/school-campus.jpg'">
            <span class="absolute top-2 left-2 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-black/60 text-white text-[10px] font-bold">Slide #${idx + 1}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="p-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-800 truncate">${s.caption || 'Slide Banner'}</span>
            <button type="button" onclick="deleteSlide(${idx})" class="p-1.5 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg transition-colors" title="Hapus Slide">
              <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"/></svg>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    function deleteSlide(idx) {
      if (confirm('Hapus slide gambar ini dari carousel beranda?')) {
        currentHeroImages.splice(idx, 1);
        renderSlides();
        showToast('Slide dihapus dari daftar.', 'info');
      }
    }

    // Submit Form Tambah Slide (Mematuhi Mode Terarah)
    document.getElementById('slide-form').addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const caption = document.getElementById('slide-caption').value.trim();
      const saveBtn = document.getElementById('btn-save-slide');
      let finalImageUrl = '';

      if (slideInputMode === 'file') {
        if (!selectedSlideFile) {
          alert('Harap pilih file foto dari komputer terlebih dahulu!');
          return;
        }
        saveBtn.disabled = true;
        saveBtn.textContent = 'Mengunggah Foto...';
        try {
          finalImageUrl = await uploadToSupabaseStorage(selectedSlideFile, 'hero_slide');
        } catch (uploadErr) {
          finalImageUrl = selectedSlideDataUrl;
        }
        if (!finalImageUrl && selectedSlideFile) {
          finalImageUrl = await new Promise(resolve => {
            const r = new FileReader();
            r.onload = ev => resolve(ev.target.result);
            r.onerror = () => resolve('');
            r.readAsDataURL(selectedSlideFile);
          });
        }
      } else {
        const urlInput = document.getElementById('slide-url').value.trim();
        if (!urlInput) {
          alert('Harap masukkan alamat URL gambar langsung!');
          return;
        }
        finalImageUrl = urlInput;
      }

      if (!finalImageUrl) {
        alert('Gagal memproses gambar. Pastikan file atau link gambar valid.');
        saveBtn.disabled = false;
        saveBtn.textContent = 'Tambahkan Slide';
        return;
      }

      saveBtn.disabled = true;
      saveBtn.textContent = 'Menyimpan...';

      try {
        currentHeroImages.push({ url: finalImageUrl, caption });
        renderSlides();
        closeAddSlideModal();
        showToast('Slide berhasil ditambahkan! Silakan klik "Simpan Semua Pengaturan" untuk menerapkan.', 'success');
      } finally {
        saveBtn.disabled = false;
        saveBtn.textContent = 'Tambahkan Slide';
      }
    });

    async function saveAllSettings() {
      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-save-all');
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.textContent = 'Menyimpan ke Database...';

      const payload = {
        school_name: document.getElementById('set-school-name').value.trim(),
        tagline: document.getElementById('set-tagline').value.trim(),
        whatsapp: document.getElementById('set-whatsapp').value.trim(),
        phone: document.getElementById('set-phone').value.trim(),
        email: document.getElementById('set-email').value.trim(),
        address: document.getElementById('set-address').value.trim(),
        campus_note: document.getElementById('set-campus-note').value.trim(),
        maps_url: document.getElementById('set-maps-url').value.trim(),
        maps_embed_url: document.getElementById('set-maps-embed-url').value.trim(),
        facebook_url: document.getElementById('set-facebook').value.trim(),
        instagram_url: document.getElementById('set-instagram').value.trim(),
        youtube_url: document.getElementById('set-youtube').value.trim(),
        footer_bg_url: document.getElementById('set-footer-bg').value.trim() || '/images/school-campus.jpg',
        hero_images: currentHeroImages
      };

      try {
        const res = await apiCall('web_settings', 'update', payload);
        if (res.success) {
          // Sinkronisasi otomatis ke modul schools agar seluruh endpoint website terintegrasi
          try {
            await apiCall('schools', 'update', {
              name: payload.school_name,
              address: payload.address,
              campus_note: payload.campus_note,
              maps_url: payload.maps_url,
              maps_embed_url: payload.maps_embed_url,
              phone: payload.phone,
              email: payload.email,
              whatsapp_number: payload.whatsapp,
              facebook_url: payload.facebook_url,
              instagram_url: payload.instagram_url,
              youtube_url: payload.youtube_url,
              footer_bg_url: payload.footer_bg_url
            });
          } catch (_) {}

          showToast('Seluruh pengaturan website & lokasi sekolah berhasil diperbarui!', 'success');
          // Clear session cache so visitors immediately see new settings
          sessionStorage.removeItem('siyayasan_school_profile');
        } else {
          showToast(res.message || 'Gagal menyimpan pengaturan', 'error');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Terjadi kesalahan sistem saat menyimpan', 'error');
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = 'Simpan Semua Pengaturan';
      }
    }

    // Expose all interactive functions to window scope
    window.showAddSlideModal = showAddSlideModal;
    window.closeAddSlideModal = closeAddSlideModal;
    window.setFooterMode = setFooterMode;
    window.setSlideInputMode = setSlideInputMode;
    window.clearSlidePreview = clearSlidePreview;
    window.onSlideFileSelected = onSlideFileSelected;
    window.onSlideUrlChanged = onSlideUrlChanged;
    window.handleFooterBgUpload = handleFooterBgUpload;
    window.resetFooterBgToDefault = resetFooterBgToDefault;
    window.updateMapsLinkButton = updateMapsLinkButton;
    window.updateMapsPreview = updateMapsPreview;
    window.resetMapsToDefault = resetMapsToDefault;
    window.renderSlides = renderSlides;
    window.deleteSlide = deleteSlide;
    window.saveAllSettings = saveAllSettings;

    // Load data immediately or upon DOM ready
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', loadSettings);
    } else {
      loadSettings();
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

                    <tbody id="dash-spp-list" class="divide-y divide-slate-100">

  <script>
    // Guard Halaman: Wajib Login sebagai Siswa
    if (!requireAuth('siswa,siswa_aktif,calon_siswa')) {
      // Handled inside requireAuth
    }

    function formatIDR(num) {
      return 'Rp ' + Number(num || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID');
    }

    async function initDashboard() {
      const user = getCurrentUser() || {};
      const rawRole = (user.role && user.role.code ? user.role.code : user.role) || 'calon_siswa';
      const isSiswaAktif = rawRole === 'siswa_aktif' || rawRole === 'siswa';

      // Setup Shell Nav (Tanpa Rapor/Akademik sesuai batasan sistem lean)
      const studentMenu = isSiswaAktif ? [
        { href: 'dashboard.html', label: '📊 Dashboard' },
        { href: 'biodata.html', label: '👤 Biodata Siswa' },
        { href: 'spp.html', label: '💳 Tagihan SPP' },
        { href: 'dokumen.html', label: '📁 Berkas Dokumen' }
      ] : [
        { href: 'dashboard.html', label: '📊 Status PPDB' },
        { href: 'biodata.html', label: '📝 Lengkapi Biodata' },
        { href: 'dokumen.html', label: '📁 Upload Dokumen' }
      ];

      Shell.init({
        menu: studentMenu,
        active: 'dashboard.html',
        portalLabel: isSiswaAktif ? 'Portal Siswa Aktif' : 'Portal Calon Siswa (PPDB)',
        title: isSiswaAktif ? 'Dashboard Siswa' : 'Status Pendaftaran PPDB',
        subtitle: 'Selamat datang di Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma Samarinda',
        showRefresh: true,
        onRefresh: () => loadData(isSiswaAktif)
      });

      await loadData(isSiswaAktif);
    }

    async function loadData(isSiswaAktif) {
      const user = getCurrentUser() || {};
      document.getElementById('loading-state').classList.remove('hidden');
      document.getElementById('dashboard-content').classList.add('hidden');

      try {
        const res = await apiCall('siswa', 'get_profile');
        const student = (res.success && res.data) ? res.data : {};

        // Render Welcome Info
        const studentName = student.full_name || student.name || user.full_name || 'Santri';
        document.getElementById('student-name').textContent = studentName;
        document.getElementById('badge-role-text').textContent = isSiswaAktif ? 'Siswa Aktif' : 'Calon Siswa PPDB';

        if (isSiswaAktif) {
          const nis = student.nis || user.nis || '-';
          document.getElementById('student-meta').textContent = `NIS: ${nis} • ${student.kelas_sekarang || student.class_name || 'PAUD / TK Terpadu'}`;
          document.getElementById('section-siswa-aktif').classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('section-calon-siswa').classList.add('hidden');

          // Load SPP & Info
          await loadSiswaAktifData(student, user);
        } else {
          document.getElementById('student-meta').textContent = `Nomor Pendaftaran: ${student.id || 'REG-' + Date.now().toString().slice(-6)} • Status: Menunggu Berkas`;
          document.getElementById('section-calon-siswa').classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('section-siswa-aktif').classList.add('hidden');

          // Update PPDB tracker
          updatePPDBTracker(student);
        }

      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error loading dashboard:', err);
      } finally {
        document.getElementById('loading-state').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('dashboard-content').classList.remove('hidden');
      }
    }

    async function loadSiswaAktifData(student, user) {
      document.getElementById('card-kelas').textContent = student.kelas_sekarang || student.class_name || 'PAUD / TK Terpadu';
      document.getElementById('card-semester').textContent = student.semester_sekarang || 'Tahun Ajaran 2026/2027';
      
      // Populate Identitas
      document.getElementById('info-nama').textContent = student.full_name || student.name || user.full_name || '-';
      document.getElementById('info-nis').textContent = student.nis || user.nis || '-';
      document.getElementById('info-kelas').textContent = student.kelas_sekarang || student.class_name || '-';
      document.getElementById('info-wali').textContent = student.parent_name || student.father_name || user.parent_name || '-';
      const ttl = [student.birth_place, student.birth_date].filter(Boolean).join(', ');
      document.getElementById('info-ttl').textContent = ttl || '-';
      document.getElementById('info-kontak').textContent = student.parent_phone || student.phone || '-';

      try {
        const sppRes = await apiCall('siswa', 'get_spp');
        const listTbody = document.getElementById('dash-spp-list');

        if (sppRes.success && sppRes.data) {
          const invoices = sppRes.data.invoices || [];
          const unpaid = invoices.filter(i => i.status === 'unpaid');
          const totalUnpaid = unpaid.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (Number(item.amount) || 0), 0);

          if (totalUnpaid > 0) {
            document.getElementById('card-spp').textContent = formatIDR(totalUnpaid);
            document.getElementById('card-spp-desc').textContent = `${unpaid.length} tagihan belum lunas`;
            document.getElementById('card-spp-desc').className = 'text-xs text-rose-600 font-semibold';
          } else {
            document.getElementById('card-spp').textContent = 'Rp 0';
            document.getElementById('card-spp-desc').textContent = 'Semua tagihan lunas';
            document.getElementById('card-spp-desc').className = 'text-xs text-emerald-600 font-medium';
          }

          if (invoices.length === 0) {
            listTbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="py-4 text-center text-slate-400 text-xs">Belum ada tagihan diterbitkan.</td></tr>';
          } else {
            // Tampilkan maksimal 4 tagihan terbaru
            const recent = invoices.slice(0, 4);
            listTbody.innerHTML = recent.map(inv => {
              const isPaid = inv.status === 'paid';
              const isWaiting = inv.status === 'menunggu_verifikasi';
              let badgeCls = 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200';
              let badgeText = 'Belum Lunas';
              if (isPaid) {
                badgeCls = 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200';
                badgeText = 'Lunas';
              } else if (isWaiting) {
                badgeCls = 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200';
                badgeText = 'Menunggu Verifikasi';
              }

              return `
                <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50/50">
                  <td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-semibold text-slate-800">${inv.period || inv.item_name || 'SPP'}</td>
                  <td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-mono font-bold text-slate-900">${formatIDR(inv.amount)}</td>
                  <td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-500">${inv.due_date || '-'}</td>
                  <td class="py-2.5 px-3">
                    <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold border ${badgeCls}">
                      ${badgeText}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              `;
            }).join('');
          }
        } else {
          listTbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="py-4 text-center text-slate-400 text-xs">Data tagihan tidak tersedia.</td></tr>';
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('SPP data fallback error:', e);
      }
    }

    function updatePPDBTracker(student) {
      const hasBiodata = student.birth_date && student.address;
      if (hasBiodata) {
        document.getElementById('step-biodata-box').className = 'flex items-start gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl bg-primary-50/60 border border-primary-200/60';
        document.getElementById('step-biodata-icon').className = 'w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-primary-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shrink-0';
        document.getElementById('step-biodata-icon').textContent = '✓';
        document.getElementById('step-biodata-desc').textContent = 'Biodata terisi';
      }

      const status = student.status_pendaftaran || 'pending';
      const pill = document.getElementById('ppdb-status-pill');
      if (status === 'accepted' || status === 'lolos') {
        pill.className = 'inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold bg-primary-50 text-primary-800 border border-primary-200';
        pill.textContent = 'Diterima Menjadi Siswa Baru';
      } else if (status === 'rejected') {
        pill.className = 'inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold bg-red-50 text-red-800 border border-red-200';
        pill.textContent = 'Belum Lolos Seleksi';
      } else {
        pill.className = 'inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200';
        pill.textContent = 'Menunggu Validasi Dokumen';
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initDashboard);
  </script>
</body>
</html>

          <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Tagihan Bulan</label>
          <input type="text" id="pay-month-name" readonly class="block w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs bg-slate-100 border border-slate-300 rounded-xl text-slate-700 font-bold">
        </div>

        <div>
          <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Jumlah Transfer</label>
          <input type="text" id="pay-amount" readonly class="block w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs bg-slate-100 border border-slate-300 rounded-xl text-slate-700 font-bold font-mono">
        </div>

        <div>
          <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Upload Bukti Transfer *</label>
          <input type="file" id="pay-proof" accept="image/*" required class="block w-full text-xs text-slate-600 file:mr-3 file:py-2 file:px-3 file:rounded-xl file:border-0 file:text-xs file:font-bold file:bg-brand-50 file:text-brand-700 hover:file:bg-brand-100">
          <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1 block">Format: JPG, PNG, atau WebP (Maks. 5 MB)</span>
        </div>

        <!-- Preview Gambar -->
        <div id="proof-preview-wrap" class="hidden">
          <span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase block mb-1">Preview Bukti:</span>
          <img id="proof-preview-img" src="" alt="Preview" class="w-full max-h-40 object-contain rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50">
        </div>

        <div class="flex justify-end gap-2.5 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
          <button type="button" onclick="closePaymentModal()" class="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-xl">Batal</button>
          <button type="submit" id="submit-pay-btn" class="px-5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-700 rounded-xl shadow-md shadow-brand-600/20 transition">
            Kirim Konfirmasi
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Scripts -->
  <script src="../../shared/js/supabase-config.js"></script>

  <script>
    if (!requireAuth('siswa,siswa_aktif')) {}

    let invoicesCache = [];

    function showRekeningModal() { document.getElementById('rekening-modal')?.classList.remove('hidden'); }
    function closeRekeningModal() { document.getElementById('rekening-modal')?.classList.add('hidden'); }
    function closePaymentModal() { document.getElementById('payment-modal')?.classList.add('hidden'); }

    function copyRekening(num, bank) {
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(num).then(() => {
          alert(`Nomor rekening ${bank} (${num}) berhasil disalin!`);
        });
      } else {
        prompt(`Salin nomor rekening ${bank}:`, num);
      }
    }

    function openPayModal(invId, month, amount) {
      document.getElementById('pay-invoice-id').value = invId;
      document.getElementById('pay-month-name').value = month;
      document.getElementById('pay-amount').value = formatIDR(amount);
      document.getElementById('proof-preview-wrap').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('pay-proof').value = '';
      document.getElementById('payment-modal')?.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    document.getElementById('pay-proof').addEventListener('change', function(e) {
      const file = e.target.files[0];
      if (file) {
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = function(evt) {
          document.getElementById('proof-preview-img').src = evt.target.result;
          document.getElementById('proof-preview-wrap').classList.remove('hidden');
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
      }
    });

    async function initPage() {
      Shell.init({
        menu: [
          { href: 'dashboard.html', label: '📊 Dashboard' },
          { href: 'biodata.html', label: '👤 Biodata Siswa' },
          { href: 'spp.html', label: '💳 Tagihan SPP' },
          { href: 'dokumen.html', label: '📁 Berkas Dokumen' }
        ],
        active: 'spp.html',
        portalLabel: 'Portal Siswa Aktif',
        title: 'Tagihan SPP Siswa',
        subtitle: 'Status Keuangan & Pembayaran Bulanan'
      });

      // Update link WA Bendahara di header
      const user = getCurrentUser() || {};
      const waMsg = `Assalamu'alaikum Bendahara Sekolah Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma Samarinda,\nSaya wali dari ${user.full_name || user.name || 'Siswa'} (NIS: ${user.nis || '-'}), ingin menanyakan informasi SPP. Terima kasih.`;
      const waBtn = document.getElementById('btn-header-wa');
      if (waBtn) waBtn.href = `https://wa.me/6281347142829?text=${encodeURIComponent(waMsg)}`;

      await loadSPPData();
    }

    async function loadSPPData() {
      try {
        const user = getCurrentUser() || {};
        const res = await apiCall('siswa', 'get_spp', { nis: user.nis });
        const invoices = (res.success && res.data && res.data.invoices) ? res.data.invoices : [];
        invoicesCache = invoices;
        const tbody = document.getElementById('invoices-table-body');

        if (invoices.length === 0) {
          tbody.innerHTML = `
            <tr>
              <td colspan="5" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">
                Belum ada tagihan SPP yang diterbitkan oleh Tata Usaha.
              </td>
            </tr>
          `;
          document.getElementById('stat-unpaid-amount').textContent = 'Rp 0';
          document.getElementById('stat-unpaid-count').textContent = '0 tagihan belum dibayar';
          document.getElementById('stat-paid-count').textContent = '0 Tagihan';
          return;
        }

        const unpaid = invoices.filter(i => i.status === 'unpaid');
        const paid = invoices.filter(i => i.status === 'paid');
        const totalUnpaid = unpaid.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (Number(item.amount) || 0), 0);

        document.getElementById('stat-unpaid-amount').textContent = formatIDR(totalUnpaid);
        document.getElementById('stat-unpaid-count').textContent = `${unpaid.length} tagihan belum dibayar`;
        document.getElementById('stat-paid-count').textContent = `${paid.length} Tagihan`;

        tbody.innerHTML = invoices.map(inv => {
          let statusBadge = '';
          let actionBtn = '';

          if (inv.status === 'paid') {
            statusBadge = '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200">✓ Lunas</span>';
            actionBtn = `
              <button onclick="printSppReceipt('${inv.id}')" title="Cetak kuitansi resmi" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition">
                <svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M17 17h2a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2h2m2 4h6a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2zm8-12V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4h10z"/></svg>
                <span>Cetak Kuitansi</span>
              </button>
            `;
          } else if (inv.status === 'menunggu_verifikasi' || inv.status === 'pending_verification') {
            statusBadge = '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200">⏳ Sedang Diverifikasi</span>';
            const waCheckMsg = `Assalamu'alaikum Bendahara Sekolah, saya telah mengunggah bukti bayar SPP untuk ${inv.month || inv.description || 'SPP'} atas nama ${user.full_name || user.name || 'Siswa'}. Mohon dicek. Terima kasih.`;
            actionBtn = `
              <div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
                <span class="text-amber-700 text-xs font-medium">Menunggu TU</span>
                <a href="https://wa.me/6281347142829?text=${encodeURIComponent(waCheckMsg)}" target="_blank" title="Cek via WhatsApp" class="p-1 text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-700 rounded-lg border border-emerald-200 hover:bg-emerald-50 transition">
                  <i class="fi fi-brands-whatsapp text-xs"></i>
                </a>
              </div>
            `;
          } else {
            statusBadge = '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200">Belum Bayar</span>';
            const waPayMsg = `Assalamu'alaikum Bendahara Yayasan At Tarbiyyah Wijaya Kusuma Samarinda, saya ingin konfirmasi pembayaran SPP:\n- Nama Siswa: ${user.full_name || user.name || '-'}\n- NIS: ${user.nis || '-'}\n- Tagihan: ${inv.month || inv.description || 'SPP'}\n- Nominal: ${formatIDR(inv.amount || 150000)}\nMohon info nomor rekening / konfirmasi pembayaran. Terima kasih.`;
            actionBtn = `
              <div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
                <button onclick="openPayModal('${inv.id}', '${inv.month || inv.description}', ${inv.amount})" class="px-3 py-1 bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-700 text-white font-bold rounded-lg text-xs transition shadow-sm">
                  Upload Bukti
                </button>
                <a href="https://wa.me/6281347142829?text=${encodeURIComponent(waPayMsg)}" target="_blank" title="Konfirmasi via WhatsApp" class="p-1 text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-700 rounded-lg border border-emerald-200 hover:bg-emerald-50 transition">
                  <i class="fi fi-brands-whatsapp text-xs"></i>
                </a>
              </div>
            `;
          }

          const monthLabel = inv.month || inv.item_name || inv.description || 'SPP Bulanan';
          const dueDateFormatted = inv.due_date ? (window.formatDate ? formatDate(inv.due_date) : inv.due_date) : 'Tgl 10';

          return `
            <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
              <td class="py-3 px-4 font-bold text-slate-900">${monthLabel}</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-slate-800 font-mono">${formatIDR(inv.amount)}</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-4 text-slate-500 font-mono">${dueDateFormatted}</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-4">${statusBadge}</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right">${actionBtn}</td>
            </tr>
          `;
        }).join('');

      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error loading SPP data:', err);
      }
    }

    function printSppReceipt(invId) {
      const inv = invoicesCache.find(x => x.id === invId);
      if (!inv) return;
      const user = getCurrentUser() || {};
      const studentName = inv.student_name || user.full_name || user.name || '-';
      const nis = user.nis || inv.nis || '-';
      const className = user.class_name || 'PAUD / TK Terpadu';
      const invNum = inv.invoice_number || ('INV/' + new Date().getFullYear() + '/SPP');
      const month = inv.month || inv.item_name || inv.description || 'SPP Bulanan';
      const amount = inv.amount || 150000;
      const paidAt = inv.paid_at ? (window.formatDate ? formatDate(inv.paid_at) : inv.paid_at) : new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
      const paidBy = inv.paid_by || 'Transfer Bank Yayasan';

      const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=750,height=800');
      if (!printWindow) {
        alert('Mohon izinkan pop-up peramban untuk mencetak kuitansi.');
        return;
      }

      printWindow.document.write(`
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html lang="id">
        <head>
          <meta charset="UTF-8">
          <title>Kuitansi Pembayaran SPP - ${invNum}</title>
          <style>
            body { font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif; padding: 36px; color: #0f172a; line-height: 1.5; }
            .kop { text-align: center; border-bottom: 3px double #0284c7; padding-bottom: 12px; margin-bottom: 24px; }
            .kop h1 { margin: 0; color: #0369a1; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.5px; }
            .kop p { margin: 3px 0 0; font-size: 12px; color: #64748b; }
            .kuitansi-title { text-align: center; margin-bottom: 20px; }
            .kuitansi-title h2 { margin: 0; font-size: 17px; font-weight: 800; text-transform: uppercase; color: #1e293b; letter-spacing: 1px; }
            .kuitansi-num { font-size: 12px; color: #64748b; font-family: monospace; font-weight: 600; margin-top: 4px; }
            .badge-lunas { display: inline-block; background: #ecfdf5; color: #065f46; border: 1.5px solid #a7f3d0; padding: 4px 14px; font-weight: 800; font-size: 13px; border-radius: 9999px; margin-top: 6px; }
            table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 24px; font-size: 13px; }
            td { padding: 9px 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; }
            td.lbl { width: 32%; font-weight: 600; color: #64748b; }
            td.val { font-weight: 700; color: #0f172a; }
            .nominal-box { background: #f0f9ff; border: 2px dashed #0284c7; border-radius: 12px; padding: 14px 20px; margin-bottom: 24px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; }
            .nominal-label { font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; color: #0369a1; text-transform: uppercase; }
            .nominal-val { font-size: 22px; font-weight: 800; color: #0284c7; font-family: monospace; }
            .terbilang { font-size: 12px; color: #475569; font-style: italic; }
            .ttd-wrap { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin-top: 36px; font-size: 12px; }
            .ttd { text-align: center; width: 220px; }
            @media print { .no-print { display: none !important; } }
          </style>
        </head>
        <body>
          <div class="kop">
            <h1>YAYASAN AT TARBIYYAH WIJAYA KUSUMA SAMARINDA</h1>
            <p>Pendidikan Anak Usia Dini (PAUD), TK Terpadu &amp; TPQ ULINNUHA</p>
            <p>Jl. Wijaya Kusuma V-A No. 38, Kel. Air Putih, Kec. Samarinda Ulu, Kota Samarinda | Telp/WA: 0813-4714-2829</p>
          </div>
          <div class="kuitansi-title">
            <h2>Kuitansi Bukti Pembayaran SPP</h2>
            <div class="kuitansi-num">No. Invoice: ${invNum}</div>
            <div class="badge-lunas">&#10003; LUNAS (PEMBAYARAN SAH)</div>
          </div>
          <table>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Telah Diterima Dari</td><td class="val">${studentName}</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Nomor Induk Siswa (NIS)</td><td class="val" style="font-family:monospace;">${nis}</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Kelas / Rombel</td><td class="val">${className}</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Untuk Pembayaran</td><td class="val">Iuran SPP Bulanan - ${month}</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Tanggal Pelunasan</td><td class="val">${paidAt}</td></tr>
            <tr><td class="lbl">Metode Pembayaran</td><td class="val">${paidBy}</td></tr>
          </table>
          <div class="nominal-box">
            <div>
              <div class="nominal-label">Jumlah Uang Diterima:</div>
              <div class="terbilang"># Seratus Lima Puluh Ribu Rupiah #</div>
            </div>
            <div class="nominal-val">${formatIDR(amount)}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="ttd-wrap">
            <div class="ttd">
              <p>Wali Murid / Penyetor,</p>
              <br><br><br>
              <p><strong>( ${studentName} )</strong></p>
            </div>
            <div class="ttd">
              <p>Samarinda, ${paidAt}<br>Bagian Keuangan &amp; Kasir TU,</p>
              <br><br>
              <p><strong>( Sri Pragawati )</strong></p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="no-print" style="text-align: center; margin-top: 32px;">
            <button onclick="window.print()" style="padding: 10px 24px; background: #0284c7; color: white; border: none; border-radius: 8px; font-weight: bold; cursor: pointer;">Cetak Kuitansi Ini</button>
          </div>
          <script>window.onload = function() { window.print(); };<\/script>
        </body>
        </html>
      `);
      printWindow.document.close();
    }

    document.getElementById('payment-form').addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const invId = document.getElementById('pay-invoice-id').value;
      const fileInput = document.getElementById('pay-proof');
      const submitBtn = document.getElementById('submit-pay-btn');

      if (!fileInput.files || fileInput.files.length === 0) {
        alert('Pilih file bukti transfer terlebih dahulu');
        return;
      }

      submitBtn.disabled = true;
      submitBtn.textContent = 'Mengirim...';

      try {
        const file = fileInput.files[0];
        let uploadedUrl = '';

        if (window.uploadToSupabaseStorage && !window.location.hostname.includes('localhost')) {
          try {
            uploadedUrl = await uploadToSupabaseStorage(file, 'spp_proof');
          } catch (_) {}
        }

        if (!uploadedUrl) {
          uploadedUrl = await new Promise((resolve) => {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (evt) => resolve(evt.target.result);
            reader.onerror = () => resolve('');
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
          });
        }

        const res = await apiCall('payments', 'create', {
          invoice_id: invId,
          proof_image_url: uploadedUrl,
          method: 'transfer',
          notes: 'Konfirmasi pembayaran SPP oleh siswa/wali murid'
        });

        if (res.success) {
          showToast('Konfirmasi pembayaran berhasil dikirim! Menunggu verifikasi TU.', 'success');
          closePaymentModal();
          loadSPPData();
        } else {
          showToast(res.message || 'Gagal mengirim konfirmasi', 'error');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Terjadi kesalahan saat mengunggah', 'error');
      } finally {
        submitBtn.disabled = false;
        submitBtn.textContent = 'Kirim Konfirmasi';
      }
    });

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initPage);
  </script>
</body>
</html>

        </div>

        <div>
          <label class="block w-full py-2.5 px-3 bg-brand-50 hover:bg-brand-100 text-brand-800 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl text-center cursor-pointer border border-brand-200 transition-colors">
            <span>Pilih Berkas KK</span>
            <input type="file" id="file-kk" accept="image/*,.pdf" class="hidden" onchange="handleFileSelect('kk')">
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Upload Akte Kelahiran -->
      <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/90 p-5 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between">
        <div>
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Akte Kelahiran</span>
            <span class="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-brand-50 text-brand-700">Wajib</span>
          </div>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-600 mb-4">Scan atau foto Akte Kelahiran resmi dari Disdukcapil. Format: JPG/PNG/PDF (Max. 5MB).</p>

          <div class="p-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 text-center mb-4">
            <svg class="w-8 h-8 text-slate-400 mx-auto mb-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.8" d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z"/></svg>
            <span id="name-akte" class="text-xs text-slate-500 block truncate">Belum dipilih</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label class="block w-full py-2.5 px-3 bg-brand-50 hover:bg-brand-100 text-brand-800 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl text-center cursor-pointer border border-brand-200 transition-colors">
            <span>Pilih Berkas Akte</span>
            <input type="file" id="file-akte" accept="image/*,.pdf" class="hidden" onchange="handleFileSelect('akte')">
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>

    </div>

    <!-- Riwayat Dokumen Terupload -->
    <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/90 p-6 shadow-sm space-y-4">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h2 class="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Daftar Dokumen Terunggah</h2>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Arsip berkas persyaratan yang tersimpan di sistem</p>
        </div>
        <button onclick="loadDocuments()" class="text-xs font-bold text-brand-700 hover:text-brand-800">
          Refresh Daftar
        </button>
      </div>

      <div class="overflow-x-auto">
        <table class="w-full text-left text-xs">
          <thead>
            <tr class="border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
              <th class="py-3 px-4">Jenis Dokumen</th>
              <th class="py-3 px-4">Nama File</th>
              <th class="py-3 px-4">Waktu Unggah</th>
              <th class="py-3 px-4">Status Verifikasi</th>
              <th class="py-3 px-4 text-right">Aksi</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody id="documents-table-body" class="divide-y divide-slate-100">
            <tr>
              <td colspan="5" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">
                Belum ada dokumen yang diunggah.
              </td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>

  <!-- Modal Preview Lightbox Dokumen Internal -->
  <div id="doc-preview-modal" class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm hidden flex items-center justify-center p-4">
    <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl max-w-2xl w-full p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4 max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-slate-100">
        <div>
          <h3 id="modal-doc-title" class="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Pratinjau Berkas Dokumen</h3>
          <p id="modal-doc-subtitle" class="text-xs text-slate-500">Berkas persyaratan siswa</p>
        </div>
        <button onclick="closeDocPreview()" class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold">&times;</button>
      </div>
      <div class="flex-1 overflow-auto flex items-center justify-center min-h-[240px] bg-slate-50 rounded-xl p-2 border border-slate-200">
        <img id="modal-doc-img" src="" alt="Pratinjau Berkas" class="max-h-[60vh] max-w-full object-contain rounded-lg hidden">
        <div id="modal-doc-fallback" class="text-center p-6 space-y-3 hidden">
          <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-primary-50 text-primary-600 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-xl">📄</div>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-600 font-semibold" id="modal-doc-filename">Dokumen Digital</p>
          <a id="modal-doc-open-btn" href="#" target="_blank" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition">
            <span>Buka Berkas di Tab Baru</span>
            &rarr;
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="flex justify-end pt-2">
        <button onclick="closeDocPreview()" class="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition">Tutup Pratinjau</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  </main>

  <!-- Scripts -->
  <script src="../../shared/js/supabase-config.js"></script>
  <script src="../../shared/js/api.js"></script>
  <script src="../../shared/js/auth.js"></script>
  <script src="../../shared/js/shell.js"></script>

  <script>
    if (!requireAuth('siswa,siswa_aktif,calon_siswa')) {}

    async function initPage() {
      const user = getCurrentUser() || {};
      const isSiswaAktif = (user.role && user.role.code === 'siswa_aktif') || user.role === 'siswa_aktif';

      const studentMenu = isSiswaAktif ? [
        { href: 'dashboard.html', label: '📊 Dashboard' },
        { href: 'biodata.html', label: '👤 Biodata Siswa' },
        { href: 'spp.html', label: '💳 Tagihan SPP' },
        { href: 'dokumen.html', label: '📁 Berkas Dokumen' }
      ] : [
        { href: 'dashboard.html', label: '📊 Status PPDB' },
        { href: 'biodata.html', label: '📝 Lengkapi Biodata' },
        { href: 'dokumen.html', label: '📁 Upload Dokumen' }
      ];

      Shell.init({
        menu: studentMenu,
        active: 'dokumen.html',
        portalLabel: isSiswaAktif ? 'Portal Siswa Aktif' : 'Portal Calon Siswa (PPDB)',
        title: 'Upload Dokumen Persyaratan',
        subtitle: 'Lengkapi berkas pendaftaran dan administrasi'
      });

      await loadDocuments();
    }

    async function handleFileSelect(type) {
      const input = document.getElementById(`file-${type}`);
      if (!input.files || input.files.length === 0) return;
      const file = input.files[0];

      // Preview Foto jika tipe adalah foto
      if (type === 'foto') {
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = (e) => {
          document.getElementById('img-preview-foto').src = e.target.result;
          document.getElementById('img-preview-foto').classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('placeholder-foto').classList.add('hidden');
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
      } else {
        document.getElementById(`name-${type}`).textContent = file.name;
      }

      let fileUrl = '';
      if (window.uploadToSupabaseStorage && !window.location.hostname.includes('localhost')) {
        try { fileUrl = await uploadToSupabaseStorage(file, 'dokumen_' + type); } catch (_) {}
      }
      if (!fileUrl) {
        fileUrl = await new Promise((resolve) => {
          const reader = new FileReader();
          reader.onload = (e) => resolve(e.target.result);
          reader.onerror = () => resolve('');
          reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        });
      }

      showToast(`Mengunggah ${file.name}...`, 'info');

      try {
        const res = await apiCall('siswa', 'upload_document', {
          document_type: type,
          file_name: file.name,
          file_size: file.size,
          file_url: fileUrl
        });

        if (res.success) {
          showToast(`Dokumen ${type.toUpperCase()} berhasil diunggah!`, 'success');
          loadDocuments();
        } else {
          showToast(res.message || 'Gagal mengunggah file', 'error');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('Terjadi kesalahan saat mengunggah', 'error');
      }
    }

    let currentDocsCache = [];

    function previewDocByIndex(idx) {
      const d = currentDocsCache[idx];
      if (!d || !d.file_url) return;
      const modal = document.getElementById('doc-preview-modal');
      const title = document.getElementById('modal-doc-title');
      const subtitle = document.getElementById('modal-doc-subtitle');
      const img = document.getElementById('modal-doc-img');
      const fallback = document.getElementById('modal-doc-fallback');
      const openBtn = document.getElementById('modal-doc-open-btn');
      const nameEl = document.getElementById('modal-doc-filename');

      title.textContent = d.file_name || 'Berkas Dokumen';
      subtitle.textContent = `Jenis Dokumen: ${d.document_type || 'Persyaratan'}`;
      openBtn.href = d.file_url;
      nameEl.textContent = d.file_name || 'Dokumen Persyaratan';

      const isImg = d.file_url.startsWith('data:image/') || /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp|gif)$/i.test(d.file_url);
      if (isImg) {
        img.src = d.file_url;
        img.classList.remove('hidden');
        fallback.classList.add('hidden');
      } else {
        img.classList.add('hidden');
        fallback.classList.remove('hidden');
      }
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeDocPreview() {
      document.getElementById('doc-preview-modal')?.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    async function loadDocuments() {
      try {
        const res = await apiCall('siswa', 'get_documents');
        const docs = (res.success && res.data) ? res.data : [];
        currentDocsCache = docs;
        const tbody = document.getElementById('documents-table-body');

        if (docs.length === 0) {
          tbody.innerHTML = `
            <tr>
              <td colspan="5" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">
                Belum ada dokumen yang diunggah.
              </td>
            </tr>
          `;
          return;
        }

        const typeLabels = {
          foto: 'Pas Foto 3x4',
          kk: 'Kartu Keluarga',
          akte: 'Akte Kelahiran',
          lainnya: 'Dokumen Tambahan'
        };

        tbody.innerHTML = docs.map((d, idx) => {
          const verifiedBadge = d.is_verified 
            ? '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200">✓ Terverifikasi</span>'
            : '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200">⏳ Menunggu Review</span>';

          return `
            <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
              <td class="py-3 px-4 font-bold text-slate-900">${typeLabels[d.document_type] || d.document_type}</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-4 text-slate-600 truncate max-w-xs">${d.file_name || 'Berkas Dokumen'}</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-4 text-slate-500">${formatDateTime(d.created_at)}</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-4">${verifiedBadge}</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right">
                <button type="button" onclick="previewDocByIndex(${idx})" class="px-3 py-1 bg-primary-50 hover:bg-primary-100 text-primary-700 font-bold rounded-lg text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer">
                  Lihat Berkas
                </button>
              </td>
            </tr>
          `;
        }).join('');
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error loading documents:', err);
      }
    }

    window.previewDocByIndex = previewDocByIndex;
    window.closeDocPreview = closeDocPreview;

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initPage);
  </script>
</body>
</html>
